





|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

aṅguttaranikāyau

aṭṭhakanipātapāḷi


1. paṭhamapaṇṇāsakaṃ


1. maittāvaggau

1. maittāsuttaṃ

1. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

a॰ ni॰ 11.15 ‘‘maittāya, bhikkhavai, caitauvimuttiyā āsaivitāya bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānīkatāya vatthukatāya  anuṭṭhitāya paricitāya susamāraddhāya aṭṭhānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhā| katamai aṭṭha? sukhaṃ supati, sukhaṃ paṭibujjhati, na pāpakaṃ supinaṃ passati, manussānaṃ piyau hauti, amanussānaṃ piyau hauti, daivatā rakkhanti, nāssa aggi vā visaṃ vā satthaṃ vā kamati, uttariṃ appaṭivijjhantau brahmalaukūpagau hauti| maittāya, bhikkhavai, caitauvimuttiyā āsaivitāya bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānīkatāya vatthukatāya anuṭṭhitāya paricitāya susamāraddhāya imai aṭṭhānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhā’’ti|

‘‘yau  ca maittaṃ bhāvayati, appamāṇaṃ paṭissatau patissatau (sī॰)|

tanū saṃyaujanā haunti, passatau upadhikkhayaṃ||

‘‘aikampi  cai pāṇamaduṭṭhacittau,

maittāyati kusalī taina hauti|

sabbai ca pāṇai manasānukampī,

pahūtamariyau pakarauti puñ‍ñaṃ||

‘‘yai sattasaṇḍaṃ pathaviṃ vijaitvā,

rājisayau yajamānā anupariyagā|

assamaidhaṃ purisamaidhaṃ,

sammāpāsaṃ vājapaiyyaṃ niraggaḷaṃ||

‘‘maittassa cittassa subhāvitassa,

kalampi tai nānubhavanti sauḷasiṃ|

candappabhā tāragaṇāva sabbai,

yathā na agghanti kalampi sauḷasiṃ ayaṃ pādau bahūsu na dissati||

‘‘yau na hanti na ghātaiti, na jināti na jāpayai|

maittaṃsau sabbabhūtānaṃ, vairaṃ tassa na kainacī’’ti|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. pañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

2. ‘‘aṭṭhimai, bhikkhavai, haitū aṭṭha pac‍cayā ādibrahmacariyikāya pañ‍ñāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya, paṭiladdhāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattanti| katamai aṭṭha? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satthāraṃ upanissāya viharati añ‍ñataraṃ vā garuṭṭhāniyaṃ sabrahmacāriṃ, yatthassa tibbaṃ hirauttappaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti paimañ‍ca gāravau ca| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau haitu paṭhamau pac‍cayau ādibrahmacariyikāya pañ‍ñāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya, paṭiladdhāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattati|

‘‘sau  taṃ satthāraṃ upanissāya viharantau añ‍ñataraṃ vā garuṭṭhāniyaṃ sabrahmacāriṃ, yatthassa tibbaṃ hirauttappaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti paimaṃ gāravau  ca, tai kālaina kālaṃ upasaṅkamitvā paripucchati paripañhati – ‘idaṃ, bhantai, kathaṃ; imassa kau atthau’ti? tassa tai āyasmantau avivaṭañ‍caiva vivaranti, anuttānīkatañ‍ca uttānī karaunti, anaikavihitaisu ca kaṅkhāṭhāniyaisu dhammaisu kaṅkhaṃ paṭivinaudainti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau haitu dutiyau pac‍cayau ādibrahmacariyikāya pañ‍ñāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya, paṭiladdhāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattati|

‘‘sau taṃ dhammaṃ sutvā dvayaina vūpakāsaina sampādaiti – kāyavūpakāsaina ca cittavūpakāsaina ca| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau haitu tatiyau pac‍cayau ādibrahmacariyikāya pañ‍ñāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya, paṭiladdhāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattati|

‘‘sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau haitu catutthau pac‍cayau ādibrahmacariyikāya pañ‍ñāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya, paṭiladdhāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattati|

‘‘bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau| yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā  sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ  satthā sabyañ‍janā (ka॰ sī॰) kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā dhatā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camau haitu pañ‍camau pac‍cayau ādibrahmacariyikāya pañ‍ñāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya, paṭiladdhāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattati|

‘‘āraddhavīriyau  viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, chaṭṭhau haitu chaṭṭhau pac‍cayau ādibrahmacariyikāya pañ‍ñāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya, paṭiladdhāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattati|

‘‘saṅghagatau  khau pana anānākathikau hauti atiracchānakathikau| sāmaṃ vā dhammaṃ bhāsati paraṃ vā ajjhaisati ariyaṃ vā tuṇhībhāvaṃ nātimañ‍ñati| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, sattamau haitu sattamau pac‍cayau ādibrahmacariyikāya pañ‍ñāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya, paṭiladdhāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattati|

‘‘pañ‍casu khau pana upādānakkhandhaisu udayabbayānupassī viharati – ‘iti rūpaṃ, iti rūpassa samudayau, iti rūpassa atthaṅgamau; iti vaidanā, iti vaidanāya samudayau, iti vaidanāya atthaṅgamau; iti sañ‍ñā…pai॰… iti saṅkhārā…pai॰… iti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayau, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa atthaṅgamau’ti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhamau haitu aṭṭhamau pac‍cayau ādibrahmacariyikāya pañ‍ñāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya, paṭiladdhāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattati|

‘‘tamainaṃ  sabrahmacārī aivaṃ sambhāvainti – ‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā satthāraṃ upanissāya viharati añ‍ñataraṃ vā garuṭṭhāniyaṃ sabrahmacāriṃ, yatthassa tibbaṃ hirauttappaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti paimañ‍ca gāravau ca| addhā ayamāyasmā jānaṃ jānāti passaṃ passatī’ti! ayampi dhammau piyattāya garuttāya piyatāya garutāya (syā॰) bhāvanāya sāmañ‍ñāya aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘‘taṃ  khau panāyamāyasmā satthāraṃ upanissāya viharantau añ‍ñataraṃ vā garuṭṭhāniyaṃ sabrahmacāriṃ, yatthassa tibbaṃ hirauttappaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti paimañ‍ca  gāravau ca, tai kālaina kālaṃ upasaṅkamitvā paripucchati paripañhati – idaṃ, bhantai, kathaṃ; imassa kau atthauti? tassa tai āyasmantau avivaṭañ‍caiva vivaranti, anuttānīkatañ‍ca uttānī karaunti, anaikavihitaisu ca kaṅkhāṭhāniyaisu dhammaisu kaṅkhaṃ paṭivinaudainti| addhā ayamāyasmā jānaṃ jānāti passaṃ passatī’ti! ayampi dhammau piyattāya garuttāya bhāvanāya sāmañ‍ñāya aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘‘taṃ khau panāyamāyasmā dhammaṃ sutvā dvayaina vūpakāsaina sampādaiti – kāyavūpakāsaina ca cittavūpakāsaina ca| addhā ayamāyasmā jānaṃ jānāti passaṃ passatī’ti! ayampi dhammau piyattāya garuttāya bhāvanāya sāmañ‍ñāya aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘‘sīlavā  khau panāyamāyasmā pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| addhā ayamāyasmā jānaṃ jānāti passaṃ passatī’ti! ayampi dhammau piyattāya garuttāya bhāvanāya sāmañ‍ñāya aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘‘bahussutau khau panāyamāyasmā  sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau| yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā| addhā ayamāyasmā jānaṃ jānāti passaṃ passatī’ti! ayampi dhammau piyattāya garuttāya bhāvanāya sāmañ‍ñāya aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘‘āraddhavīriyau khau panāyamāyasmā viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| addhā ayamāyasmā jānaṃ jānāti passaṃ passatī’ti! ayampi dhammau piyattāya garuttāya bhāvanāya sāmañ‍ñāya aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘‘saṅghagatau  khau panāyamāyasmā anānākathikau hauti atiracchānakathikau| sāmaṃ vā dhammaṃ  bhāsati paraṃ vā ajjhaisati ariyaṃ vā tuṇhībhāvaṃ nātimañ‍ñati| addhā ayamāyasmā jānaṃ jānāti passaṃ passatī’ti! ayampi dhammau piyattāya garuttāya bhāvanāya sāmañ‍ñāya aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘‘pañ‍casu khau panāyamāyasmā upādānakkhandhaisu udayabbayānupassī viharati – iti rūpaṃ, iti rūpassa samudayau, iti rūpassa atthaṅgamau; iti vaidanā…pai॰… iti sañ‍ñā…pai॰… iti saṅkhārā…pai॰… iti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayau, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa atthaṅgamauti| addhā ayamāyasmā jānaṃ jānāti passaṃ passatī’ti! ayampi dhammau piyattāya garuttāya bhāvanāya sāmañ‍ñāya aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘imai khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha haitū aṭṭha pac‍cayā  ādibrahmacariyikāya pañ‍ñāya appaṭiladdhāya paṭilābhāya, paṭiladdhāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā saṃvattantī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. paṭhamaappiyasuttaṃ

3. ‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ appiyau ca hauti amanāpau ca agaru ca abhāvanīyau ca| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu appiyapasaṃsī ca hauti, piyagarahī ca, lābhakāmau ca, sak‍kārakāmau ca, ahirikau ca, anauttappī ca, pāpicchau ca, micchādiṭṭhi ca| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ appiyau ca hauti amanāpau ca agaru ca abhāvanīyau ca|

‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau ca| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na appiyapasaṃsī ca hauti, na piyagarahī ca, na lābhakāmau ca, na sak‍kārakāmau ca, hirīmā ca hauti, auttappī ca, appicchau ca, sammādiṭṭhi ca| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau  bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau cā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyaappiyasuttaṃ

4. ‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ appiyau ca hauti amanāpau ca agaru ca abhāvanīyau ca| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu lābhakāmau ca hauti, sak‍kārakāmau ca, anavañ‍ñattikāmau ca, akālañ‍ñū ca, amattañ‍ñū ca, asuci ca, bahubhāṇī ca, ak‍kausakaparibhāsakau ca sabrahmacārīnaṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ appiyau ca hauti amanāpau ca agaru ca abhāvanīyau ca|

‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau ca| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na lābhakāmau ca hauti, na sak‍kārakāmau ca, na anavañ‍ñattikāmau ca, kālañ‍ñū ca, mattañ‍ñū ca, suci ca, na bahubhāṇī ca, anak‍kausakaparibhāsakau ca sabrahmacārīnaṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau cā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamalaukadhammasuttaṃ

5. ‘‘aṭṭhimai, bhikkhavai, laukadhammā laukaṃ anuparivattanti, laukau ca aṭṭha laukadhammai anuparivattati| katamai aṭṭha? lābhau  ca, alābhau ca, yasau ca, ayasau ca, nindā ca, pasaṃsā ca, sukhañ‍ca, dukkhañ‍ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha laukadhammā laukaṃ anuparivattanti, laukau ca imai aṭṭha laukadhammai anuparivattatī’’ti|

‘‘lābhau alābhau ca yasāyasau ca,

nindā pasaṃsā ca sukhaṃ dukhañ‍ca|

aitai  anic‍cā manujaisu dhammā,

asassatā vipariṇāmadhammā||

‘‘aitai ca ñatvā satimā sumaidhau,

avaikkhati vipariṇāmadhammai|

iṭṭhassa dhammā na mathainti cittaṃ,

aniṭṭhatau nau paṭighātamaiti||

‘‘tassānuraudhā  atha vā viraudhā,

vidhūpitā atthaṅgatā na santi|

padañ‍ca ñatvā virajaṃ asaukaṃ,

sammappajānāti bhavassa pāragū’’ti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyalaukadhammasuttaṃ

6. ‘‘aṭṭhimai, bhikkhavai, laukadhammā laukaṃ anuparivattanti, laukau ca aṭṭha laukadhammai anuparivattati| katamai aṭṭha? lābhau ca, alābhau ca, yasau ca, ayasau ca, nindā ca, pasaṃsā ca, sukhañ‍ca, dukkhañ‍ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha laukadhammā laukaṃ anuparivattanti, laukau ca imai aṭṭha laukadhammai anuparivattati|

‘‘assutavatau, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janassa uppaj‍jati lābhaupi alābhaupi yasaupi ayasaupi nindāpi pasaṃsāpi sukhampi dukkhampi| sutavataupi, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa uppaj‍jati lābhaupi alābhaupi yasaupi ayasaupi nindāpi pasaṃsāpi sukhampi dukkhampi| tatra, bhikkhavai, kau visaisau  kau  adhippayāsau adhippāyau (sī॰), adhippāyasau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) adhi + pa + yasu + ṇa = adhippayāsau kiṃ nānākaraṇaṃ sutavatau ariyasāvakassa assutavatā puthuj‍janainā’’ti? ‘‘bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā bhagavaṃnaittikā bhagavaṃpaṭisaraṇā| sādhu vata, bhantai, bhagavantaṃyaiva paṭibhātu aitassa bhāsitassa atthau| bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti|

‘‘taina  hi, bhikkhavai, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘assutavatau, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janassa uppaj‍jati lābhau| sau na iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘uppan‍nau khau mai ayaṃ lābhau; sau ca khau anic‍cau dukkhau vipariṇāmadhammau’ti  yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| uppaj‍jati alābhau…pai॰… uppaj‍jati yasau… uppaj‍jati ayasau… uppaj‍jati nindā… uppaj‍jati pasaṃsā… uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ… uppaj‍jati dukkhaṃ| sau na iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘uppan‍naṃ khau mai idaṃ dukkhaṃ; tañ‍ca khau anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhamma’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti’’|

‘‘tassa lābhaupi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, alābhaupi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, yasaupi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, ayasaupi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, nindāpi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, pasaṃsāpi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, sukhampi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, dukkhampi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati| sau uppan‍naṃ lābhaṃ anurujjhati, alābhai paṭivirujjhati; uppan‍naṃ yasaṃ anurujjhati, ayasai paṭivirujjhati; uppan‍naṃ pasaṃsaṃ anurujjhati, nindāya paṭivirujjhati; uppan‍naṃ sukhaṃ anurujjhati, dukkhai paṭivirujjhati| sau aivaṃ anuraudhaviraudhasamāpan‍nau na parimuc‍cati jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi| ‘na parimuc‍cati dukkhasmā’ti vadāmi’’|

‘‘sutavatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa uppaj‍jati lābhau| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘uppan‍nau khau mai ayaṃ lābhau; sau ca khau anic‍cau dukkhau vipariṇāmadhammau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| uppaj‍jati alābhau…pai॰… uppaj‍jati yasau… uppaj‍jati  ayasau… uppaj‍jati nindā… uppaj‍jati pasaṃsā… uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ… uppaj‍jati dukkhaṃ| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘uppan‍naṃ khau mai idaṃ  dukkhaṃ; tañ‍ca khau anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhamma’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti’’|

‘‘tassa  lābhaupi cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, alābhaupi cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, yasaupi cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, ayasaupi cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, nindāpi cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, pasaṃsāpi cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, sukhampi cittaṃ  na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, dukkhampi cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati| sau uppan‍naṃ lābhaṃ nānurujjhati, alābhai nappaṭivirujjhati; uppan‍naṃ yasaṃ nānurujjhati, ayasai nappaṭivirujjhati; uppan‍naṃ pasaṃsaṃ nānurujjhati, nindāya nappaṭivirujjhati; uppan‍naṃ sukhaṃ nānurujjhati, dukkhai nappaṭivirujjhati| sau aivaṃ anuraudhaviraudhavippahīnau parimuc‍cati jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi| ‘parimuc‍cati dukkhasmā’ti vadāmi| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, visaisau ayaṃ adhippayāsau idaṃ nānākaraṇaṃ sutavatau ariyasāvakassa assutavatā puthuj‍janainā’’ti|

‘‘lābhau alābhau ca yasāyasau ca,

nindā pasaṃsā ca sukhaṃ dukhañ‍ca|

aitai anic‍cā manujaisu dhammā,

asassatā vipariṇāmadhammā||

‘‘aitai ca ñatvā satimā sumaidhau,

avaikkhati vipariṇāmadhammai|

iṭṭhassa  dhammā na mathainti cittaṃ,

aniṭṭhatau nau paṭighātamaiti||

‘‘tassānuraudhā  atha vā viraudhā,

vidhūpitā atthaṅgatā na santi|

padañ‍ca ñatvā virajaṃ asaukaṃ,

sammappajānāti bhavassa pāragū’’ti|| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. daivadattavipattisuttaṃ

7. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai acirapak‍kantai daivadattai| tatra bhagavā daivadattaṃ ārabbha bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ attavipattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitā hauti| sādhu, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ paravipattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitā  hauti| sādhu, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ attasampattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitā hauti| sādhu, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ parasampattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitā hauti| aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, asaddhammaihi abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau daivadattau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau’’|

cūḷava॰ 348 ‘‘katamaihi aṭṭhahi? lābhaina hi, bhikkhavai, abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau daivadattau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau| alābhaina, bhikkhavai…pai॰… yasaina, bhikkhavai… ayasaina, bhikkhavai… sak‍kāraina, bhikkhavai… asak‍kāraina, bhikkhavai… pāpicchatāya, bhikkhavai… pāpamittatāya, bhikkhavai, abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau daivadattau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahi asaddhammaihi abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau daivadattau āpāyikau  nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau|

‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu uppan‍naṃ lābhaṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharaiyya, uppan‍naṃ alābhaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ yasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ ayasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ sak‍kāraṃ … uppan‍naṃ asak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpicchataṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpamittataṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharaiyya|

‘‘kiñ‍ca kathañ‍ca (ka॰), bhikkhavai, bhikkhu atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍naṃ lābhaṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharaiyya, uppan‍naṃ alābhaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ yasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ ayasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ sak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ asak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpicchataṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpamittataṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharaiyya?

‘‘yaṃ hissa, bhikkhavai, uppan‍naṃ lābhaṃ anabhibhuyya anabhibhūyya anabhibhūyya (ka॰) viharatau uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, uppan‍naṃ lābhaṃ abhibhuyya abhibhūyya abhibhūyya (ka॰) viharatau aivaṃsa tai āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na haunti| yaṃ hissa, bhikkhavai, uppan‍naṃ alābhaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ yasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ ayasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ sak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ asak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpicchataṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpamittataṃ anabhibhuyya viharatau uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, uppan‍naṃ pāpamittataṃ abhibhuyya viharatau aivaṃsa tai āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na haunti| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍naṃ lābhaṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharaiyya, uppan‍naṃ alābhaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ yasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ ayasaṃ  … uppan‍naṃ sak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ asak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpicchataṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpamittataṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharaiyya|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘uppan‍naṃ lābhaṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharissāma, uppan‍naṃ alābhaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ yasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ ayasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ sak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ asak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpicchataṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpamittataṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. uttaravipattisuttaṃ

8. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā uttarau mahisavatthusmiṃ viharati saṅkhaiyyakai pabbatai vaṭajālikāyaṃ dhavajālikāyaṃ (sī॰), vaṭṭajālikāyaṃ (syā॰)| tatra khau āyasmā uttarau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘sādhāvusau, bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ attavipattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitā hauti| sādhāvusau, bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ paravipattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitā hauti| sādhāvusau, bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ attasampattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitā hauti| sādhāvusau, bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ parasampattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitā hautī’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina vaissavaṇau mahārājā uttarāya disāya dakkhiṇaṃ disaṃ gacchati kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| assausi khau vaissavaṇau mahārājā āyasmatau uttarassa mahisavatthusmiṃ saṅkhaiyyakai pabbatai vaṭajālikāyaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisaintassa – ‘‘sādhāvusau, bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ attavipattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitā hauti| sādhāvusau, bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ paravipattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitā hauti| sādhāvusau, bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ attasampattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitā hauti| sādhāvusau, bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ parasampattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitā hautī’’ti|

atha khau vaissavaṇṇau mahārājā – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ sammiñ‍jitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya sammiñ‍jaiyya (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), aivamaivaṃ mahisavatthusmiṃ saṅkhaiyyakai pabbatai vaṭajālikāyaṃ antarahitau daivaisu tāvatiṃsaisu pāturahausi| atha khau vaissavaṇṇau mahārājā yaina sak‍kau daivānamindau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā sak‍kaṃ  daivānamindaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yagghai mārisa, jānaiyyāsi! aisau āyasmā uttarau mahisavatthusmiṃ saṅkhaiyyakai  pabbatai vaṭajālikāyaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ  aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘sādhāvusau, bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ attavipattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitā hauti| sādhāvusau, bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ paravipattiṃ…pai॰… attasampattiṃ… parasampattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitā hautī’’’ti|

atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaivaṃ daivaisu tāvatiṃsaisu antarahitau mahisavatthusmiṃ saṅkhaiyyakai pabbatai vaṭajālikāyaṃ āyasmatau uttarassa sammukhai pāturahausi| atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau yaināyasmā uttarau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ uttaraṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau sak‍kau daivānamindau āyasmantaṃ uttaraṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhantai, āyasmā uttarau bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisaisi – ‘sādhāvusau, bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ attavipattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitā hauti, sādhāvusau, bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ paravipattiṃ…pai॰… attasampattiṃ… parasampattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitā hautī’’’ ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, daivānamindā’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ panidaṃ kiṃ pana (syā॰), bhantai, āyasmatau uttarassa sakaṃ paṭibhānaṃ sakapaṭibhānaṃ upādāya (ka॰), udāhu tassa bhagavatau vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassā’’ti? ‘‘taina hi, daivānaminda, upamaṃ tai karissāmi| upamāya midhaikac‍cai viñ‍ñū purisā bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājāna’’nti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, daivānaminda, gāmassa vā nigamassa vā avidūrai mahādhañ‍ñarāsi| tatau mahājanakāyau dhañ‍ñaṃ āharaiyya – kājaihipi piṭakaihipi ucchaṅgaihipi  añ‍jalīhipi| yau nu khau, daivānaminda, taṃ mahājanakāyaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ pucchaiyya – ‘kutau imaṃ dhañ‍ñaṃ āharathā’ti, kathaṃ byākaramānau nu khau, daivānaminda, sau mahājanakāyau sammā byākaramānau byākaraiyyā’’ti? ‘‘‘amumhā mahādhañ‍ñarāsimhā āharāmā’ti khau, bhantai, sau mahājanakāyau sammā byākaramānau byākaraiyyā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, daivānaminda, yaṃ kiñ‍ci subhāsitaṃ sabbaṃ taṃ tassa bhagavatau vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| tatau upādāyupādāya mayaṃ cañ‍ñai ca bhaṇāmā’’ti|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ bhantai! yāva subhāsitaṃ cidaṃ āyasmatā uttaraina – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci  subhāsitaṃ sabbaṃ taṃ tassa bhagavatau vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| tatau upādāyupādāya mayaṃ cañ‍ñai ca bhaṇāmā’ti| aikamidaṃ, bhantai uttara, samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai acirapak‍kantai daivadattai| tatra khau bhagavā daivadattaṃ ārabbha bhikkhū āmantaisi –

‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ attavipattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitā hauti| sādhu, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ paravipattiṃ…pai॰… attasampattiṃ… parasampattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitā hauti| aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, asaddhammaihi abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau daivadattau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? lābhaina hi, bhikkhavai, abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau daivadattau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau; alābhaina, bhikkhavai…pai॰… yasaina, bhikkhavai … ayasaina, bhikkhavai… sak‍kāraina, bhikkhavai… asak‍kāraina, bhikkhavai… pāpicchatāya, bhikkhavai… pāpamittatāya, bhikkhavai, abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau daivadattau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahi asaddhammaihi abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau daivadattau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau|

‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu uppan‍naṃ lābhaṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharaiyya; uppan‍naṃ alābhaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ yasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ ayasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ sak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ asak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpicchataṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpamittataṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharaiyya|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍naṃ lābhaṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharaiyya; uppan‍naṃ alābhaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ yasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ ayasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ sak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ asak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpicchataṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpamittataṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharaiyya?

‘‘yaṃ hissa, bhikkhavai, uppan‍naṃ lābhaṃ anabhibhuyya viharatau uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, uppan‍naṃ lābhaṃ abhibhuyya viharatau aivaṃsa tai āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na haunti| yaṃ hissa, bhikkhavai, uppan‍naṃ alābhaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ yasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ ayasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ sak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ asak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpicchataṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpamittataṃ anabhibhuyya viharatau  uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, uppan‍naṃ pāpamittataṃ abhibhuyya viharatau aivaṃsa  tai āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na haunti| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍naṃ lābhaṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharaiyya; uppan‍naṃ alābhaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ yasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ ayasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ sak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ asak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpicchataṃ … uppan‍naṃ pāpamittataṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharaiyya|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – uppan‍naṃ  lābhaṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharissāma, uppan‍naṃ alābhaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ yasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ ayasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ sak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ asak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpicchataṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpamittataṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharissāmāti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti|

‘‘aittāvatā, bhantai uttara, manussaisu catassau parisā – bhikkhū, bhikkhuniyau, upāsakā, upāsikāyau| nāyaṃ dhammapariyāyau kismiñ‍ci upaṭṭhitau patiṭṭhitau (sī॰ syā॰)| uggaṇhatu, bhantai, āyasmā uttarau imaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ| pariyāpuṇātu, bhantai, āyasmā uttarau imaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ| dhāraitu, bhantai, āyasmā uttarau imaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ| atthasaṃhitau ayaṃ, bhantai, dhammapariyāyau ādibrahmacariyakau’’ti ādibrahmacariyikau (sī॰ ka॰)| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. nandasuttaṃ

9. ‘‘‘kulaputtau’ti, bhikkhavai, nandaṃ sammā vadamānau vadaiyya| ‘balavā’ti, bhikkhavai, nandaṃ sammā vadamānau vadaiyya| ‘pāsādikau’ti, bhikkhavai, nandaṃ sammā vadamānau vadaiyya| ‘tibbarāgau’ti, bhikkhavai, nandaṃ sammā vadamānau vadaiyya| kimañ‍ñatra, bhikkhavai, nandau indriyaisu guttadvārau, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū, jāgariyaṃ anuyuttau, satisampajañ‍ñaina saman‍nāgatau, yaihi yaina (ka॰) nandau sak‍kauti paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ! tatridaṃ, bhikkhavai, nandassa indriyaisu guttadvāratāya hauti| sacai, bhikkhavai, nandassa puratthimā disā ālaukaitabbā hauti, sabbaṃ caitasā saman‍nāharitvā  nandau puratthimaṃ disaṃ ālaukaiti – ‘aivaṃ mai puratthimaṃ disaṃ ālaukayatau nābhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavissantī’ti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, nandassa pacchimā disā ālaukaitabbā hauti…pai॰… uttarā disā ālaukaitabbā hauti… dakkhiṇā disā ālaukaitabbā hauti… uddhaṃ ul‍laukaitabbā hauti… adhau aulaukaitabbā hauti… anudisā anuvilaukaitabbā hauti, sabbaṃ caitasā saman‍nāharitvā nandau anudisaṃ anuvilaukaiti – ‘aivaṃ mai anudisaṃ anuvilaukayatau nābhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavissantī’ti| itiha tattha sampajānau hauti| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, nandassa indriyaisu guttadvāratāya hauti|

‘‘tatridaṃ, bhikkhavai, nandassa bhaujanai mattañ‍ñutāya hauti| idha, bhikkhavai, nandau paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau āhāraṃ āhāraiti – ‘naiva davāya na madāya na maṇḍanāya na vibhūsanāya, yāvadaiva imassa kāyassa ṭhitiyā yāpanāya vihiṃsūparatiyā brahmacariyānuggahāya iti purāṇañ‍ca vaidanaṃ paṭihaṅkhāmi, navañ‍ca vaidanaṃ na uppādaissāmi, yātrā ca mai bhavissati anavaj‍jatā ca phāsuvihārau cā’ti| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, nandassa bhaujanai mattañ‍ñutāya hauti|

‘‘tatridaṃ, bhikkhavai, nandassa jāgariyānuyaugasmiṃ hauti| idha, bhikkhavai, nandau divasaṃ caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; rattiyā paṭhamaṃ yāmaṃ caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; rattiyā majjhimaṃ yāmaṃ dakkhiṇaina passaina sīhasaiyyaṃ kappaiti pādai pādaṃ ac‍cādhāya satau sampajānau uṭṭhānasañ‍ñaṃ manasi karitvā; rattiyā pacchimaṃ yāmaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, nandassa jāgariyānuyaugasmiṃ hauti|

‘‘tatridaṃ, bhikkhavai, nandassa satisampajañ‍ñasmiṃ hauti| idha, bhikkhavai, nandassa viditā vaidanā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti; viditā sañ‍ñā…pai॰… viditā vitak‍kā…pai॰… abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, nandassa satisampajañ‍ñasmiṃ hauti|

‘‘kimañ‍ñatra, bhikkhavai, nandau indriyaisu guttadvārau, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū, jāgariyaṃ anuyuttau, satisampajañ‍ñaina saman‍nāgatau, yaihi nandau sak‍kauti paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ caritu’’nti! navamaṃ|

10. kāraṇḍavasuttaṃ

10. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā campāyaṃ viharati gaggarāya paukkharaṇiyā tīrai| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā caudainti| sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi āpattiyā caudiyamānau añ‍ñaināñ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati, bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmaiti, kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti|

atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘niddhamathaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, puggalaṃ; niddhamathaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, puggalaṃ| apanaiyyaisau apanaiyyau sau (sī॰), apanaiyyau (syā॰), bhikkhavai, puggalau| kiṃ vau taina paraputtaina visaudhitaina kiṃ vauparaputtau vihaiṭhiyati (sī॰), kiṃ paraputtau vihaiṭhaiti (syā॰), kiṃ vau paraputtā vihaiṭhaiti (pī॰), kiṃ sau paraputtau visaudhaiti (ka॰)! idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa tādisaṃyaiva hauti abhik‍kantaṃ paṭik‍kantaṃ ālaukitaṃ vilaukitaṃ samiñ‍jitaṃ pasāritaṃ saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇaṃ, saiyyathāpi añ‍ñaisaṃ bhaddakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ – yāvassa bhikkhū āpattiṃ na passanti| yatau ca khvassa bhikkhū āpattiṃ passanti, tamainaṃ aivaṃ jānanti – ‘samaṇadūsīvāyaṃ samaṇarūpī (ka॰) samaṇapalāpau samaṇakāraṇḍavau’ti samaṇakaraṇḍavauti (ka॰)| tamainaṃ iti viditvā bahiddhā nāsainti| taṃ kissa haitu? mā añ‍ñai bhaddakai bhikkhū dūsaisī’’ti!

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, sampan‍nai yavakaraṇai yavadūsī yavarūpī (ka॰) jāyaitha yavapalāpau yavakāraṇḍavauti| tassa tādisaṃyaiva mūlaṃ hauti, saiyyathāpi añ‍ñaisaṃ bhaddakānaṃ yavānaṃ; tādisaṃyaiva nāḷaṃ hauti, saiyyathāpi añ‍ñaisaṃ bhaddakānaṃ yavānaṃ; tādisaṃyaiva pattaṃ hauti, saiyyathāpi añ‍ñaisaṃ bhaddakānaṃ yavānaṃ – yāvassa sīsaṃ na nibbattati| yatau ca khvassa sīsaṃ nibbattati, tamainaṃ aivaṃ jānanti – ‘yavadūsīvāyaṃ yavapalāpau yavakāraṇḍavau’ti| tamainaṃ iti viditvā samūlaṃ uppāṭaitvā bahiddhā yavakaraṇassa chaḍḍainti| taṃ kissa haitu? mā añ‍ñai bhaddakai yavai dūsaisīti!

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cassa puggalassa tādisaṃyaiva hauti abhik‍kantaṃ paṭik‍kantaṃ ālaukitaṃ vilaukitaṃ samiñ‍jitaṃ pasāritaṃ saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇaṃ, saiyyathāpi añ‍ñaisaṃ  bhaddakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ – yāvassa  bhikkhū āpattiṃ na passanti| yatau ca khvassa bhikkhū āpattiṃ passanti, tamainaṃ aivaṃ jānanti – ‘samaṇadūsīvāyaṃ samaṇapalāpau samaṇakāraṇḍavau’ti| tamainaṃ iti viditvā bahiddhā nāsainti| taṃ kissa  haitu? mā añ‍ñai bhaddakai bhikkhū dūsaisīti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahatau dhañ‍ñarāsissa phuṇamānassa vuyhamānassa (sī॰ pī॰), phusayamānassa (syā॰), punamānassa (?) tattha yāni tāni dhañ‍ñāni daḷhāni sāravantāni tāni aikamantaṃ puñ‍jaṃ hauti, yāni pana tāni dhañ‍ñāni dubbalāni palāpāni tāni vātau aikamantaṃ apavahati apakassati (sī॰)| tamainaṃ sāmikā sammaj‍janiṃ gahaitvā bhiyyausaumattāya apasammaj‍janti| taṃ kissa haitu? mā añ‍ñai bhaddakai dhañ‍ñai dūsaisīti! aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cassa puggalassa tādisaṃyaiva hauti abhik‍kantaṃ paṭik‍kantaṃ ālaukitaṃ vilaukitaṃ samiñ‍jitaṃ pasāritaṃ saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇaṃ, saiyyathāpi añ‍ñaisaṃ bhaddakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ – yāvassa bhikkhū āpattiṃ na passanti| yatau ca khvassa bhikkhū āpattiṃ passanti, tamainaṃ aivaṃ jānanti  – ‘samaṇadūsīvāyaṃ samaṇapalāpau samaṇakāraṇḍavau’ti| tamainaṃ iti viditvā bahiddhā nāsainti| taṃ kissa haitu? mā añ‍ñai bhaddakai bhikkhū dūsaisīti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau udapānapanāḷiyatthikau tiṇhaṃ kuṭhāriṃ kudhāriṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) ādāya vanaṃ pavisaiyya| sau yaṃ yadaiva rukkhaṃ kuṭhāripāsaina ākauṭaiyya tattha yāni tāni rukkhāni daḷhāni sāravantāni tāni kuṭhāripāsaina ākauṭitāni kakkhaḷaṃ paṭinadanti; yāni pana tāni rukkhāni antaupūtīni avassutāni kasambujātāni tāni kuṭhāripāsaina ākauṭitāni daddaraṃ paṭinadanti| tamainaṃ mūlai chindati, mūlai chinditvā aggai chindati, aggai chinditvā antau suvisaudhitaṃ visaudhaiti, antau suvisaudhitaṃ visaudhaitvā udapānapanāḷiṃ yaujaiti| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cassa puggalassa tādisaṃyaiva hauti abhik‍kantaṃ paṭik‍kantaṃ ālaukitaṃ vilaukitaṃ samiñ‍jitaṃ pasāritaṃ saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇaṃ, saiyyathāpi añ‍ñaisaṃ bhaddakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ – yāvassa bhikkhū āpattiṃ na passanti| yatau ca khvassa bhikkhū āpattiṃ passanti, tamainaṃ aivaṃ  jānanti – ‘samaṇadūsīvāyaṃ samaṇapalāpau samaṇakāraṇḍavau’ti| tamainaṃ iti viditvā bahiddhā nāsainti| taṃ kissa haitu? mā añ‍ñai bhaddakai bhikkhū dūsaisī’’ti|

‘‘saṃvāsāyaṃ  vijānātha, pāpicchau kaudhanau iti|

makkhī thambhī paḷāsī ca, issukī maccharī saṭhau||

‘‘santavācau janavati, samaṇau viya bhāsati|

rahau karauti karaṇaṃ, pāpadiṭṭhi anādarau||

‘‘saṃsappī ca musāvādī, taṃ viditvā yathātathaṃ|

sabbai samaggā hutvāna, abhinibbaj‍jayātha abhinibbij‍jayaitha (ka॰) naṃ||

‘‘kāraṇḍavaṃ karaṇḍavaṃ (ka॰) su॰ ni॰ 283 passitabbaṃ niddhamatha, kasambuṃ apakassatha avakassatha (ka॰)|

tatau palāpai vāhaitha, assamaṇai samaṇamāninai||

‘‘niddhamitvāna pāpicchai, pāpaācāragaucarai|

suddhāsuddhaihi saṃvāsaṃ, kappayavhau patissatā|

tatau samaggā nipakā, dukkhassantaṃ karissathā’’ti|| dasamaṃ|

maittāvaggau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

maittaṃ  pañ‍ñā ca dvai piyā, dvai laukā dvai vipattiyau|

daivadattau ca uttarau, nandau kāraṇḍavaina cāti||


2. mahāvaggau

1. vairañ‍jasuttaṃ

11. pārā॰ 1 ādayau aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vairañ‍jāyaṃ viharati naḷairupucimandamūlai| atha khau vairañ‍jau brāhmaṇau  yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vītisāraitvā  aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vairañ‍jau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhau gautama – ‘na samaṇau gautamau brāhmaṇai jiṇṇai vuḍḍhai mahal‍lakai addhagatai vayauanuppattai abhivādaiti vā pac‍cuṭṭhaiti vā āsanaina vā nimantaitī’ti| tayidaṃ, bhau gautama, tathaiva| na hi bhavaṃ gautamau brāhmaṇai jiṇṇai vuḍḍhai mahal‍lakai addhagatai vayauanuppattai abhivādaiti vā pac‍cuṭṭhaiti vā āsanaina vā nimantaiti| tayidaṃ, bhau gautama, na sampan‍namaivā’’ti| ‘‘nāhaṃ taṃ, brāhmaṇa, passāmi sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya yamahaṃ abhivādaiyyaṃ vā pac‍cuṭṭhaiyyaṃ vā āsanaina vā nimantaiyyaṃ| yañhi, brāhmaṇa, tathāgatau abhivādaiyya  vā pac‍cuṭṭhaiyya vā āsanaina vā nimantaiyya, muddhāpi tassa vipataiyyā’’ti|

‘‘arasarūpau bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti! ‘‘atthi khvaisa, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘arasarūpau samaṇau gautamau’ti| yai tai, brāhmaṇa, rūparasā saddarasā gandharasā rasarasā phauṭṭhabbarasā, tai tathāgatassa pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā anabhāvakatā (sī॰ pī॰) āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘arasarūpau samaṇau gautamau’ti, nau ca khau yaṃ tvaṃ sandhāya vadaisī’’ti vadaisi (sī॰ ka॰)|

‘‘nibbhaugau  bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti! ‘‘atthi khvaisa, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina  sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘nibbhaugau samaṇau gautamau’ti| yai tai, brāhmaṇa, rūpabhaugā saddabhaugā gandhabhaugā rasabhaugā phauṭṭhabbabhaugā, tai tathāgatassa pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘nibbhaugau samaṇau gautamau’ti, nau ca khau yaṃ tvaṃ sandhāya vadaisī’’ti|

‘‘akiriyavādau  bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti! ‘‘atthi khvaisa, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘akiriyavādau samaṇau gautamau’ti| ahañhi, brāhmaṇa, akiriyaṃ vadāmi kāyaduc‍caritassa vacīduc‍caritassa manauduc‍caritassa; anaikavihitānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ akiriyaṃ  vadāmi| ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘akiriyavādau samaṇau gautamau’ti, nau ca khau yaṃ tvaṃ sandhāya vadaisī’’ti|

‘‘ucchaidavādau bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti! ‘‘atthi khvaisa, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘ucchaidavādau samaṇau gautamau’ti| ahañhi, brāhmaṇa, ucchaidaṃ vadāmi rāgassa dausassa mauhassa; anaikavihitānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ucchaidaṃ vadāmi| ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘ucchaidavādau samaṇau gautamau’ti, nau ca khau yaṃ tvaṃ sandhāya vadaisī’’ti|

‘‘jaigucchī bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti! ‘‘atthi khvaisa, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘jaigucchī samaṇau gautamau’ti| ahañhi, brāhmaṇa, jigucchāmi kāyaduc‍caritaina vacīduc‍caritaina manauduc‍caritaina; jigucchāmi anaikavihitānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ  samāpattiyā| ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘jaigucchī samaṇau gautamau’ti, nau ca khau yaṃ tvaṃ sandhāya vadaisī’’ti|

‘‘vainayikau bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti! ‘‘atthi khvaisa, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘vainayikau samaṇau gautamau’ti| ahañhi, brāhmaṇa, vinayāya dhammaṃ daisaimi rāgassa dausassa mauhassa; anaikavihitānaṃ  pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ vinayāya dhammaṃ  daisaimi| ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘vainayikau samaṇau gautamau’ti, nau ca khau yaṃ tvaṃ sandhāya vadaisī’’ti|

‘‘tapassī bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti! ‘‘atthi khvaisa, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘tapassī samaṇau gautamau’ti| tapanīyāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, pāpakai akusalai dhammai vadāmi kāyaduc‍caritaṃ vacīduc‍caritaṃ manauduc‍caritaṃ| yassa khau, brāhmaṇa, tapanīyā pāpakā akusalā dhammā pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā, tamahaṃ ‘tapassī’ti vadāmi| tathāgatassa khau, brāhmaṇa, tapanīyā pāpakā akusalā dhammā pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘tapassī samaṇau gautamau’ti, nau ca khau yaṃ tvaṃ sandhāya vadaisī’’ti|

‘‘apagabbhau bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti! ‘‘atthi khvaisa, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘apagabbhau samaṇau gautamau’ti| yassa khau, brāhmaṇa, āyatiṃ gabbhasaiyyā punabbhavābhinibbatti pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā, tamahaṃ ‘apagabbhau’ti vadāmi| tathāgatassa khau, brāhmaṇa, āyatiṃ gabbhasaiyyā punabbhavābhinibbatti pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘apagabbhau samaṇau gautamau’ti, nau ca khau yaṃ tvaṃ sandhāya vadaisi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, kuk‍kuṭiyā aṇḍāni aṭṭha vā dasa vā dvādasa vā| tānāssu kuk‍kuṭiyā sammā adhisayitāni sammā parisaiditāni sammā paribhāvitāni| yau nu khau taisaṃ kuk‍kuṭacchāpakānaṃ paṭhamataraṃ pādanakhasikhāya vā mukhatuṇḍakaina vā aṇḍakausaṃ padālaitvā sautthinā abhinibbhij‍jaiyya, kinti svāssa vacanīyau – ‘jaiṭṭhau vā kaniṭṭhau vā’’’ti? ‘‘jaiṭṭhau tissa, bhau gautama, vacanīyau| sau hi naisaṃ, bhau gautama, jaiṭṭhau hautī’’ti|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, avij‍jāgatāya pajāya aṇḍabhūtāya pariyaunaddhāya avij‍jaṇḍakausaṃ padālaitvā aikauva laukai anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau| ahañhi, brāhmaṇa, jaiṭṭhau saiṭṭhau laukassa| āraddhaṃ khau pana mai, brāhmaṇa, vīriyaṃ ahausi asal‍līnaṃ, upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā, passaddhau kāyau asāraddhau, samāhitaṃ cittaṃ aikaggaṃ|

‘‘sau  khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi; vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ  jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi; pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharāmi satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina  paṭisaṃvaidaimi yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi; sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi|

‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaisiṃ| sau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarāmi, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattālīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai – ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau| sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau| sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’ti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarāmi|

‘‘ayaṃ khau mai, brāhmaṇa, rattiyā paṭhamai yāmai paṭhamā vij‍jā adhigatā; avij‍jā vihatā vij‍jā uppan‍nā; tamau vihatau ālaukau uppan‍nau, yathā taṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau| ayaṃ khau mai, brāhmaṇa, paṭhamā abhinibbhidā ahausi kuk‍kuṭacchāpakassaiva  aṇḍakausamhā|

‘‘sau  aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai sattānaṃ cutūpapātañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaisiṃ| sau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passāmi cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai  suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāmi – ‘imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā, vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā, manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā, ariyānaṃ upavādakā, micchādiṭṭhikā, micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā| tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nāti| imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā, vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā, manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā, ariyānaṃ anupavādakā, sammādiṭṭhikā, sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā| tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’ti| iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passāmi cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāmi|

‘‘ayaṃ khau mai, brāhmaṇa, rattiyā majjhimai yāmai dutiyā vij‍jā adhigatā; avij‍jā vihatā vij‍jā uppan‍nā; tamau vihatau ālaukau uppan‍nau, yathā taṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau| ayaṃ khau mai, brāhmaṇa, dutiyā abhinibbhidā ahausi kuk‍kuṭacchāpakassaiva aṇḍakausamhā|

‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai  vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaisiṃ| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ; ‘imai āsavā’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ  āsavasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ| tassa mai aivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ passatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cittha, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cittha, avij‍jāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cittha| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ ahausi| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ|

‘‘ayaṃ  khau mai, brāhmaṇa, rattiyā pacchimai yāmai tatiyā vij‍jā adhigatā; avij‍jā vihatā vij‍jā uppan‍nā; tamau vihatau ālaukau uppan‍nau, yathā taṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau| ayaṃ khau mai, brāhmaṇa, tatiyā abhinibbhidā ahausi kuk‍kuṭacchāpakassaiva aṇḍakausamhā’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai vairañ‍jau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘jaiṭṭhau bhavaṃ gautamau, saiṭṭhau bhavaṃ gautamau| abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama! saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ nikuj‍jitaṃ (ka॰) vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti; aivamaivaṃ bhautā gautamaina  anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. sīhasuttaṃ

12. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā licchavī santhāgārai sandhāgārai (ka॰) san‍nisin‍nā san‍nipatitā anaikapariyāyaina buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, dhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti|

taina  khau pana samayaina sīhau saināpati nigaṇṭhasāvakau tassaṃ parisāyaṃ nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau sīhassa saināpatissa aitadahausi – ‘‘nissaṃsayaṃ khau sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau bhavissati, tathā himai sambahulā abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā licchavī santhāgārai san‍nisin‍nā san‍nipatitā anaikapariyāyaina buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, dhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti| yaṃnūnāhaṃ taṃ bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamaiyyaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddha’’nti| atha khau sīhau saināpati yaina nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau nāthaputtau (ka॰ sī॰), nātaputtau (ka॰ sī॰) tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, bhantai, samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamitu’’nti|

‘‘kiṃ  pana tvaṃ, sīha, kiriyavādau samānau akiriyavādaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissasi? samaṇau hi, sīha, gautamau akiriyavādau, akiriyāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’’ti| atha khau sīhassa  saināpatissa yau ahausi gamiyābhisaṅkhārau gamikābhisaṅkhārau (ka॰ sī॰) mahāva॰ 290 bhagavantaṃ dassanāya, sau paṭippassambhi|

dutiyampi khau sambahulā abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā licchavī santhāgārai san‍nisin‍nā san‍nipatitā anaikapariyāyaina buddhassa…pai॰… dhammassa…pai॰… saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti| dutiyampi khau sīhassa saināpatissa aitadahausi – ‘‘nissaṃsayaṃ khau sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau bhavissati, tathā himai sambahulā abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā licchavī santhāgārai san‍nisin‍nā san‍nipatitā anaikapariyāyaina buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, dhammassa…pai॰… saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti| yaṃnūnāhaṃ taṃ bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamaiyyaṃ arahantaṃ  sammāsambuddha’’nti| atha khau sīhau saināpati yaina nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, bhantai, samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamitu’’nti|

‘‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, sīha, kiriyavādau samānau akiriyavādaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissasi? samaṇau hi, sīha, gautamau akiriyavādau akiriyāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’’ti| dutiyampi khau sīhassa saināpatissa yau ahausi gamiyābhisaṅkhārau bhagavantaṃ dassanāya, sau paṭippassambhi|

tatiyampi khau sambahulā abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā licchavī santhāgārai san‍nisin‍nā san‍nipatitā anaikapariyāyaina buddhassa…pai॰… dhammassa…pai॰… saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti| tatiyampi khau sīhassa  saināpatissa aitadahausi – ‘‘nissaṃsayaṃ khau sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau bhavissati, tathā himai sambahulā abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā licchavī santhāgārai san‍nisin‍nā san‍nipatitā anaikapariyāyaina buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, dhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti| kiṃ himai karissanti nigaṇṭhā  apalaukitā vā anapalaukitā vā? yaṃnūnāhaṃ anapalaukaitvāva nigaṇṭhai nigaṇṭhaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) mahāva॰ 290 passitabbaṃ taṃ bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamaiyyaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddha’’nti|

atha  khau sīhau saināpati pañ‍camattaihi rathasataihi divādivassa vaisāliyā niyyāsi bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| yāvatikā yānassa bhūmi, yānaina gantvā yānā pac‍caurauhitvā pattikauva agamāsi| atha khau sīhau saināpati yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sīhau saināpati bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai – ‘akiriyavādau samaṇau gautamau, akiriyāya dhammaṃ  daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’ti| yai tai, bhantai, aivamāhaṃsu – ‘akiriyavādau samaṇau gautamau, akiriyāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’ti, kac‍ci tai, bhantai, bhagavatau vuttavādinau na ca bhagavantaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhanti dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraunti na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau vādānupātau (ka॰ sī॰ syā॰) a॰ ni॰ 3.58; 5.5 gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchati? anabbhakkhātukāmā hi mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavanta’’nti|

‘‘atthi, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘akiriyavādau samaṇau gautamau, akiriyāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’’’ti|

‘‘atthi, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘kiriyavādau samaṇau gautamau, kiriyāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’’’ti|

‘‘atthi, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘ucchaidavādau samaṇau gautamau, ucchaidāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’’’ti|

‘‘atthi, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘jaigucchī samaṇau gautamau, jaigucchitāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’’’ti|

‘‘atthi, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘vainayikau samaṇau gautamau, vinayāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’’’ti|

‘‘atthi, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘tapassī samaṇau gautamau, tapassitāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’’’ti|

‘‘atthi, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘apagabbhau samaṇau gautamau, apagabbhatāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’’’ti|

‘‘atthi, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘assāsakau samaṇau gautamau, assāsāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’’’ti|

‘‘katamau ca, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘akiriyavādau samaṇau gautamau, akiriyāya  dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’ti? ahañhi, sīha, akiriyaṃ vadāmi kāyaduc‍caritassa vacīduc‍caritassa manauduc‍caritassa; anaikavihitānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ  akiriyaṃ vadāmi| ayaṃ khau, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘akiriyavādau samaṇau gautamau, akiriyāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’’’ti|

‘‘katamau ca, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘kiriyavādau samaṇau gautamau, kiriyāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’ti? ahañhi, sīha, kiriyaṃ vadāmi kāyasucaritassa vacīsucaritassa manausucaritassa; anaikavihitānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ kiriyaṃ vadāmi| ayaṃ khau, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘kiriyavādau samaṇau gautamau, kiriyāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’’’ti|

‘‘katamau ca, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘ucchaidavādau samaṇau gautamau, ucchaidāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’ti? ahañhi, sīha, ucchaidaṃ vadāmi rāgassa dausassa  mauhassa; anaikavihitānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ucchaidaṃ vadāmi| ayaṃ khau, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau  vadaiyya – ‘ucchaidavādau samaṇau gautamau, ucchaidāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’’’ti|

‘‘katamau ca, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘jaigucchī samaṇau gautamau, jaigucchitāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’ti? ahañhi, sīha, jigucchāmi kāyaduc‍caritaina vacīduc‍caritaina  manauduc‍caritaina; jigucchāmi anaikavihitānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā| ayaṃ khau, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘jaigucchī samaṇau gautamau, jaigucchitāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’’’ti|

‘‘katamau ca, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘vainayikau samaṇau gautamau, vinayāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’ti? ahañhi, sīha, vinayāya dhammaṃ daisaimi rāgassa dausassa mauhassa; anaikavihitānaṃ  pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ vinayāya dhammaṃ daisaimi| ayaṃ khau, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘vainayikau samaṇau gautamau, vinayāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’’’ti|

‘‘katamau ca, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘tapassī samaṇau gautamau, tapassitāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’ti? tapanīyāhaṃ, sīha, pāpakai akusalai dhammai vadāmi kāyaduc‍caritaṃ vacīduc‍caritaṃ manauduc‍caritaṃ| yassa khau, sīha, tapanīyā pāpakā akusalā dhammā pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā, tamahaṃ ‘tapassī’ti vadāmi| tathāgatassa khau, sīha, tapanīyā pāpakā akusalā dhammā pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| ayaṃ khau, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘tapassī samaṇau  gautamau, tapassitāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’’’ti|

‘‘katamau  ca, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘apagabbhau samaṇau gautamau, apagabbhatāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’ti? yassa khau, sīha, āyatiṃ gabbhasaiyyā punabbhavābhinibbatti pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā, tamahaṃ ‘apagabbhau’ti vadāmi| tathāgatassa khau, sīha, āyatiṃ gabbhasaiyyā punabbhavābhinibbatti pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| ayaṃ khau, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘apagabbhau samaṇau gautamau, apagabbhatāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’’’ti|

‘‘katamau ca, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘assāsakau samaṇau gautamau, assāsāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’ti? ahañhi, sīha, assāsakau  paramaina assāsaina, assāsāya dhammaṃ daisaimi, taina ca sāvakai vinaimi| ayaṃ khau, sīha, pariyāyau, yaina maṃ pariyāyaina sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘assāsakau samaṇau gautamau, assāsāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, taina ca sāvakai vinaitī’’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai sīhau saināpati bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai  pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

‘‘anuvic‍cakāraṃ khau, sīha, karauhi| anuvic‍cakārau tumhādisānaṃ ñātamanussānaṃ sādhu hautī’’ti| ‘‘imināpāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau bhiyyausaumattāya attamanau abhiraddhau, yaṃ maṃ bhagavā aivamāha – ‘anuvic‍cakāraṃ khau, sīha, karauhi| anuvic‍cakārau tumhādisānaṃ ñātamanussānaṃ sādhu hautī’ti| mañhi, bhantai, añ‍ñatitthiyā sāvakaṃ labhitvā kaivalakappaṃ vaisāliṃ paṭākaṃ pariharaiyyuṃ – ‘sīhau amhākaṃ saināpati sāvakattaṃ upagatau’ti| atha ca pana bhagavā aivamāha – ‘anuvic‍cakāraṃ, sīha, karauhi| anuvic‍cakārau tumhādisānaṃ ñātamanussānaṃ sādhu hautī’ti| aisāhaṃ, bhantai, dutiyampi bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

‘‘dīgharattaṃ  khau tai, sīha, nigaṇṭhānaṃ aupānabhūtaṃ kulaṃ, yaina naisaṃ upagatānaṃ piṇḍakaṃ dātabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyāsī’’ti| ‘‘imināpāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau bhiyyausaumattāya attamanau abhiraddhau, yaṃ maṃ bhagavā aivamāha – ‘dīgharattaṃ khau tai, sīha, nigaṇṭhānaṃ aupānabhūtaṃ kulaṃ, yaina  naisaṃ upagatānaṃ piṇḍakaṃ dātabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyāsī’ti| sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai – ‘samaṇau gautamau aivamāha – mayhamaiva  dānaṃ dātabbaṃ, mayhamaiva sāvakānaṃ dātabbaṃ; mayhamaiva din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ, na añ‍ñaisaṃ din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ; mayhamaiva sāvakānaṃ din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ, na añ‍ñaisaṃ sāvakānaṃ din‍naṃ mahapphala’nti, atha ca pana maṃ bhagavā nigaṇṭhaisupi dānai  samādapaiti samādāpaiti (?)| api ca, bhantai, mayamaittha kālaṃ jānissāma| aisāhaṃ, bhantai, tatiyampi bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

atha khau bhagavā sīhassa saināpatissa anupubbiṃ kathaṃ anupubbikathaṃ (sabbattha) kathaisi, saiyyathidaṃ – dānakathaṃ sīlakathaṃ saggakathaṃ, kāmānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsaisi| yadā bhagavā añ‍ñāsi sīhaṃ saināpatiṃ kal‍lacittaṃ muducittaṃ vinīvaraṇacittaṃ udaggacittaṃ pasan‍nacittaṃ, atha yā buddhānaṃ sāmuk‍kaṃsikā dhammadaisanā taṃ pakāsaisi – dukkhaṃ samudayaṃ niraudhaṃ maggaṃ| saiyyathāpi nāma suddhaṃ vatthaṃ apagatakāḷakaṃ sammadaiva rajanaṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyya; aivamaivaṃ sīhassa saināpatissa tasmiṃyaiva āsanai virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’’nti|

atha khau sīhau saināpati diṭṭhadhammau pattadhammau viditadhammau pariyaugāḷhadhammau tiṇṇavicikicchau vigatakathaṃkathau vaisāraj‍jappattau aparappac‍cayau satthusāsanai bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaitu mai, bhantai, bhagavā svātanāya  bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina|

atha khau sīhau saināpati bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau sīhau saināpati añ‍ñataraṃ purisaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘gaccha tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, pavattamaṃsaṃ jānāhī’’ti| atha khau sīhau saināpati tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina sakai nivaisanai paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā bhagavatau  kālaṃ āraucāpaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhantai! niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti|

atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina sīhassa saināpatissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina| taina khau pana  samayaina sambahulā nigaṇṭhā vaisāliyaṃ rathikāya rathikaṃ rathiyāya rathiyaṃ (bahūsu) siṅghāṭakaina siṅghāṭakaṃ bāhā paggayha kandanti – ‘‘aj‍ja sīhaina saināpatinā thūlaṃ pasuṃ vadhitvā samaṇassa gautamassa bhattaṃ kataṃ| taṃ samaṇau gautamau jānaṃ uddissakataṃ maṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jati paṭic‍cakamma’’nti|

atha khau añ‍ñatarau purisau yaina sīhau saināpati tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā sīhassa saināpatissa upakaṇṇakai āraucaisi – ‘‘yagghai, bhantai, jānaiyyāsi! aitai sambahulā nigaṇṭhā vaisāliyaṃ rathikāya rathikaṃ siṅghāṭakaina siṅghāṭakaṃ bāhā paggayha kandanti – ‘aj‍ja sīhaina saināpatinā thūlaṃ pasuṃ vadhitvā samaṇassa gautamassa bhattaṃ kataṃ| taṃ samaṇau gautamau jānaṃ uddissakataṃ maṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jati  paṭic‍cakamma’nti| alaṃ ayyau dīgharattañhi tai āyasmantau avaṇṇakāmā buddhassa avaṇṇakāmā dhammassa avaṇṇakāmā saṅghassa| na ca panaitai āyasmantau jiridanti taṃ bhagavantaṃ asatā tucchā musā abhūtaina abbhācikkhituṃ; na ca mayaṃ jīvitahaitupi sañ‍cic‍ca pāṇaṃ jīvitā vauraupaiyyāmā’’ti|

atha khau sīhau saināpati buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaisi sampavāraisi| atha khau sīhau saināpati  bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau sīhaṃ saināpatiṃ bhagavā dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmīti| dutiyaṃ|

3. assājānīyasuttaṃ

13. ‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau bhaddau assājānīyau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau bhaddau assājānīyau ubhatau sujātau hauti – mātitau ca pititau ca| yassaṃ disāyaṃ añ‍ñaipi bhaddā assājānīyā jāyanti, tassaṃ disāyaṃ jātau hauti| yaṃ khau panassa bhaujanaṃ dainti – al‍laṃ vā sukkhaṃ vā – taṃ sak‍kac‍caṃyaiva paribhuñ‍jati avikirantau| jaigucchī hauti uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā abhinisīdituṃ vā abhinipaj‍jituṃ vā| sauratau hauti  sukhasaṃvāsau, na ca añ‍ñai assai ubbaijaitā| yāni khau panassa haunti yāni khau panassa tāni (syā॰) sāṭhaiyyāni kūṭaiyyāni jimhaiyyāni vaṅkaiyyāni, tāni yathābhūtaṃ sārathissa āvikattā hauti| taisamassa sārathi  abhinimmadanāya vāyamati| vāhī khau pana hauti| ‘kāmañ‍ñai assā vahantu vā mā vā, ahamaittha vahissāmī’ti cittaṃ uppādaiti| gacchantau khau pana ujumaggainaiva gacchati| thāmavā hauti yāva jīvitamaraṇapariyādānā thāmaṃ upadaṃsaitā| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau bhaddau assājānīyau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahi dhammaihi  saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| yaṃ khau panassa bhaujanaṃ dainti – lūkhaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā – taṃ sak‍kac‍caṃyaiva paribhuñ‍jati avihañ‍ñamānau| jaigucchī hauti kāyaduc‍caritaina vacīduc‍caritaina manauduc‍caritaina; jaigucchī hauti anaikavihitānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā| sauratau hauti sukhasaṃvāsau, na añ‍ñai bhikkhū ubbaijaitā| yāni khau panassa haunti sāṭhaiyyāni kūṭaiyyāni  jimhaiyyāni vaṅkaiyyāni, tāni yathābhūtaṃ āvikattā hauti satthari vā viñ‍ñūsu vā sabrahmacārīsu| taisamassa satthā vā viñ‍ñū vā sabrahmacārī abhinimmadanāya vāyamati| sikkhitā khau pana hauti| ‘kāmañ‍ñai  bhikkhū sikkhantu vā mā vā, ahamaittha sikkhissāmī’ti cittaṃ uppādaiti| gacchantau khau pana ujumaggainaiva gacchati; tatrāyaṃ ujumaggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| āraddhavīriyau viharati – ‘kāmaṃ tacau ca nhāru nahāru (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ca aṭṭhi ca avasissatu, sarīrai upasussatu maṃsalauhitaṃ; yaṃ taṃ purisathāmaina purisavīriyaina purisaparak‍kamaina pattabbaṃ, na taṃ apāpuṇitvā vīriyassa saṇṭhānaṃ bhavissatī’ti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. assakhaḷuṅkasuttaṃ

14. ‘‘aṭṭha ca aṭṭha (syā॰), bhikkhavai, assakhaḷuṅkai assakhaluṅkai (sī॰) daisaissāmi aṭṭha ca assadausai, aṭṭha ca purisakhaḷuṅkai aṭṭha ca purisadausai| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamai  ca, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha assakhaḷuṅkā aṭṭha ca assadausā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau assakhaḷuṅkau ‘paihī’ti vuttau, viddhau samānau cauditau sārathinā pacchatau paṭik‍kamati, piṭṭhitau  rathaṃ pavattaiti| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau assakhaḷuṅkau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau assadausau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau assakhaḷuṅkau ‘paihī’ti vuttau, viddhau samānau cauditau sārathinā pacchā laṅghati, kubbaraṃ hanati, tidaṇḍaṃ bhañ‍jati| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau assakhaḷuṅkau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau assadausau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau assakhaḷuṅkau ‘paihī’ti vuttau, viddhau samānau cauditau sārathinā rathīsāya satthiṃ ussaj‍jitvā rathīsaṃyaiva ajjhaumaddati| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau assakhaḷuṅkau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau assadausau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau assakhaḷuṅkau ‘paihī’ti vuttau, viddhau samānau cauditau sārathinā ummaggaṃ gaṇhati, ubbaṭumaṃ rathaṃ karauti| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau assakhaḷuṅkau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau assadausau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau assakhaḷuṅkau ‘paihī’ti vuttau, viddhau samānau cauditau sārathinā laṅghati purimakāyaṃ paggaṇhati purimai pādai| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau assakhaḷuṅkau  hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camau assadausau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau assakhaḷuṅkau ‘paihī’ti vuttau, viddhau samānau cauditau sārathinā anādiyitvā sārathiṃ anādiyitvā pataudalaṭṭhiṃ pataudaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), pataudayaṭṭhiṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) dantaihi mukhādhānaṃ mukhāṭhānaṃ (ka॰) vidhaṃsitvā  yaina kāmaṃ pak‍kamati| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau assakhaḷuṅkau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, chaṭṭhau assadausau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau assakhaḷuṅkau ‘paihī’ti vuttau, viddhau samānau cauditau  sārathinā naiva abhik‍kamati nau paṭik‍kamati tatthaiva khīlaṭṭhāyī ṭhitau hauti| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau assakhaḷuṅkau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, sattamau assadausau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau assakhaḷuṅkau ‘paihī’ti vuttau, viddhau samānau cauditau sārathinā purimai ca pādai saṃharitvā pacchimai ca pādai saṃharitvā saṅkharitvā (ka॰) tatthaiva cattārau pādai abhinisīdati| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau assakhaḷuṅkau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhamau assadausau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha assakhaḷuṅkā aṭṭha ca assadausā|

vibha॰ 956 ‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha purisakhaḷuṅkā aṭṭha ca purisadausā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā caudainti| sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi āpattiyā caudiyamānau ‘na sarāmī’ti asatiyā nibbaiṭhaiti| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, assakhaḷuṅkau ‘paihī’ti vuttau, viddhau samānau cauditau sārathinā pacchatau paṭik‍kamati, piṭṭhitau rathaṃ vattaiti; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau purisakhaḷuṅkau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau purisadausau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā caudainti| sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi āpattiyā caudiyamānau caudakaṃyaiva  paṭippharati – ‘kiṃ nu khau tuyhaṃ bālassa abyattassa bhaṇitaina! tvampi nāma bhaṇitabbaṃ mañ‍ñasī’ti! saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, assakhaḷuṅkau ‘paihī’ti vuttau, viddhau samānau cauditau sārathinā pacchā laṅghati, kubbaraṃ hanati, tidaṇḍaṃ bhañ‍jati; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau purisakhaḷuṅkau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau purisadausau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā caudainti| sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi āpattiyā caudiyamānau caudakassaiva pac‍cāraupaiti – ‘tvaṃ khausi itthan‍nāmaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nau, tvaṃ tāva paṭhamaṃ paṭikarauhī’ti| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, assakhaḷuṅkau ‘paihī’ti vuttau, viddhau samānau cauditau sārathinā rathīsāya satthiṃ ussaj‍jitvā rathīsaṃyaiva ajjhaumaddati; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau purisakhaḷuṅkau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau purisadausau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā caudainti| sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi āpattiyā caudiyamānau añ‍ñaināñ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati, bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmaiti, kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, assakhaḷuṅkau ‘paihī’ti vuttau, viddhau samānau cauditau sārathinā ummaggaṃ gaṇhati, ubbaṭumaṃ rathaṃ karauti; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau purisakhaḷuṅkau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau purisadausau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā  caudainti| sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi āpattiyā caudiyamānau saṅghamajjhai bāhuvikkhaipaṃ karauti| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, assakhaḷuṅkau ‘paihī’ti vuttau, viddhau samānau cauditau sārathinā laṅghati, purimakāyaṃ paggaṇhati purimai pādai; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau purisakhaḷuṅkau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camau purisadausau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā caudainti| sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi āpattiyā caudiyamānau anādiyitvā saṅghaṃ anādiyitvā caudakaṃ sāpattikauva yaina kāmaṃ pak‍kamati| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, assakhaḷuṅkau ‘paihī’ti vuttau, viddhau samānau cauditau sārathinā anādiyitvā sārathiṃ anādiyitvā pataudalaṭṭhiṃ dantaihi mukhādhānaṃ vidhaṃsitvā yaina kāmaṃ pak‍kamati; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau purisakhaḷuṅkau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, chaṭṭhau purisadausau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā caudainti| sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi āpattiyā caudiyamānau ‘naivāhaṃ āpan‍naumhi, na panāhaṃ āpan‍naumhī’ti sau tuṇhībhāvaina saṅghaṃ vihaiṭhaiti vihaisaiti (pī॰ ka॰)| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, assakhaḷuṅkau ‘paihī’ti vuttau, viddhau samānau cauditau sārathinā naiva abhik‍kamati nau paṭik‍kamati tatthaiva khīlaṭṭhāyī ṭhitau hauti; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau purisakhaḷuṅkau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, sattamau purisadausau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā caudainti| sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi āpattiyā  caudiyamānau aivamāha  – ‘kiṃ nu khau tumhai āyasmantau atibāḷhaṃ mayi byāvaṭā yāva idaṃ padaṃ sīhaḷapautthakai natthi idānāhaṃ sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattissāmī’ti| sau sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattitvā aivamāha – ‘idāni khau tumhai āyasmantau attamanā hauthā’ti? saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, assakhaḷuṅkau ‘paihī’ti vuttau, viddhau samānau cauditau sārathinā purimai ca pādai saṃharitvā pacchimai ca pādai saṃharitvā tatthaiva cattārau pādai abhinisīdati; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau purisakhaḷuṅkau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhamau purisadausau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha purisakhaḷuṅkā aṭṭha ca purisadausā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. malasuttaṃ

15. ‘‘aṭṭhimāni, bhikkhavai, malāni| katamāni aṭṭha? asajjhāyamalā, bhikkhavai, mantā; anuṭṭhānamalā, bhikkhavai, gharā; malaṃ, bhikkhavai, vaṇṇassa kausaj‍jaṃ; pamādau, bhikkhavai, rakkhatau malaṃ; malaṃ, bhikkhavai, itthiyā duc‍caritaṃ; macchairaṃ, bhikkhavai, dadatau malaṃ; malā, bhikkhavai, pāpakā akusalā dhammā asmiṃ laukai paramhi ca; tatau tatau ca (syā॰ pī॰), bhikkhavai, malā malataraṃ avij‍jā paramaṃ malaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha malānī’’ti|

‘‘asajjhāyamalā mantā, anuṭṭhānamalā gharā|

malaṃ vaṇṇassa kausaj‍jaṃ, pamādau rakkhatau malaṃ||

‘‘malitthiyā duc‍caritaṃ, macchairaṃ dadatau malaṃ|

malā vai pāpakā dhammā, asmiṃ laukai paramhi ca|

tatau malā malataraṃ, avij‍jā paramaṃ mala’’nti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dūtaiyyasuttaṃ

16. cūḷava॰ 347 ‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu dūtaiyyaṃ gantumarahati| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sautā ca hauti, sāvaitā ca, uggahaitā ca, dhāraitā ca, viñ‍ñātā  ca, viñ‍ñāpaitā ca, kusalau ca sahitāsahitassa, nau ca kalahakārakau – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu dūtaiyyaṃ gantumarahati| aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau sāriputtau dūtaiyyaṃ gantumarahati| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? idha, bhikkhavai, sāriputtau sautā ca hauti, sāvaitā ca, uggahaitā ca, dhāraitā ca, viñ‍ñātā ca, viñ‍ñāpaitā ca, kusalau ca sahitāsahitassa, nau ca kalahakārakau| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau sāriputtau dūtaiyyaṃ gantumarahatī’’ti|

‘‘yau vai na byathati na vaidhati (sī॰), na byādhati (syā॰ pī॰) patvā, parisaṃ uggavādiniṃ uggavādinaṃ (sī॰), uggahavādinaṃ (syā॰ pī॰), uggatavādiniṃ (ka॰)|

na ca hāpaiti vacanaṃ, na ca chādaiti sāsanaṃ||

‘‘asandiddhañ‍ca  bhaṇati asandiddhau ca akkhāti (cūḷava॰ 347), pucchitau na ca kuppati|

sa vai tādisakau bhikkhu, dūtaiyyaṃ gantumarahatī’’ti|| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamabandhanasuttaṃ

17. ‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, ākāraihi itthī purisaṃ bandhati| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? ruṇṇaina, bhikkhavai, itthī purisaṃ bandhati; hasitaina, bhikkhavai, itthī purisaṃ bandhati; bhaṇitaina, bhikkhavai, itthī purisaṃ bandhati; ākappaina, bhikkhavai, itthī purisaṃ bandhati; vanabhaṅgaina, bhikkhavai, itthī purisaṃ bandhati; gandhaina, bhikkhavai, itthī purisaṃ bandhati; rasaina, bhikkhavai, itthī purisaṃ bandhati; phassaina, bhikkhavai, itthī purisaṃ bandhati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahākāraihi  itthī purisaṃ bandhati| tai, bhikkhavai, sattā subaddhā subandhā (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰), yai yaiva (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) phassaina baddhā’’ti bandhāti (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰)| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyabandhanasuttaṃ

18. ‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, ākāraihi purisau itthiṃ bandhati| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? ruṇṇaina, bhikkhavai, purisau itthiṃ bandhati; hasitaina, bhikkhavai, purisau itthiṃ bandhati; bhaṇitaina, bhikkhavai, purisau itthiṃ bandhati; ākappaina, bhikkhavai, purisau itthiṃ bandhati; vanabhaṅgaina, bhikkhavai, purisau itthiṃ bandhati; gandhaina, bhikkhavai, purisau itthiṃ bandhati; rasaina, bhikkhavai, purisau itthiṃ bandhati; phassaina, bhikkhavai, purisau itthiṃ bandhati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahākāraihi purisau itthiṃ bandhati| tai, bhikkhavai, sattā subaddhā, yai phassaina baddhā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. pahārādasuttaṃ

19. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vairañ‍jāyaṃ vi harati naḷairupucimandamūlai| atha khau pahārādau asurindau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ khau pahārādaṃ asurindaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘api  kiṃ (ka॰) pana, pahārāda, asurā mahāsamuddai abhiramantī’’ti? ‘‘abhiramanti, bhantai, asurā mahāsamuddai’’ti| ‘‘kati pana, pahārāda, mahāsamuddai acchariyā abbhutā dhammā abbhutadhammā (syā॰ ka॰) cūḷava॰ 384 passitabbaṃ, yai disvā disvā asurā mahāsamuddai abhiramantī’’ti? ‘‘aṭṭha, bhantai, mahāsamuddai acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, yai disvā disvā asurā mahāsamuddai abhiramanti| katamai aṭṭha? mahāsamuddau, bhantai, anupubbanin‍nau anupubbapauṇau anupubbapabbhārau, na āyatakainaiva papātau| yampi, bhantai, mahāsamuddau anupubbanin‍nau anupubbapauṇau anupubbapabbhārau, na āyatakainaiva papātau| ayaṃ, bhantai, mahāsamuddai paṭhamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamuddai abhiramanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, mahāsamuddau ṭhitadhammau vailaṃ nātivattati| yampi, bhantai, mahāsamuddau ṭhitadhammau vailaṃ nātivattati; ayaṃ ayampi (ka॰), bhantai, mahāsamuddai dutiyau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamuddai abhiramanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, mahāsamuddau na mataina kuṇapaina saṃvasati saṃvattati (syā॰)| yaṃ hauti mahāsamuddai mataṃ kuṇapaṃ, taṃ khippamaiva khippaṃyaiva (sī॰), khippaṃaiva (pī॰), khippañ‍ñaiva (cūḷava॰ 384) tīraṃ vāhaiti, thalaṃ ussāraiti| yampi, bhantai, mahāsamuddau na mataina kuṇapaina saṃvasati, yaṃ hauti mahāsamuddai mataṃ kuṇapaṃ, taṃ khippamaiva tīraṃ vāhaiti, thalaṃ ussāraiti; ayaṃ, bhantai, mahāsamuddai tatiyau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamuddai abhiramanti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhantai, yā kāci mahānadiyau, saiyyathidaṃ – gaṅgā yamunā aciravatī sarabhū mahī, tā mahāsamuddaṃ patvā pattā (ka॰, cūḷava॰ 384) jahanti purimāni nāmagauttāni, ‘mahāsamuddau’  tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchanti| yampi, bhantai, yā kāci mahānadiyau, saiyyathidaṃ – gaṅgā yamunā aciravatī sarabhū mahī, tā mahāsamuddaṃ patvā jahanti purimāni nāmagauttāni, ‘mahāsamuddau’ tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchanti; ayaṃ, bhantai, mahāsamuddai catutthau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamuddai abhiramanti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhantai, yā ca yā kāci (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) laukai savantiyau mahāsamuddaṃ  appainti yā ca antalikkhā dhārā papatanti, na taina mahāsamuddassa ūnattaṃ vā pūrattaṃ vā pañ‍ñāyati| yampi, bhantai, yā ca laukai savantiyau mahāsamuddaṃ appainti yā ca antalikkhā dhārā papatanti, na taina mahāsamuddassa ūnattaṃ vā pūrattaṃ vā pañ‍ñāyati; ayaṃ, bhantai, mahāsamuddai pañ‍camau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamuddai abhiramanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, mahāsamuddau aikarasau lauṇarasau| yampi, bhantai, mahāsamuddau aikarasau lauṇarasau; ayaṃ, bhantai, mahāsamuddai chaṭṭhau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamuddai abhiramanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, mahāsamuddau bahuratanau pahūtaratanau (ka॰) anaikaratanau| tatrimāni ratanāni, saiyyathidaṃ – muttā maṇi vaiḷuriyau saṅkhau silā pavāḷaṃ rajataṃ jātarūpaṃ lauhitakau masāragal‍laṃ| yampi, bhantai, mahāsamuddau bahuratanau anaikaratanau; tatrimāni ratanāni, saiyyathidaṃ – muttā maṇi vaiḷuriyau saṅkhau silā pavāḷaṃ rajataṃ jātarūpaṃ lauhitakau masāragal‍laṃ| ayaṃ, bhantai, mahāsamuddai  sattamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamuddai abhiramanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, mahāsamuddau mahataṃ bhūtānaṃ āvāsau| tatrimai bhūtā – timi timiṅgalau timirapiṅgalau timitimiṅgalā timirapiṅgalā (sī॰), timitimiṅgalā timiramiṅgalā (syā॰ pī॰) asurā nāgā gandhabbā| santi mahāsamuddai yaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā, dviyaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā, tiyaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā, catuyaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā, pañ‍cayaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā| yampi, bhantai, mahāsamuddau mahataṃ  bhūtānaṃ  āvāsau; tatrimai bhūtā – timi timiṅgalau timirapiṅgalau asurā nāgā gandhabbā; santi mahāsamuddai yaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā…pai॰… dviyaujana… tiyaujana… catuyaujana… pañ‍cayaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā; ayaṃ, bhantai, mahāsamuddai aṭṭhamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamuddai abhiramanti| imai khau, bhantai, mahāsamuddai aṭṭha acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, yai disvā disvā asurā mahāsamuddai abhiramantīti|

‘‘api pana, bhantai, bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramantī’’ti? ‘‘abhiramanti, pahārāda, bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai’’ti| ‘‘kati pana, bhantai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, yai disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramantī’’ti? ‘‘aṭṭha, pahārāda, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, yai disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramanti| katamai aṭṭha? saiyyathāpi, pahārāda, mahāsamuddau anupubbanin‍nau anupubbapauṇau anupubbapabbhārau, na āyatakainaiva papātau; aivamaivaṃ khau, pahārāda, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai anupubbasikkhā anupubbakiriyā anupubbapaṭipadā, na āyatakainaiva añ‍ñāpaṭivaidhau| yampi, pahārāda, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai anupubbasikkhā anupubbakiriyā anupubbapaṭipadā, na āyatakainaiva añ‍ñāpaṭivaidhau| ayaṃ, pahārāda, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai paṭhamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramanti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, pahārāda, mahāsamuddau ṭhitadhammau vailaṃ nātivattati; aivamaivaṃ khau, pahārāda, yaṃ mayā sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ taṃ mama sāvakā  jīvitahaitupi nātik‍kamanti| yampi, pahārāda, mayā sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ taṃ mama sāvakā jīvitahaitupi nātik‍kamanti| ayaṃ, pahārāda, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai dutiyau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramanti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, pahārāda, mahāsamuddau na mataina kuṇapaina saṃvasati| yaṃ hauti mahāsamuddai mataṃ kuṇapaṃ, taṃ khippamaiva tīraṃ vāhaiti thalaṃ ussāraiti; aivamaivaṃ khau, pahārāda, yau sau puggalau dussīlau pāpadhammau asucisaṅkassarasamācārau paṭicchan‍nakammantau assamaṇau samaṇapaṭiñ‍ñau  abrahmacārī brahmacāripaṭiñ‍ñau antaupūti avassutau kasambujātau, na taina saṅghau saṃvasati; khippamaiva naṃ san‍nipatitvā ukkhipati|

‘‘kiñ‍cāpi sau hauti majjhai bhikkhusaṅghassa san‍nisin‍nau, atha khau sau ārakāva saṅghamhā saṅghau ca taina| yampi, pahārāda, yau sau puggalau  dussīlau pāpadhammau asucisaṅkassarasamācārau paṭicchan‍nakammantau assamaṇau samaṇapaṭiñ‍ñau abrahmacārī brahmacāripaṭiñ‍ñau antaupūti avassutau kasambujātau, na  taina saṅghau saṃvasati; khippamaiva naṃ san‍nipatitvā ukkhipati; kiñ‍cāpi sau hauti majjhai bhikkhusaṅghassa san‍nisin‍nau, atha khau sau ārakāva saṅghamhā saṅghau ca taina| ayaṃ, pahārāda, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai tatiyau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramanti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, pahārāda, yā kāci mahānadiyau, saiyyathidaṃ – gaṅgā yamunā aciravatī sarabhū mahī, tā mahāsamuddaṃ patvā jahanti purimāni nāmagauttāni, ‘mahāsamuddau’  tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchanti; aivamaivaṃ khau, pahārāda, cattāraumai vaṇṇā – khattiyā, brāhmaṇā, vaissā, suddā, tai tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitvā jahanti purimāni nāmagauttāni, ‘samaṇā sakyaputtiyā’ tvaiva samaṇau sakyaputtiyau tvaiva (syā॰ ka॰) saṅkhaṃ gacchanti| yampi, pahārāda, cattāraumai vaṇṇā – khattiyā, brāhmaṇā, vaissā, suddā, tai tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitvā jahanti purimāni nāmagauttāni, ‘samaṇā sakyaputtiyā’ tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchanti| ayaṃ, pahārāda, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai catutthau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramanti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, pahārāda, yā ca laukai savantiyau mahāsamuddaṃ appainti yā ca antalikkhā dhārā papatanti, na taina mahāsamuddassa ūnattaṃ vā pūrattaṃ vā pañ‍ñāyati; aivamaivaṃ khau, pahārāda, bahū caipi bhikkhū anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyanti, na taina nibbānadhātuyā ūnattaṃ vā pūrattaṃ vā pañ‍ñāyati| yampi, pahārāda, bahū caipi bhikkhū anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā  parinibbāyanti, na taina nibbānadhātuyā ūnattaṃ vā pūrattaṃ vā pañ‍ñāyati| ayaṃ, pahārāda, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai pañ‍camau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramanti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, pahārāda, mahāsamuddau aikarasau lauṇarasau; aivamaivaṃ khau, pahārāda, ayaṃ dhammavinayau aikarasau, vimuttirasau| yampi pahārāda, ayaṃ dhammavinayau aikarasau, vimuttirasau; ayaṃ, pahārāda, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai chaṭṭhau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramanti|
