‘‘saiyyathāpi, pahārāda, mahāsamuddau bahuratanau anaikaratanau; tatrimāni ratanāni, saiyyathidaṃ – muttā maṇi vaiḷuriyau saṅkhau silā pavāḷaṃ rajataṃ jātarūpaṃ lauhitakau masāragal‍laṃ; aivamaivaṃ khau, pahārāda, ayaṃ dhammavinayau bahuratanau anaikaratanau| tatrimāni ratanāni, saiyyathidaṃ – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā, cattārau sammappadhānā, cattārau iddhipādā, pañ‍cindriyāni, pañ‍ca balāni, satta baujjhaṅgā, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau| yampi, pahārāda, ayaṃ dhammavinayau bahuratanau anaikaratanau; tatrimāni ratanāni, saiyyathidaṃ – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā, cattārau sammappadhānā, cattārau iddhipādā, pañ‍cindriyāni, pañ‍ca balāni, satta baujjhaṅgā, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau; ayaṃ, pahārāda, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai sattamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramanti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, pahārāda, mahāsamuddau mahataṃ bhūtānaṃ āvāsau; tatrimai bhūtā – timi timiṅgalau timirapiṅgalau  asurā nāgā gandhabbā; santi mahāsamuddai yaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā, dviyaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā, tiyaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā, catuyaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā, pañ‍cayaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā; aivamaivaṃ khau, pahārāda, ayaṃ dhammavinayau mahataṃ bhūtānaṃ āvāsau; tatrimai bhūtā – sautāpan‍nau sautāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau, sakadāgāmī sakadāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya  paṭipan‍nau, anāgāmī anāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau, arahā arahattāya paṭipan‍nau| yampi, pahārāda, ayaṃ dhammavinayau mahataṃ bhūtānaṃ āvāsau; tatrimai bhūtā – sautāpan‍nau sautāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau, sakadāgāmī sakadāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau, anāgāmī anāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau, arahā arahattāya paṭipan‍nau; ayaṃ, pahārāda, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai aṭṭhamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramanti| imai khau, pahārāda, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai aṭṭha  acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, yai disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramantī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. upausathasuttaṃ

20. cūḷava॰ 383; udā॰ 45; kathā॰ 346 aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati pubbārāmai migāramātupāsādai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā tadahupausathai bhikkhusaṅghaparivutau nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau āyasmā ānandau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā, nikkhantai paṭhamai yāmai, uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantā, bhantai, ratti, nikkhantau paṭhamau yāmau, ciranisin‍nau bhikkhusaṅghau| uddisatu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ pātimaukkha’’nti|

aivaṃ vuttai bhagavā tuṇhī ahausi| dutiyampi  khau āyasmā ānandau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā, nikkhantai majjhimai yāmai, uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantā, bhantai, ratti, nikkhantau majjhimau yāmau, ciranisin‍nau bhikkhusaṅghau| uddisatu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ pātimaukkha’’nti| dutiyampi khau bhagavā tuṇhī ahausi| tatiyampi khau āyasmā ānandau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā, nikkhantai pacchimai yāmai, uddhastai aruṇai, nandimukhiyā rattiyā uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantā, bhantai, ratti, nikkhantau pacchimau yāmau, uddhastaṃ aruṇaṃ, nandimukhī ratti; ciranisin‍nau bhikkhusaṅghau| uddisatu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ pātimaukkha’’nti| ‘‘aparisuddhā, ānanda, parisā’’ti|

atha khau āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa aitadahausi – ‘‘kaṃ nu khau bhagavā puggalaṃ sandhāya aivamāha – ‘aparisuddhā, ānanda, parisā’’’ti? atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau sabbāvantaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca manasākāsi| addasā khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau taṃ puggalaṃ dussīlaṃ pāpadhammaṃ asuciṃ saṅkassarasamācāraṃ paṭicchan‍nakammantaṃ assamaṇaṃ samaṇapaṭiñ‍ñaṃ abrahmacāriṃ brahmacāripaṭiñ‍ñaṃ antaupūtiṃ avassutaṃ kasambujātaṃ majjhai bhikkhusaṅghassa nisin‍naṃ; disvāna uṭṭhāyāsanā yaina sau puggalau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ puggalaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘uṭṭhaihāvusau, diṭṭhausi bhagavatā| natthi tai bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ saṃvāsau’’ti|

aivaṃ  vuttai sau puggalau tuṇhī ahausi| dutiyampi khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau taṃ puggalaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘uṭṭhaihāvusau, diṭṭhausi bhagavatā| natthi  tai bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ saṃvāsau’’ti| dutiyampi khau sau puggalau tuṇhī ahausi| tatiyampi khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau taṃ puggalaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘uṭṭhaihāvusau, diṭṭhausi bhagavatā| natthi tai bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ saṃvāsau’’ti| tatiyampi khau sau puggalau tuṇhī ahausi|

atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau taṃ puggalaṃ bāhāyaṃ gahaitvā bahidvārakauṭṭhakā nikkhāmaitvā sūcighaṭikaṃ datvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘nikkhāmitau sau, bhantai, puggalau mayā| parisuddhā parisā| uddisatu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ pātimaukkha’’nti| ‘‘acchariyaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, abbhutaṃ, mauggal‍lāna! yāva bāhā gahaṇāpi nāma sau maughapurisau āgamissatī’’ti!

atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘tumhaiva dāni, bhikkhavai, upausathaṃ karaiyyātha, pātimaukkhaṃ uddisaiyyātha| na dānāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aj‍jataggai upausathaṃ karissāmi, pātimaukkhaṃ uddisissāmi| aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, anavakāsau yaṃ tathāgatau aparisuddhāya parisāya pātimaukkhaṃ uddisaiyya’’|

‘‘aṭṭhimai, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddai acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, yai disvā disvā asurā mahāsamuddai abhiramanti| katamai aṭṭha? mahāsamuddau, bhikkhavai, anupubbanin‍nau anupubbapauṇau anupubbapabbhārau, na āyatakainaiva papātau| yampi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau anupubbanin‍nau anupubbapauṇau anupubbapabbhārau, na āyatakainaiva papātau; ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddai paṭhamau  acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamuddai abhiramanti…pai॰… (yathā purimai tathā vitthāraitabbau)|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau mahataṃ bhūtānaṃ āvāsau| tatrimai bhūtā – timi timiṅgalau timirapiṅgalau  asurā nāgā gandhabbā| vasanti mahāsamuddai yaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā…pai॰… pañ‍cayaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā| yampi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau mahataṃ bhūtānaṃ āvāsau; tatrimai bhūtā – timi timiṅgalau timirapiṅgalau asurā nāgā gandhabbā; vasanti  mahāsamuddai yaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā…pai॰… pañ‍cayaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā; ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddai aṭṭhamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamuddai abhiramanti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddai aṭṭha acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamuddai abhiramanti|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha imasmiṃ dhammavinayai acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, yai disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramanti| katamai aṭṭha? saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau anupubbanin‍nau anupubbapauṇau anupubbapabbhārau, na āyatakainaiva papātau; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai anupubbasikkhā anupubbakiriyā anupubbapaṭipadā, na āyatakainaiva añ‍ñāpaṭivaidhau| yampi, bhikkhavai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai anupubbasikkhā anupubbakiriyā anupubbapaṭipadā, na āyatakainaiva añ‍ñāpaṭivaidhau; ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai paṭhamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā  bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramanti…pai॰… saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau mahataṃ bhūtānaṃ āvāsau; tatrimai bhūtā – timi timiṅgalau timirapiṅgalau asurā nāgā gandhabbā, vasanti mahāsamuddai yaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā…pai॰… pañ‍cayaujanasatikāpi  attabhāvā; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, ayaṃ dhammavinayau mahataṃ bhūtānaṃ āvāsau| tatrimai bhūtā – sautāpan‍nau sautāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau…pai॰… arahā arahattāya paṭipan‍nau| yampi, bhikkhavai, ayaṃ dhammavinayau mahataṃ bhūtānaṃ āvāsau; tatrimai bhūtā – sautāpan‍nau sautāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau…pai॰… arahā arahattāya paṭipan‍nau; ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai aṭṭhamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramanti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai aṭṭha acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, yai disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramantī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

mahāvaggau dutiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

vairañ‍jau  sīhau ājañ‍ñaṃ, khaḷuṅkaina malāni ca|

dūtaiyyaṃ dvai ca bandhanā, pahārādau upausathauti||


3. gahapativaggau

1. paṭhamauggasuttaṃ

21. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi ‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, acchariyaihi abbhutaihi dhammaihi abbhutadhammaihi (syā॰ ka॰) saman‍nāgataṃ uggaṃ gahapatiṃ vaisālikaṃ dhāraithā’’ti| idamavauca  bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ pāvisi|

atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina uggassa gahapatinau vaisālikassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau uggau gahapati vaisālikau yaina sau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau uggaṃ gahapatiṃ vaisālikaṃ sau bhikkhu aitadavauca –

‘‘aṭṭhahi khau tvaṃ, gahapati, acchariyaihi abbhutaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhagavatā byākatau| katamai tai, gahapati, aṭṭha acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, yaihi tvaṃ saman‍nāgatau bhagavatā byākatau’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, bhantai, jānāmi – katamaihi aṭṭhahi acchariyaihi abbhutaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhagavatā byākatauti| api ca, bhantai, yai mai aṭṭha acchariyā abbhutā dhammā saṃvij‍janti, taṃ suṇauhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, gahapatī’’ti khau sau bhikkhu uggassa gahapatinau vaisālikassa pac‍cassausi| uggau gahapati vaisālikau aitadavauca – ‘‘yadāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ paṭhamaṃ dūratauva addasaṃ; saha dassanainaiva mai, bhantai, bhagavatau cittaṃ pasīdi| ayaṃ khau mai, bhantai, paṭhamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau saṃvij‍jati’’|

‘‘sau khau ahaṃ, bhantai, pasan‍nacittau bhagavantaṃ payirupāsiṃ| tassa mai bhagavā anupubbiṃ kathaṃ  kathaisi, saiyyathidaṃ – dānakathaṃ sīlakathaṃ saggakathaṃ; kāmānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ, naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsaisi| yadā maṃ bhagavā añ‍ñāsi kal‍lacittaṃ muducittaṃ vinīvaraṇacittaṃ udaggacittaṃ pasan‍nacittaṃ, atha yā buddhānaṃ  sāmuk‍kaṃsikā dhammadaisanā taṃ pakāsaisi – dukkhaṃ, samudayaṃ, niraudhaṃ, maggaṃ| saiyyathāpi nāma suddhaṃ vatthaṃ apagatakāḷakaṃ sammadaiva rajanaṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyya; aivamaivaṃ khau mai tasmiṃyaiva āsanai virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’nti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhantai, diṭṭhadhammau pattadhammau viditadhammau pariyaugāḷhadhammau tiṇṇavicikicchau vigatakathaṃkathau vaisāraj‍jappattau aparappac‍cayau satthusāsanai tatthaiva buddhañ‍ca dhammañ‍ca saṅghañ‍ca saraṇaṃ agamāsiṃ, brahmacariyapañ‍camāni ca sikkhāpadāni samādiyiṃ| ayaṃ khau mai, bhantai, dutiyau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau saṃvij‍jati|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, catassau kaumāriyau pajāpatiyau ahaisuṃ| atha khvāhaṃ, bhantai, yaina tā pajāpatiyau tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā tā pajāpatiyau aitadavacaṃ – ‘mayā khau, bhaginiyau, brahmacariyapañ‍camāni sikkhāpadāni samādin‍nāni samādiṇṇāni (sī॰ ka॰)| yā icchati sā idhaiva bhaugai ca bhuñ‍jatu puñ‍ñāni ca karautu, sakāni vā ñātikulāni gacchatu| hauti vā pana purisādhippāyau, kassa vau dammī’ti? aivaṃ vuttai sā, bhantai, jaiṭṭhā pajāpati maṃ aitadavauca – ‘itthan‍nāmassa maṃ, ayyaputta, purisassa daihī’ti| atha khau ahaṃ, bhantai, taṃ purisaṃ pak‍kausāpaitvā vāmaina hatthaina pajāpatiṃ gahaitvā  dakkhiṇaina hatthaina bhiṅgāraṃ gahaitvā tassa purisassa auṇaujaisiṃ| kaumāriṃ khau panāhaṃ, bhantai, dāraṃ paric‍cajantau nābhijānāmi cittassa añ‍ñathattaṃ| ayaṃ khau mai, bhantai, tatiyau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau saṃvij‍jati|

‘‘saṃvij‍janti  khau pana mai, bhantai, kulai bhaugā| tai ca khau appaṭivibhattā sīlavantaihi kalyāṇadhammaihi| ayaṃ khau mai, bhantai, catutthau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau saṃvij‍jati|

‘‘yaṃ  khau panāhaṃ, bhantai, bhikkhuṃ payirupāsāmi; sak‍kac‍caṃyaiva payirupāsāmi, nau asak‍kac‍caṃ| ayaṃ khau mai, bhantai, pañ‍camau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau saṃvij‍jati|

‘‘sau  cai, bhantai, mai āyasmā dhammaṃ daisaiti; sak‍kac‍caṃyaiva suṇaumi, nau asak‍kac‍caṃ| nau cai mai sau āyasmā dhammaṃ daisaiti, ahamassa dhammaṃ daisaimi| ayaṃ khau mai, bhantai chaṭṭhau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau saṃvij‍jati|

‘‘anacchariyaṃ khau pana maṃ, bhantai, daivatā upasaṅkamitvā āraucainti – ‘svākkhātau, gahapati, bhagavatā dhammau’ti| aivaṃ vuttai ahaṃ, bhantai, tā daivatā aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘vadaiyyātha vā aivaṃ khau tumhai daivatā nau vā vadaiyyātha, atha khau svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau’ti| na khau panāhaṃ, bhantai, abhijānāmi tataunidānaṃ cittassa un‍natiṃ uṇṇatiṃ (ka॰) dha॰ sa॰ 1121; vibha॰ 843, 845 passitabbaṃ – ‘maṃ vā daivatā upasaṅkamanti, ahaṃ vā daivatāhi saddhiṃ sal‍lapāmī’ti| ayaṃ khau mai, bhantai, sattamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau saṃvij‍jati|

‘‘yānimāni, bhantai, bhagavatā daisitāni pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni, nāhaṃ taisaṃ kiñ‍ci attani appahīnaṃ samanupassāmi| ayaṃ  khau mai, bhantai, aṭṭhamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau saṃvij‍jati| imai  khau mai, bhantai, aṭṭha acchariyā abbhutā dhammā saṃvij‍janti| na ca khau ahaṃ jānāmi – katamaihi cāhaṃ katamaihipahaṃ (sī॰), katamaihipāhaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) aṭṭhahi acchariyaihi abbhutaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhagavatā byākatau’’ti|

atha khau sau bhikkhu uggassa gahapatinau vaisālikassa nivaisanai piṇḍapātaṃ gahaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi| atha khau sau bhikkhu pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu yāvatakau ahausi uggaina gahapatinā vaisālikaina saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpau, taṃ sabbaṃ bhagavatau āraucaisi|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhu! yathā taṃ uggau gahapati vaisālikau sammā byākaramānau byākaraiyya, imaihaiva khau, bhikkhu, aṭṭhahi acchariyaihi abbhutaihi  dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau uggau gahapati vaisālikau mayā byākatau| imaihi ca pana, bhikkhu, aṭṭhahi acchariyaihi abbhutaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ uggaṃ gahapatiṃ vaisālikaṃ dhāraihī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyauggasuttaṃ

22. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā vaj‍jīsu viharati hatthigāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, acchariyaihi abbhutaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ uggaṃ gahapatiṃ hatthigāmakaṃ dhāraithā’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ pāvisi|

atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya  yaina uggassa gahapatinau hatthigāmakassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau uggau gahapati hatthigāmakau yaina sau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau uggaṃ gahapatiṃ hatthigāmakaṃ sau bhikkhu aitadavauca – ‘‘aṭṭhahi  khau tvaṃ, gahapati, acchariyaihi abbhutaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhagavatā byākatau| katamai tai, gahapati, aṭṭha acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, yaihi tvaṃ saman‍nāgatau bhagavatā byākatau’’ti?

‘‘na khau ahaṃ, bhantai, jānāmi – katamaihi aṭṭhahi acchariyaihi abbhutaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhagavatā byākatauti| api ca, bhantai, yai mai aṭṭha acchariyā abbhutā dhammā saṃvij‍janti, taṃ suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, gahapatī’’ti khau sau bhikkhu uggassa gahapatinau hatthigāmakassa pac‍cassausi| uggau gahapati hatthigāmakau aitadavauca – ‘‘yadāhaṃ, bhantai, nāgavanai paricarantau bhagavantaṃ paṭhamaṃ dūratauva addasaṃ; saha dassanainaiva mai, bhantai, bhagavatau cittaṃ pasīdi, surāmadau ca pahīyi| ayaṃ khau mai, bhantai, paṭhamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau saṃvij‍jati|

‘‘sau khau ahaṃ, bhantai, pasan‍nacittau bhagavantaṃ payirupāsiṃ| tassa mai bhagavā anupubbiṃ kathaṃ kathaisi, saiyyathidaṃ – dānakathaṃ sīlakathaṃ saggakathaṃ; kāmānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ, naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsaisi| yadā maṃ bhagavā añ‍ñāsi kal‍lacittaṃ muducittaṃ vinīvaraṇacittaṃ udaggacittaṃ pasan‍nacittaṃ, atha yā  buddhānaṃ sāmuk‍kaṃsikā dhammadaisanā  taṃ pakāsaisi – dukkhaṃ, samudayaṃ, niraudhaṃ, maggaṃ| saiyyathāpi nāma suddhaṃ vatthaṃ apagatakāḷakaṃ sammadaiva  rajanaṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyya; aivamaivaṃ khau mai tasmiṃyaiva āsanai virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’nti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhantai, diṭṭhadhammau pattadhammau viditadhammau pariyaugāḷhadhammau tiṇṇavicikicchau vigatakathaṃkathau vaisāraj‍jappattau aparappac‍cayau satthusāsanai tatthaiva  buddhañ‍ca dhammañ‍ca saṅghañ‍ca saraṇaṃ agamāsiṃ, brahmacariyapañ‍camāni ca sikkhāpadāni samādiyiṃ| ayaṃ khau mai, bhantai, dutiyau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau saṃvij‍jati|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, catassau kaumāriyau pajāpatiyau ahaisuṃ| atha khvāhaṃ, bhantai, yaina tā pajāpatiyau tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā tā pajāpatiyau aitadavacaṃ – ‘mayā khau, bhaginiyau, brahmacariyapañ‍camāni sikkhāpadāni samādin‍nāni| yā icchati sā idhaiva bhaugai ca bhuñ‍jatu puñ‍ñāni ca karautu, sakāni vā ñātikulāni gacchatu| hauti vā pana purisādhippāyau, kassa vau dammī’ti? aivaṃ vuttai sā, bhantai, jaiṭṭhā pajāpati maṃ aitadavauca – ‘itthan‍nāmassa maṃ, ayyaputta, purisassa daihī’ti| atha khau ahaṃ, bhantai, taṃ purisaṃ pak‍kausāpaitvā vāmaina hatthaina pajāpatiṃ gahaitvā dakkhiṇaina hatthaina bhiṅgāraṃ gahaitvā tassa purisassa auṇaujaisiṃ| kaumāriṃ khau panāhaṃ, bhantai, dāraṃ paric‍cajantau nābhijānāmi cittassa añ‍ñathattaṃ| ayaṃ khau mai, bhantai, tatiyau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau saṃvij‍jati|

‘‘saṃvij‍janti khau pana  mai, bhantai, kulai bhaugā| tai ca khau appaṭivibhattā sīlavantaihi kalyāṇadhammaihi| ayaṃ khau mai, bhantai, catutthau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau saṃvij‍jati|

‘‘yaṃ  khau panāhaṃ, bhantai, bhikkhuṃ payirupāsāmi; sak‍kac‍caṃyaiva payirupāsāmi, nau asak‍kac‍caṃ| sau cai mai āyasmā dhammaṃ daisaiti; sak‍kac‍caṃyaiva suṇaumi, nau asak‍kac‍caṃ| nau cai mai sau āyasmā dhammaṃ daisaiti, ahamassa dhammaṃ daisaimi| ayaṃ khau mai, bhantai, pañ‍camau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau saṃvij‍jati|

‘‘anacchariyaṃ  khau pana, bhantai, saṅghai nimantitai daivatā upasaṅkamitvā āraucainti – ‘asukau, gahapati, bhikkhu ubhataubhāgavimuttau asukau pañ‍ñāvimuttau asukau kāyasakkhī asukau  diṭṭhippattau diṭṭhappattau (ka॰) asukau saddhāvimuttau asukau dhammānusārī asukau saddhānusārī asukau sīlavā kalyāṇadhammau asukau dussīlau pāpadhammau’ti| saṅghaṃ khau panāhaṃ, bhantai, parivisantau nābhijānāmi aivaṃ cittaṃ uppādaintau – ‘imassa vā thaukaṃ daimi imassa vā bahuka’nti| atha khvāhaṃ, bhantai, samacittauva daimi| ayaṃ khau mai, bhantai, chaṭṭhau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau saṃvij‍jati|

‘‘anacchariyaṃ khau pana maṃ, bhantai, daivatā upasaṅkamitvā āraucainti – ‘svākkhātau, gahapati, bhagavatā dhammau’ti| aivaṃ vuttai ahaṃ, bhantai, tā daivatā aivaṃ vadaimi – ‘vadaiyyātha vā aivaṃ khau tumhai daivatā nau vā vadaiyyātha, atha khau svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau’ti| na khau panāhaṃ, bhantai, abhijānāmi tataunidānaṃ cittassa un‍natiṃ – ‘maṃ tā daivatā upasaṅkamanti, ahaṃ vā  daivatāhi saddhiṃ sal‍lapāmī’ti| ayaṃ khau mai, bhantai, sattamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau saṃvij‍jati|

‘‘sacai  khau panāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau paṭhamataraṃ kālaṃ karaiyyaṃ, anacchariyaṃ khau panaitaṃ yaṃ maṃ bhagavā aivaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘natthi taṃ saṃyaujanaṃ yaina saṃyuttau uggau gahapati hatthigāmakau puna imaṃ laukaṃ āgacchaiyyā’ti| ayaṃ khau mai, bhantai, aṭṭhamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau saṃvij‍jati| imai khau mai, bhantai, aṭṭha acchariyā abbhutā dhammā saṃvij‍janti| na ca khau ahaṃ jānāmi – katamaihi cāhaṃ aṭṭhahi acchariyaihi abbhutaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhagavatā byākatau’’ti|

‘‘atha khau sau bhikkhu uggassa gahapatinau hatthigāmakassa nivaisanai piṇḍapātaṃ gahaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi| atha khau sau bhikkhu pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu yāvatakau ahausi uggaina gahapatinā hatthigāmakaina saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpau, taṃ sabbaṃ bhagavatau āraucaisi|

‘‘sādhu  sādhu, bhikkhu! yathā taṃ uggau gahapati hatthigāmakau sammā byākaramānau byākaraiyya, imaihaiva khau bhikkhu, aṭṭhahi acchariyaihi abbhutaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau uggau gahapati  hatthigāmakau mayā byākatau| imaihi ca pana, bhikkhu, aṭṭhahi acchariyaihi abbhutaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ uggaṃ gahapatiṃ hatthigāmakaṃ dhāraihī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. paṭhamahatthakasuttaṃ

23. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā āḷaviyaṃ viharati aggāḷavai caitiyai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘sattahi, bhikkhavai, acchariyaihi abbhutaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ hatthakaṃ āḷavakaṃ dhāraitha| katamaihi sattahi? saddhau hi, bhikkhavai, hatthakau āḷavakau; sīlavā, bhikkhavai, hatthakau āḷavakau; hirīmā, bhikkhavai, hatthakau āḷavakau; auttappī, bhikkhavai, hatthakau āḷavakau; bahussutau, bhikkhavai, hatthakau āḷavakau; cāgavā, bhikkhavai, hatthakau āḷavakau; pañ‍ñavā, bhikkhavai, hatthakau āḷavakau – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, sattahi acchariyaihi abbhutaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ hatthakaṃ āḷavakaṃ dhāraithā’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ pāvisi|

atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina hatthakassa āḷavakassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau hatthakau āḷavakau yaina sau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau hatthakaṃ āḷavakaṃ sau bhikkhu aitadavauca –

‘‘sattahi khau tvaṃ, āvusau, acchariyaihi abbhutaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhagavatā byākatau| katamaihi sattahi? ‘saddhau, bhikkhavai, hatthakau āḷavakau; sīlavā…pai॰… hirimā… auttappī… bahussutau… cāgavā… pañ‍ñavā, bhikkhavai, hatthakau āḷavakau’ti| imaihi khau tvaṃ, āvusau, sattahi acchariyaihi abbhutaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhagavatā byākatau’’ti| ‘‘kac‍cittha, bhantai, na kauci gihī ahausi audātavasanau’’ti? ‘‘na haittha, āvusau, kauci gihī ahausi audātavasanau’’ti| ‘‘sādhu, bhantai, yadaittha na kauci gihī ahausi audātavasanau’’ti|

atha  khau sau bhikkhu hatthakassa āḷavakassa nivaisanai piṇḍapātaṃ gahaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi| atha khau sau bhikkhu pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina hatthakassa āḷavakassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdiṃ| atha khau, bhantai, hatthakau āḷavakau yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, hatthakaṃ āḷavakaṃ aitadavacaṃ – ‘sattahi khau tvaṃ, āvusau, acchariyaihi abbhutaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhagavatā byākatau| katamaihi sattahi? saddhau, bhikkhavai, hatthakau āḷavakau; sīlavā…pai॰… hirimā… auttappī… bahussutau… cāgavā… pañ‍ñavā, bhikkhavai, hatthakau āḷavakauti| imaihi khau tvaṃ, āvusau, sattahi acchariyaihi abbhutaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhagavatā byākatau’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ vuttai, bhantai, hatthakau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘kac‍cittha, bhantai, na kauci gihī ahausi audātavasanau’ti? ‘na haittha, āvusau, kauci gihī ahausi audātavasanau’ti| ‘sādhu, bhantai, yadaittha na kauci gihī ahausi audātavasanau’’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhu! appicchau sau, bhikkhu, kulaputtau| santaiyaiva attani kusaladhammai na icchati paraihi ñāyamānai pañ‍ñāpayamānai (ka॰)| taina hi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, imināpi aṭṭhamaina acchariyaina abbhutaina dhammaina saman‍nāgataṃ hatthakaṃ āḷavakaṃ dhāraihi, yadidaṃ appicchatāyā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyahatthakasuttaṃ

24. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā āḷaviyaṃ viharati aggāḷavai caitiyai| atha khau hatthakau āḷavakau pañ‍camattaihi upāsakasataihi  parivutau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau hatthakaṃ āḷavakaṃ bhagavā  aitadavauca – ‘‘mahatī khau tyāyaṃ, hatthaka, parisā| kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, hatthaka, imaṃ mahatiṃ parisaṃ saṅgaṇhāsī’’ti? ‘‘yānimāni, bhantai, bhagavatā daisitāni a॰ ni॰ 4.32; dī॰ ni॰ 3.313 cattāri saṅgahavatthūni, taihāhaṃ taināhaṃ (sī॰) imaṃ mahatiṃ  parisaṃ saṅgaṇhāmi| ahaṃ, bhantai, yaṃ jānāmi – ‘ayaṃ dānaina saṅgahaitabbau’ti, taṃ dānaina saṅgaṇhāmi; yaṃ jānāmi – ‘ayaṃ paiyyavaj‍jaina saṅgahaitabbau’ti, taṃ paiyyavaj‍jaina saṅgaṇhāmi; yaṃ jānāmi – ‘ayaṃ atthacariyāya saṅgahaitabbau’ti, taṃ atthacariyāya saṅgaṇhāmi; yaṃ jānāmi – ‘ayaṃ samānattatāya saṅgahaitabbau’ti, taṃ samānattatāya saṅgaṇhāmi| saṃvij‍janti khau pana mai, bhantai, kulai bhaugā| daliddassa khau nau tathā sautabbaṃ mañ‍ñantī’’ti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, hatthaka! yauni khau tyāyaṃ, hatthaka, mahatiṃ parisaṃ saṅgahaituṃ| yai hi kaici, hatthaka, atītamaddhānaṃ mahatiṃ parisaṃ saṅgahaisuṃ, sabbai tai imaihaiva catūhi saṅgahavatthūhi mahatiṃ parisaṃ saṅgahaisuṃ| yaipi hi kaici, hatthaka, anāgatamaddhānaṃ mahatiṃ parisaṃ saṅgaṇhissanti, sabbai tai imaihaiva catūhi saṅgahavatthūhi mahatiṃ parisaṃ saṅgaṇhissanti| yaipi hi kaici, hatthaka, aitarahi mahatiṃ parisaṃ saṅgaṇhanti, sabbai tai imaihaiva catūhi saṅgahavatthūhi mahatiṃ parisaṃ saṅgaṇhantī’’ti|

atha khau hatthakau āḷavakau bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā acirapak‍kantai hatthakai āḷavakai bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, acchariyaihi abbhutaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ hatthakaṃ āḷavakaṃ dhāraitha| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? saddhau, bhikkhavai, hatthakau āḷavakau; sīlavā, bhikkhavai…pai॰… hirīmā… auttappī… bahussutau… cāgavā… pañ‍ñavā, bhikkhavai, hatthakau āḷavakau; appicchau, bhikkhavai, hatthakau āḷavakau| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahi acchariyaihi abbhutaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ hatthakaṃ āḷavakaṃ dhāraithā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. mahānāmasuttaṃ

25. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| atha khau mahānāmau sak‍kau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau mahānāmau sak‍kau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, upāsakau hautī’’ti? ‘‘yatau khau, mahānāma, buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau hauti, dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau hauti, saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau hauti; aittāvatā khau, mahānāma, upāsakau hautī’’ti|

‘‘kittāvatā  pana, bhantai, upāsakau sīlavā hautī’’ti? ‘‘yatau khau, mahānāma, upāsakau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti; aittāvatā khau, mahānāma, upāsakau sīlavā hautī’’ti|

‘‘kittāvatā pana, bhantai, upāsakau attahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau parahitāyā’’ti? ‘‘yatau  khau, mahānāma, upāsakau attanāva saddhāsampan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ saddhāsampadāya samādapaiti samādāpaiti (?); attanāva sīlasampan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ sīlasampadāya samādapaiti; attanāva cāgasampan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ cāgasampadāya samādapaiti; attanāva bhikkhūnaṃ dassanakāmau hauti, nau paraṃ bhikkhūnaṃ dassanai samādapaiti; attanāva saddhammaṃ sautukāmau hauti, nau paraṃ saddhammassavanai samādapaiti; attanāva sutānaṃ dhammānaṃ dhāraṇajātikau hauti, nau paraṃ dhammadhāraṇāya samādapaiti; attanāva sutānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhitā hauti, nau paraṃ atthūpaparikkhāya samādapaiti; attanāva atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ dhammānudhammappaṭipattiyā samādapaiti| aittāvatā khau, mahānāma, upāsakau attahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau parahitāyā’’ti|

‘‘kittāvatā pana, bhantai, upāsakau attahitāya ca paṭipan‍nau hauti parahitāya cā’’ti? ‘‘yatau khau, mahānāma, upāsakau attanā ca saddhāsampan‍nau hauti, parañ‍ca saddhāsampadāya samādapaiti; attanā ca sīlasampan‍nau  hauti, parañ‍ca sīlasampadāya samādapaiti; attanā ca cāgasampan‍nau hauti, parañ‍ca cāgasampadāya samādapaiti; attanā ca bhikkhūnaṃ dassanakāmau hauti, parañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ dassanai samādapaiti; attanā ca saddhammaṃ  sautukāmau hauti, parañ‍ca saddhammassavanai samādapaiti; attanā ca sutānaṃ dhammānaṃ dhāraṇajātikau hauti, parañ‍ca dhammadhāraṇāya samādapaiti; attanā ca sutānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhitā hauti, parañ‍ca atthūpaparikkhāya samādapaiti, attanā ca atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya  dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau hauti, parañ‍ca dhammānudhammappaṭipattiyā samādapaiti| aittāvatā khau, mahānāma, upāsakau attahitāya ca paṭipan‍nau hauti parahitāya cā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. jīvakasuttaṃ

26. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati jīvakambavanai| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, upāsakau hautī’’ti? ‘‘yatau khau, jīvaka, buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau hauti, dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau hauti, saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau hauti; aittāvatā khau jīvaka, upāsakau hautī’’ti|

‘‘kittāvatā pana, bhantai, upāsakau sīlavā hautī’’ti? ‘‘yatau khau, jīvaka, upāsakau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā  paṭiviratau hauti; aittāvatā khau, jīvaka, upāsakau sīlavā hautī’’ti|

‘‘kittāvatā pana, bhantai, upāsakau attahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau parahitāyā’’ti? ‘‘yatau khau, jīvaka, upāsakau attanāva saddhāsampan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ saddhāsampadāya samādapaiti…pai॰… attanāva atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ dhammānudhammappaṭipattiyā samādapaiti| aittāvatā khau, jīvaka, upāsakau attahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau parahitāyā’’ti|

‘‘kittāvatā pana, bhantai, upāsakau attahitāya ca paṭipan‍nau hauti parahitāya cā’’ti? ‘‘yatau  khau, jīvaka, upāsakau attanā ca saddhāsampan‍nau hauti, parañ‍ca saddhāsampadāya samādapaiti; attanā ca sīlasampan‍nau hauti, parañ‍ca sīlasampadāya samādapaiti; attanā ca cāgasampan‍nau  hauti, parañ‍ca cāgasampadāya samādapaiti; attanā ca bhikkhūnaṃ dassanakāmau hauti, parañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ dassanai samādapaiti; attanā ca saddhammaṃ sautukāmau hauti, parañ‍ca saddhammassavanai samādapaiti; attanā ca sutānaṃ dhammānaṃ dhāraṇajātikau hauti, parañ‍ca dhammadhāraṇāya samādapaiti; attanā ca sutānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhitā hauti, parañ‍ca atthūpaparikkhāya samādapaiti; attanā ca atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau hauti, parañ‍ca dhammānudhammappaṭipattiyā samādapaiti| aittāvatā khau, jīvaka, upāsakau attahitāya ca paṭipan‍nau hauti parahitāya  cā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamabalasuttaṃ

27. ‘‘aṭṭhimāni, bhikkhavai, balāni| katamāni aṭṭha? ruṇṇabalā, bhikkhavai, dārakā, kaudhabalā mātugāmā, āvudhabalā caurā, issariyabalā rājānau, ujjhattibalā bālā, nijjhattibalā paṇḍitā, paṭisaṅkhānabalā bahussutā, khantibalā samaṇabrāhmaṇā – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha balānī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyabalasuttaṃ

28. atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ  bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘kati nu khau, sāriputta, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau balāni, yaihi balaihi saman‍nāgatau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti – ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’’’ti? ‘‘aṭṭha, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau balāni, yaihi balaihi saman‍nāgatau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti – ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’’’ti|

‘‘katamāni aṭṭha? a॰ ni॰ 10.90; paṭi॰ ma॰ 2.44 idha, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau anic‍catau sabbai saṅkhārā yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhā haunti| yampi, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau anic‍catau sabbai saṅkhārā yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhā haunti, idampi, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau balaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma khīṇāsavau bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti – ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’’’ti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau aṅgārakāsūpamā kāmā yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhā haunti| yampi, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau aṅgārakāsūpamā  kāmā yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhā haunti, idampi, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau balaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma khīṇāsavau bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti – ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’’’ti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau vivaikanin‍naṃ cittaṃ hauti vivaikapauṇaṃ vivaikapabbhāraṃ vivaikaṭṭhaṃ naikkhammābhirataṃ byantibhūtaṃ sabbasau āsavaṭṭhāniyaihi dhammaihi| yampi, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau vivaikanin‍naṃ cittaṃ hauti vivaikapauṇaṃ vivaikapabbhāraṃ vivaikaṭṭhaṃ naikkhammābhirataṃ byantibhūtaṃ sabbasau āsavaṭṭhāniyaihi dhammaihi, idampi, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau balaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma khīṇāsavau bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti – ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’’’ti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā haunti subhāvitā| yampi, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa  bhikkhunau cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvitā haunti subhāvitā, idampi, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau balaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma khīṇāsavau bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti – ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’’’ti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā haunti subhāvitā…pai॰… pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvitāni haunti subhāvitāni…pai॰… satta baujjhaṅgā bhāvitā haunti subhāvitā…pai॰… ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvitau hauti subhāvitau| yampi, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvitau hauti subhāvitau, idampi, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau balaṃ hauti, yaṃ balaṃ āgamma khīṇāsavau bhikkhu āsavānaṃ  khayaṃ paṭijānāti – ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’’’ti|

‘‘imāni khau, bhantai, aṭṭha khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau balāni, yaihi balaihi saman‍nāgatau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayaṃ paṭijānāti – ‘khīṇā mai āsavā’’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. akkhaṇasuttaṃ

29. ‘‘‘khaṇakic‍cau  laukau, khaṇakic‍cau laukau’ti, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau bhāsati, nau ca khau sau jānāti khaṇaṃ vā akkhaṇaṃ vā| aṭṭhimai, bhikkhavai, akkhaṇā asamayā brahmacariyavāsāya| katamai aṭṭha? idha, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau ca laukai uppan‍nau hauti arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ  buddhau bhagavā, dhammau ca daisiyati aupasamikau parinibbānikau sambaudhagāmī sugatappavaiditau; ayañ‍ca puggalau nirayaṃ upapan‍nau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau akkhaṇau asamayau brahmacariyavāsāya|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau ca laukai uppan‍nau hauti…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā, dhammau ca daisiyati aupasamikau parinibbānikau sambaudhagāmī sugatappavaiditau; ayañ‍ca puggalau tiracchānayauniṃ upapan‍nau hauti…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai…pai॰… ayañ‍ca puggalau paittivisayaṃ upapan‍nau hauti…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai…pai॰… ayañ‍ca puggalau añ‍ñataraṃ dīghāyukaṃ daivanikāyaṃ upapan‍nau hauti…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai…pai॰… ayañ‍ca puggalau pac‍cantimaisu janapadaisu pac‍cājātau hauti, sau ca hauti aviñ‍ñātāraisu milakkhaisu milakkhūsu (syā॰ ka॰) dī॰ ni॰ 3.358, yattha natthi gati bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ upāsakānaṃ upāsikānaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍camau akkhaṇau asamayau brahmacariyavāsāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai…pai॰… ayañ‍ca puggalau majjhimaisu janapadaisu pac‍cājātau hauti, sau ca hauti micchādiṭṭhikau  viparītadassanau – ‘natthi din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ, natthi hutaṃ, natthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau, natthi ayaṃ laukau, natthi parau laukau, natthi mātā, natthi pitā, natthi sattā aupapātikā, natthi laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammā paṭipan‍nā yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’ti…pai॰…|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai…pai॰… ayañ‍ca puggalau majjhimaisu janapadaisu pac‍cājātau hauti, sau ca hauti duppañ‍ñau jaḷau aiḷamūgau appaṭibalau subhāsitadubbhāsitassa atthamañ‍ñātuṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, sattamau akkhaṇau asamayau brahmacariyavāsāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau ca laukai anuppan‍nau hauti arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā| dhammau ca na daisiyati  aupasamikau parinibbānikau sambaudhagāmī sugatappavaiditau| ayañ‍ca puggalau majjhimaisu janapadaisu pac‍cājātau hauti, sau ca hauti pañ‍ñavā ajaḷau anaiḷamūgau paṭibalau subhāsitadubbhāsitassa atthamañ‍ñātuṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhamau akkhaṇau asamayau brahmacariyavāsāya| ‘imai khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha akkhaṇā asamayā brahmacariyavāsāya’’’|

‘‘aikauva khau, bhikkhavai, khaṇau ca samayau ca brahmacariyavāsāya| katamau aikau? idha, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau ca laukai uppan‍nau hauti arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā| dhammau ca daisiyati aupasamikau parinibbānikau sambaudhagāmī sugatappavaiditau| ayañ‍ca puggalau majjhimaisu  janapadaisu pac‍cājātau hauti, sau ca hauti pañ‍ñavā ajaḷau anaiḷamūgau paṭibalau subhāsitadubbhāsitassa atthamañ‍ñātuṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, aikauva khaṇau ca samayau ca brahmacariyavāsāyā’’ti|

‘‘manussalābhaṃ manussalaukaṃ (syā॰) laddhāna, saddhammai suppavaiditai|

yai khaṇaṃ nādhigacchanti, atināmainti tai khaṇaṃ||

‘‘bahū hi akkhaṇā vuttā, maggassa antarāyikā|

kadāci karahaci laukai, uppaj‍janti tathāgatā||

‘‘tayidaṃ tassidaṃ (ka॰) sammukhībhūtaṃ, yaṃ laukasmiṃ sudul‍labhaṃ|

manussapaṭilābhau ca, saddhammassa ca daisanā|

alaṃ vāyamituṃ tattha, attakāmaina atthakāmaina (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) jantunā||

‘‘kathaṃ  vijañ‍ñā saddhammaṃ, khaṇau vai vau (syā॰) mā upac‍cagā|

khaṇātītā hi saucanti, nirayamhi samappitā||

‘‘idha cai naṃ virādhaiti, saddhammassa niyāmataṃ niyāmitaṃ (syā॰)|

vāṇijauva atītatthau, cirattaṃ cirantaṃ (ka॰) anutapissati||

‘‘avij‍jānivutau pausau, saddhammaṃ aparādhikau|

jātimaraṇasaṃsāraṃ, ciraṃ pac‍canubhaussati||

‘‘yai ca laddhā manussattaṃ, saddhammai suppavaiditai|

akaṃsu satthu vacanaṃ, karissanti karaunti vā||

‘‘khaṇaṃ pac‍caviduṃ laukai, brahmacariyaṃ anuttaraṃ|

yai maggaṃ paṭipaj‍jiṃsu, tathāgatappavaiditaṃ||

‘‘yai  saṃvarā cakkhumatā, daisitādic‍cabandhunā|

taisu taisaṃ (ka॰) guttau sadā satau, viharai anavassutau||

‘‘sabbai anusayai chaitvā, māradhaiyyaparānugai|

tai vai pāraṅgatā pāragatā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) laukai, yai pattā āsavakkhaya’’nti|| navamaṃ|

10. anuruddhamahāvitak‍kasuttaṃ

30. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā bhaggaisu viharati suṃsumāragirai bhaisakaḷāvanai migadāyai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā anuruddhau caitīsu viharati pācīnavaṃsadāyai| atha khau āyasmatau anuruddhassa rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘appicchassāyaṃ dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau mahicchassa; santuṭṭhassāyaṃ dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau asantuṭṭhassa; pavivittassāyaṃ dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau saṅgaṇikārāmassa; āraddhavīriyassāyaṃ dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau kusītassa; upaṭṭhitassatissāyaṃ  upaṭṭhitasatissāyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau muṭṭhassatissa muṭṭhasatissa (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰); samāhitassāyaṃ dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau asamāhitassa; pañ‍ñavatau ayaṃ dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau duppañ‍ñassā’’ti|

atha  khau bhagavā āyasmatau anuruddhassa caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya; aivamaivaṃ – bhaggaisu suṃsumāragirai bhaisakaḷāvanai migadāyai antarahitau caitīsu pācīnavaṃsadāyai āyasmatau anuruddhassa sammukhai pāturahausi| nisīdi bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai| āyasmāpi khau anuruddhau bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ

 nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘sādhu sādhu, anuruddha! sādhu khau tvaṃ, anuruddha, (yaṃ taṃ mahāpurisavitak‍kaṃ) satta mahāpurisavitak‍kai (sī॰ pī॰) dī॰ ni॰ 3.358 vitak‍kaisi – ‘appicchassāyaṃ dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau mahicchassa; santuṭṭhassāyaṃ dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau asantuṭṭhassa; pavivittassāyaṃ dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau saṅgaṇikārāmassa; āraddhavīriyassāyaṃ dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau kusītassa; upaṭṭhitassatissāyaṃ dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau muṭṭhassatissa; samāhitassāyaṃ dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau asamāhitassa; pañ‍ñavatau ayaṃ dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau duppañ‍ñassā’ti| taina hi tvaṃ, anuruddha, imampi aṭṭhamaṃ mahāpurisavitak‍kaṃ vitak‍kaihi – ‘nippapañ‍cārāmassāyaṃ dhammau nippapañ‍caratinau, nāyaṃ dhammau papañ‍cārāmassa papañ‍caratinau’’’ti|

‘‘yatau khau tvaṃ, anuruddha, imai aṭṭha mahāpurisavitak‍kai vitak‍kaissasi, tatau tvaṃ, anuruddha, yāvadaiva yāvadai (saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.152) ākaṅkhissasi, vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi  savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharissasi|

‘‘yatau khau tvaṃ, anuruddha, imai aṭṭha mahāpurisavitak‍kai vitak‍kaissasi, tatau tvaṃ, anuruddha, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhissasi, vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharissasi|

‘‘yatau khau tvaṃ, anuruddha, imai aṭṭha mahāpurisavitak‍kai vitak‍kaissasi, tatau tvaṃ, anuruddha, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhissasi, pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharissasi satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidissasi  yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau  satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharissasi|

‘‘yatau khau tvaṃ, anuruddha, imai aṭṭha mahāpurisavitak‍kai vitak‍kaissasi, tatau tvaṃ, anuruddha, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhissasi, sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharissasi|

‘‘yatau khau tvaṃ, anuruddha, imai ca aṭṭha mahāpurisavitak‍kai vitak‍kaissasi, imaisañ‍ca catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī bhavissasi akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, tatau tuyhaṃ, anuruddha, saiyyathāpi nāma gahapatissa vā gahapatiputtassa vā nānārattānaṃ dussānaṃ dussakaraṇḍakau pūrau; aivamaivaṃ tai paṃsukūlacīvaraṃ khāyissati santuṭṭhassa viharatau ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya auk‍kamanāya nibbānassa|

‘‘yatau  khau tvaṃ, anuruddha, imai ca aṭṭha mahāpurisavitak‍kai vitak‍kaissasi, imaisañ‍ca catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī bhavissasi akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, tatau tuyhaṃ, anuruddha, saiyyathāpi nāma gahapatissa vā gahapatiputtassa vā sālīnaṃ audanau vicitakāḷakau anaikasūpau anaikabyañ‍janau; aivamaivaṃ tai piṇḍiyālaupabhaujanaṃ khāyissati santuṭṭhassa viharatau ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya auk‍kamanāya nibbānassa|

‘‘yatau khau tvaṃ, anuruddha, imai ca aṭṭha mahāpurisavitak‍kai vitak‍kaissasi, imaisañ‍ca catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī bhavissasi akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, tatau tuyhaṃ, anuruddha, saiyyathāpi  nāma gahapatissa vā gahapatiputtassa vā kūṭāgāraṃ ul‍littāvalittaṃ nivātaṃ phusitaggaḷaṃ pihitavātapānaṃ; aivamaivaṃ tai rukkhamūlasaināsanaṃ khāyissati santuṭṭhassa viharatau ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya auk‍kamanāya nibbānassa|

‘‘yatau  khau tvaṃ, anuruddha, imai ca aṭṭha mahāpurisavitak‍kai vitak‍kaissasi, imaisañ‍ca catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī bhavissasi akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, tatau tuyhaṃ, anuruddha, saiyyathāpi nāma gahapatissa vā gahapatiputtassa vā pal‍laṅkau gaunakatthatau paṭikatthatau paṭalikatthatau kadalimigapavarapac‍cattharaṇau kādali… pac‍cattharaṇau (sī॰) sauttaracchadau ubhataulauhitakūpadhānau; aivamaivaṃ tai tiṇasanthārakasayanāsanaṃ khāyissati santuṭṭhassa viharatau ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya auk‍kamanāya nibbānassa|

‘‘yatau  khau tvaṃ, anuruddha, imai ca aṭṭha mahāpurisavitak‍kai vitak‍kaissasi, imaisañ‍ca catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī bhavissasi akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, tatau tuyhaṃ, anuruddha, saiyyathāpi nāma gahapatissa vā gahapatiputtassa vā nānābhaisaj‍jāni, saiyyathidaṃ – sappi navanītaṃ tailaṃ madhu phāṇitaṃ; aivamaivaṃ tai pūtimuttabhaisaj‍jaṃ khāyissati santuṭṭhassa viharatau ratiyā aparitassāya phāsuvihārāya auk‍kamanāya nibbānassa| taina hi tvaṃ, anuruddha, āyatikampi vassāvāsaṃ idhaiva caitīsu pācīnavaṃsadāyai viharaiyyāsī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā anuruddhau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi|

atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ iminā auvādaina auvaditvā – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā  purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaivaṃ – caitīsu pācīnavaṃsadāyai antarahitau bhaggaisu suṃsumāragirai bhaisakaḷāvanai migadāyai pāturahausīti| nisīdi bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘aṭṭha khau, bhikkhavai, mahāpurisavitak‍kai daisaissāmi, taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamai ca, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha mahāpurisavitak‍kā? appicchassāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau mahicchassa; santuṭṭhassāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau asantuṭṭhassa; pavivittassāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau saṅgaṇikārāmassa; āraddhavīriyassāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau kusītassa; upaṭṭhitassatissāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau muṭṭhassatissa; samāhitassāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau asamāhitassa; pañ‍ñavatau ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammau, nāyaṃ  dhammau duppañ‍ñassa; nippapañ‍cārāmassāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammau nippapañ‍caratinau, nāyaṃ dhammau papañ‍cārāmassa papañ‍caratinau’’|

‘‘‘appicchassāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau mahicchassā’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu appicchau samānau ‘appicchauti maṃ jānaiyyu’nti na icchati, santuṭṭhau samānau ‘santuṭṭhauti maṃ jānaiyyu’nti na icchati, pavivittau samānau ‘pavivittauti maṃ jānaiyyu’nti na icchati, āraddhavīriyau samānau ‘āraddhavīriyauti maṃ jānaiyyu’nti na icchati, upaṭṭhitassati samānau ‘upaṭṭhitassatīti maṃ jānaiyyu’nti na icchati, samāhitau samānau ‘samāhitauti  maṃ jānaiyyu’nti na icchati, pañ‍ñavā samānau ‘pañ‍ñavāti maṃ jānaiyyu’nti na icchati, nippapañ‍cārāmau samānau ‘nippapañ‍cārāmauti maṃ jānaiyyu’nti na icchati| ‘appicchassāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau mahicchassā’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘santuṭṭhassāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau asantuṭṭhassā’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina| ‘santuṭṭhassāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau asantuṭṭhassā’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘pavivittassāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau saṅgaṇikārāmassā’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pavivittassa viharatau bhavanti upasaṅkamitārau bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau upāsakā upāsikāyau rājānau rājamahāmattā titthiyā titthiyasāvakā| tatra bhikkhu vivaikanin‍naina cittaina vivaikapauṇaina vivaikapabbhāraina vivaikaṭṭhaina naikkhammābhirataina añ‍ñadatthu uyyaujanikapaṭisaṃyuttaṃyaiva kathaṃ kattā pavattā (ka॰) hauti| ‘pavivittassāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau saṅgaṇikārāmassā’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘āraddhavīriyassāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau kusītassā’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya  thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| ‘āraddhavīriyassāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau kusītassā’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘upaṭṭhitassatissāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau muṭṭhassatissā’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satimā hauti paramaina satinaipak‍kaina saman‍nāgatau, cirakatampi cirabhāsitampi saritā anussaritā| ‘upaṭṭhitassatissāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau, muṭṭhassatissā’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘samāhitassāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau asamāhitassā’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ‘samāhitassāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau asamāhitassā’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘pañ‍ñavatau ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau duppañ‍ñassā’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍ñavā hauti udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| ‘pañ‍ñavatau ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammau, nāyaṃ dhammau duppañ‍ñassā’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘nippapañ‍cārāmassāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammau nippapañ‍caratinau, nāyaṃ dhammau papañ‍cārāmassa papañ‍caratinau’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ  paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau papañ‍caniraudhai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati| ‘nippapañ‍cārāmassāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammau, nippapañ‍caratinau, nāyaṃ dhammau papañ‍cārāmassa papañ‍caratinau’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vutta’’nti|

atha khau āyasmā anuruddhau āyatikampi vassāvāsaṃ tatthaiva caitīsu pācīnavaṃsadāyai vihāsi| atha khau āyasmā anuruddhau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā anuruddhau arahataṃ  ahausīti| atha khau āyasmā anuruddhau arahattappattau tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

thairagā॰ 901-903 ‘‘mama  saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, satthā laukai anuttarau|

manaumayaina kāyaina, iddhiyā upasaṅkami||

‘‘yathā mai ahu saṅkappau, tatau uttari daisayi|

nippapañ‍caratau buddhau, nippapañ‍caṃ adaisayi||

‘‘tassāhaṃ dhammamañ‍ñāya, vihāsiṃ sāsanai ratau|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti|| dasamaṃ|

gahapativaggau tatiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

dvai  uggā dvai ca hatthakā, mahānāmaina jīvakau|

dvai balā akkhaṇā vuttā, anuruddhaina tai dasāti||


4. dānavaggau

1. paṭhamadānasuttaṃ

31. dī॰ ni॰ 3.336 ‘‘aṭṭhimāni, bhikkhavai, dānāni| katamāni aṭṭha? āsaj‍ja dānaṃ daiti, bhayā dānaṃ daiti, ‘adāsi mai’ti dānaṃ daiti, ‘dassati mai’ti dānaṃ daiti, ‘sāhu dāna’nti dānaṃ daiti, ‘ahaṃ pacāmi, imai na pacanti; nārahāmi pacantau apacantānaṃ dānaṃ adātu’nti dānaṃ daiti, ‘imaṃ mai dānaṃ dadatau kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggacchatī’ti dānaṃ daiti, cittālaṅkāracittaparikkhāratthaṃ dānaṃ daiti| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha dānānī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyadānasuttaṃ

32.

 kathā॰ 480 ‘‘saddhā hiriyaṃ kusalañ‍ca dānaṃ,

dhammā aitai sappurisānuyātā|

aitañhi maggaṃ diviyaṃ vadanti,

aitaina hi gacchati daivalauka’’nti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. dānavatthusuttaṃ

33. ‘‘aṭṭhimāni, bhikkhavai, dānavatthūni| katamāni aṭṭha? chandā dānaṃ daiti, dausā dānaṃ daiti, mauhā dānaṃ daiti, bhayā dānaṃ daiti, ‘din‍napubbaṃ katapubbaṃ pitupitāmahaihi, nārahāmi paurāṇaṃ kulavaṃsaṃ hāpaitu’nti dānaṃ daiti, ‘imāhaṃ dānaṃ datvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ  saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jissāmī’ti dānaṃ daiti, ‘imaṃ mai dānaṃ dadatau cittaṃ pasīdati, attamanatā  saumanassaṃ upajāyatī’ti dānaṃ daiti, cittālaṅkāracittaparikkhāratthaṃ dānaṃ daiti| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha dānavatthūnī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. khaittasuttaṃ

34. ‘‘aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatai, bhikkhavai, khaittai bījaṃ vuttaṃ na mahapphalaṃ hauti na mahassādaṃ na phātisaiyyaṃ na phātisaiyyanti (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰), na phātisaiyyā (katthaci)| kathaṃ aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatai? idha, bhikkhavai, khaittaṃ un‍nāmanin‍nāmi ca hauti, pāsāṇasakkharikañ‍ca hauti, ūsarañ‍ca hauti, na ca gambhīrasitaṃ hauti, na āyasampan‍naṃ hauti, na apāyasampan‍naṃ hauti, na mātikāsampan‍naṃ hauti, na mariyādasampan‍naṃ hauti| aivaṃ aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatai, bhikkhavai, khaittai bījaṃ vuttaṃ na mahapphalaṃ hauti na mahassādaṃ na phātisaiyyaṃ|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataisu samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu dānaṃ din‍naṃ na mahapphalaṃ hauti na mahānisaṃsaṃ na mahājutikaṃ na mahāvipphāraṃ| kathaṃ aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataisu? idha, bhikkhavai, samaṇabrāhmaṇā micchādiṭṭhikā haunti, micchāsaṅkappā, micchāvācā, micchākammantā, micchāājīvā, micchāvāyāmā, micchāsatinau, micchāsamādhinau| aivaṃ aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataisu, bhikkhavai, samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu dānaṃ din‍naṃ na mahapphalaṃ hauti na mahānisaṃsaṃ na mahājutikaṃ na mahāvipphāraṃ|

‘‘aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatai, bhikkhavai, khaittai bījaṃ vuttaṃ mahapphalaṃ hauti mahassādaṃ  phātisaiyyaṃ| kathaṃ aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatai? idha, bhikkhavai, khaittaṃ anun‍nāmānin‍nāmi ca hauti, apāsāṇasakkharikañ‍ca hauti, anūsarañ‍ca hauti, gambhīrasitaṃ  hauti, āyasampan‍naṃ hauti, apāyasampan‍naṃ hauti, mātikāsampan‍naṃ  hauti, mariyādasampan‍naṃ hauti| aivaṃ aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatai, bhikkhavai, khaittai bījaṃ vuttaṃ mahapphalaṃ hauti mahassādaṃ phātisaiyyaṃ|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataisu samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu dānaṃ din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ hauti mahānisaṃsaṃ mahājutikaṃ mahāvipphāraṃ| kathaṃ aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataisu? idha, bhikkhavai, samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammādiṭṭhikā haunti, sammāsaṅkappā, sammāvācā, sammākammantā, sammāājīvā, sammāvāyāmā, sammāsatinau, sammāsamādhinau| aivaṃ aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataisu, bhikkhavai, samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu dānaṃ din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ hauti mahānisaṃsaṃ mahājutikaṃ mahāvipphāra’’nti|

‘‘yathāpi  khaittai sampan‍nai, pavuttā bījasampadā|

daivai sampādayantamhi sañ‍jāyantamhi (ka॰), hauti dhañ‍ñassa sampadā||

‘‘anītisampadā hauti, virūḷhī bhavati sampadā|

vaipul‍lasampadā hauti, phalaṃ vai hauti sampadā||

‘‘aivaṃ sampan‍nasīlaisu, din‍nā bhaujanasampadā|

sampadānaṃ upanaiti, sampan‍naṃ hissa taṃ kataṃ||

‘‘tasmā sampadamākaṅkhī, sampan‍natthūdha puggalau|

sampan‍napañ‍ñai  saivaitha, aivaṃ ijjhanti sampadā||

‘‘vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nai, laddhā cittassa sampadaṃ|

karauti kammasampadaṃ, labhati catthasampadaṃ||

‘‘laukaṃ ñatvā yathābhūtaṃ, pappuyya diṭṭhisampadaṃ|

maggasampadamāgamma, yāti sampan‍namānasau||

‘‘audhunitvā  malaṃ sabbaṃ, patvā nibbānasampadaṃ|

muc‍cati sabbadukkhaihi, sā hauti sabbasampadā’’ti|| catutthaṃ|

5. dānūpapattisuttaṃ

35. dī॰ ni॰ 3.337 ‘‘aṭṭhimā, bhikkhavai, dānūpapattiyau| katamā aṭṭha? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau dānaṃ daiti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā an‍naṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ  mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyaṃ| sau yaṃ daiti taṃ pac‍cāsīsati pac‍cāsiṃsati (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| sau passati khattiyamahāsālai vā brāhmaṇamahāsālai vā gahapatimahāsālai vā pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitai samaṅgībhūtai paricārayamānai| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā khattiyamahāsālānaṃ  vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti! sau taṃ cittaṃ dahati, taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, taṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| tassa taṃ cittaṃ hīnai vimuttaṃ hīnaidhimuttaṃ (syā॰ pī॰) vimuttanti adhimuttaṃ, vimuttanti vā vissaṭṭhaṃ (ṭīkāsaṃvaṇṇanā), uttari abhāvitaṃ, tatrūpapattiyā saṃvattati| kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā khattiyamahāsālānaṃ vā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ vā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ vā sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| tañ‍ca khau sīlavatau vadāmi, nau dussīlassa| ijjhati, bhikkhavai, sīlavatau caitaupaṇidhi visuddhattā|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau dānaṃ daiti  samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā an‍naṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyaṃ| sau yaṃ daiti taṃ pac‍cāsīsati| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘cātumahārājikā cātummahārājikā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) daivā  dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulā’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau taṃ cittaṃ dahati, taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, taṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| tassa taṃ cittaṃ hīnai vimuttaṃ, uttari abhāvitaṃ, tatrūpapattiyā saṃvattati| kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| tañ‍ca khau sīlavatau vadāmi, nau dussīlassa| ijjhati, bhikkhavai, sīlavatau caitaupaṇidhi visuddhattā|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau dānaṃ daiti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā an‍naṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyaṃ| sau yaṃ daiti taṃ pac‍cāsīsati| tassa sutaṃ hauti – tāvatiṃsā daivā…pai॰… yāmā daivā… tusitā daivā… nimmānaratī daivā… paranimmitavasavattī daivā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulāti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau taṃ cittaṃ dahati, taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, taṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| tassa taṃ cittaṃ hīnai vimuttaṃ, uttari abhāvitaṃ, tatrūpapattiyā saṃvattati| kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| tañ‍ca khau sīlavatau vadāmi, nau dussīlassa| ijjhati, bhikkhavai, sīlavatau caitaupaṇidhi visuddhattā|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau  dānaṃ daiti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā an‍naṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyaṃ| sau yaṃ daiti taṃ pac‍cāsīsati| tassa sutaṃ hauti  – ‘brahmakāyikā daivā dīghāyukā  vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulā’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā brahmakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau taṃ cittaṃ dahati, taṃ cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, taṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| tassa taṃ cittaṃ hīnai vimuttaṃ, uttari abhāvitaṃ, tatrūpapattiyā saṃvattati| kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā brahmakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| tañ‍ca khau sīlavatau vadāmi, nau dussīlassa; vītarāgassa, nau sarāgassa| ijjhati, bhikkhavai, sīlavatau caitaupaṇidhi vītarāgattā| imā khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha dānūpapattiyau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. puñ‍ñakiriyavatthusuttaṃ

36. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, puñ‍ñakiriyavatthūni| katamāni tīṇi? dānamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu puñ‍ñakiriyavatthuṃ (sī॰ pī॰) aivamuparipi, sīlamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu, bhāvanāmayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu| idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa dānamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu parittaṃ kataṃ hauti, sīlamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu parittaṃ kataṃ hauti, bhāvanāmayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthuṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu (syā॰) nābhisambhauti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā manussadaubhagyaṃ upapaj‍jati|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa dānamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu mattasau kataṃ hauti, sīlamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu mattasau kataṃ hauti, bhāvanāmayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthuṃ nābhisambhauti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā manussasaubhagyaṃ upapaj‍jati|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa dānamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu adhimattaṃ kataṃ hauti, sīlamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu  adhimattaṃ kataṃ hauti, bhāvanāmayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthuṃ nābhisambhauti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| tatra, bhikkhavai, cattārau mahārājānau dānamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthuṃ atiraikaṃ karitvā, sīlamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthuṃ atiraikaṃ karitvā, cātumahārājikai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhanti – dibbaina āyunā, dibbaina vaṇṇaina, dibbaina sukhaina, dibbaina yasaina, dibbaina ādhipataiyyaina, dibbaihi rūpaihi, dibbaihi saddaihi, dibbaihi gandhaihi, dibbaihi rasaihi, dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa dānamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu adhimattaṃ kataṃ hauti, sīlamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu adhimattaṃ kataṃ hauti, bhāvanāmayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthuṃ nābhisambhauti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā tāvatiṃsānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| tatra, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau dānamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthuṃ atiraikaṃ karitvā sīlamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthuṃ atiraikaṃ karitvā tāvatiṃsai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhāti – dibbaina āyunā…pai॰… dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa dānamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu adhimattaṃ kataṃ hauti, sīlamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu adhimattaṃ kataṃ hauti, bhāvanāmayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthuṃ nābhisambhauti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā yāmānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| tatra, bhikkhavai, suyāmau daivaputtau dānamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthuṃ atiraikaṃ karitvā, sīlamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthuṃ atiraikaṃ karitvā, yāmai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhāti – dibbaina āyunā…pai॰… dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa dānamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu adhimattaṃ kataṃ hauti, sīlamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu adhimattaṃ kataṃ hauti, bhāvanāmayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthuṃ nābhisambhauti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā tusitānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| tatra, bhikkhavai, santusitau daivaputtau dānamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthuṃ atiraikaṃ karitvā, sīlamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthuṃ atiraikaṃ karitvā, tusitai daivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhāti – dibbaina āyunā…pai॰… dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa dānamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu adhimattaṃ kataṃ hauti, sīlamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu adhimattaṃ kataṃ hauti, bhāvanāmayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthuṃ nābhisambhauti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā nimmānaratīnaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| tatra, bhikkhavai, sunimmitau daivaputtau dānamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthuṃ atiraikaṃ karitvā, sīlamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthuṃ atiraikaṃ karitvā, nimmānaratīdaivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhāti – dibbaina āyunā…pai॰… dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa dānamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu adhimattaṃ kataṃ hauti, sīlamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu adhimattaṃ kataṃ hauti, bhāvanāmayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthuṃ nābhisambhauti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| tatra, bhikkhavai, vasavattī daivaputtau dānamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthuṃ atiraikaṃ  karitvā, sīlamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthuṃ atiraikaṃ karitvā, paranimmitavasavattīdaivai dasahi ṭhānaihi adhigaṇhāti – dibbaina āyunā, dibbaina vaṇṇaina, dibbaina sukhaina, dibbaina yasaina, dibbaina ādhipataiyyaina, dibbaihi rūpaihi, dibbaihi saddaihi, dibbaihi gandhaihi, dibbaihi rasaihi, dibbaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi puñ‍ñakiriyavatthūnī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sappurisadānasuttaṃ

37. ‘‘aṭṭhimāni, bhikkhavai, sappurisadānāni| katamāni aṭṭha? suciṃ  daiti, paṇītaṃ daiti, kālaina daiti, kappiyaṃ daiti, vicaiyya daiti, abhiṇhaṃ daiti, dadaṃ cittaṃ pasādaiti, datvā attamanau hauti| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha sappurisadānānī’’ti|

‘‘suciṃ paṇītaṃ kālaina, kappiyaṃ pānabhaujanaṃ|

abhiṇhaṃ dadāti dānaṃ, sukhaittaisu sukhaittai (sī॰ pī॰) brahmacārisu||

‘‘naiva na ca (sī॰ pī॰) vippaṭisārissa, cajitvā āmisaṃ bahuṃ|

aivaṃ din‍nāni dānāni, vaṇṇayanti vipassinau||

‘‘aivaṃ yajitvā maidhāvī, saddhau muttaina caitasā|

abyābajjhaṃ abyāpajjhaṃ (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 4.40; 6.37 sukhaṃ laukaṃ, paṇḍitau upapaj‍jatī’’ti|| sattamaṃ|

8. sappurisasuttaṃ

38. ‘‘sappurisau, bhikkhavai, kulai jāyamānau bahunau janassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti – mātāpitūnaṃ atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti, puttadārassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti, dāsakammakarapaurisassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti, mittāmac‍cānaṃ atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti, pubbapaitānaṃ atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti, rañ‍ñau  atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti, daivatānaṃ atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti, samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahāmaighau sabbasassāni sampādaintau bahunau janassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya hitāya…pai॰… (syā॰ ka॰) hauti; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sappurisau kulai jāyamānau bahunau janassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti – mātāpitūnaṃ atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti, puttadārassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti, dāsakammakarapaurisassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti, mittāmac‍cānaṃ atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti, pubbapaitānaṃ atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti, rañ‍ñau atthāya hitāya sukhāya  hauti, daivatānaṃ atthāya hitāya sukhāya hauti, samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ atthāya hitāya sukhāya hautī’’ti|

‘‘bahūnaṃ bahun‍naṃ (sī॰ pī॰) vata atthāya, sappañ‍ñau gharamāvasaṃ|

mātaraṃ pitaraṃ pubbai, rattindivamatanditau||

‘‘pūjaiti sahadhammaina, pubbaikatamanussaraṃ|

anāgārai pabbajitai, apacai brahmacārayau brahmacārinau (syā॰)||

‘‘niviṭṭhasaddhau pūjaiti, ñatvā dhammai ca paisalau paisalai (ka॰)|

rañ‍ñau hitau daivahitau, ñātīnaṃ sakhinaṃ hitau||

‘‘sabbaisaṃ sabbaisu (ka॰) sau sa (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) hitau hauti, saddhammai suppatiṭṭhitau|

vinaiyya macchairamalaṃ, sa laukaṃ bhajatai siva’’nti|| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. abhisandasuttaṃ

39. ‘‘aṭṭhimai, bhikkhavai, puñ‍ñābhisandā kusalābhisandā sukhassāhārā  sauvaggikā sukhavipākā  saggasaṃvattanikā, iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattanti| katamai aṭṭha? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau sauvaggikau sukhavipākau saggasaṃvattanikau, iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau puñ‍ñābhisandau…pai॰… saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau sauvaggikau sukhavipākau saggasaṃvattanikau, iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattati|

kathā॰ 480 ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, dānāni mahādānāni aggañ‍ñāni rattañ‍ñāni vaṃsañ‍ñāni paurāṇāni asaṃkiṇṇāni asaṃkiṇṇapubbāni, na saṃkiyanti na saṃkiyissanti, appaṭikuṭṭhāni appatikuṭṭhāni (sī॰) samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhi| katamāni pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti| pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau aparimāṇānaṃ sattānaṃ abhayaṃ daiti, avairaṃ daiti, abyābajjhaṃ abyāpajjhaṃ (ka॰) aivamuparipi daiti| aparimāṇānaṃ sattānaṃ abhayaṃ datvā avairaṃ datvā abyābajjhaṃ datvā aparimāṇassa abhayassa avairassa abyābajjhassa bhāgī hauti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ dānaṃ mahādānaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ rattañ‍ñaṃ vaṃsañ‍ñaṃ paurāṇaṃ asaṃkiṇṇaṃ asaṃkiṇṇapubbaṃ, na saṃkiyati na saṃkiyissati, appaṭikuṭṭhaṃ samaṇaihi  brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhi| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau sauvaggikau sukhavipākau saggasaṃvattanikau, iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau adin‍nādānaṃ pahāya adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… kāmaisumicchācāraṃ pahāya kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau  hauti…pai॰… musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānaṃ pahāya surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti| surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau  aparimāṇānaṃ sattānaṃ abhayaṃ daiti avairaṃ daiti abyābajjhaṃ daiti| aparimāṇānaṃ sattānaṃ abhayaṃ datvā avairaṃ datvā abyābajjhaṃ datvā, aparimāṇassa abhayassa avairassa abyābajjhassa bhāgī hauti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camaṃ dānaṃ mahādānaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ rattañ‍ñaṃ vaṃsañ‍ñaṃ paurāṇaṃ asaṃkiṇṇaṃ asaṃkiṇṇapubbaṃ, na saṃkiyati na saṃkiyissati, appaṭikuṭṭhaṃ samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhi| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhamau puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau  sukhassāhārau sauvaggikau sukhavipākau saggasaṃvattanikau, iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha puñ‍ñābhisandā kusalābhisandā sukhassāhārā sauvaggikā sukhavipākā saggasaṃvattanikā, iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattantī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. duc‍caritavipākasuttaṃ

40. ‘‘pāṇātipātau, bhikkhavai, āsaivitau bhāvitau bahulīkatau nirayasaṃvattanikau  tiracchānayaunisaṃvattanikau paittivisayasaṃvattanikau| yau sabbalahusau sabbalahusauti sabbalahukau (syā॰ aṭṭha॰) pāṇātipātassa vipākau, manussabhūtassa appāyukasaṃvattanikau hauti|

‘‘adin‍nādānaṃ, bhikkhavai, āsaivitaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ nirayasaṃvattanikaṃ tiracchānayaunisaṃvattanikaṃ paittivisayasaṃvattanikaṃ| yau sabbalahusau adin‍nādānassa vipākau, manussabhūtassa bhaugabyasanasaṃvattanikau hauti|

‘‘kāmaisumicchācārau, bhikkhavai, āsaivitau bhāvitau bahulīkatau nirayasaṃvattanikau tiracchānayaunisaṃvattanikau paittivisayasaṃvattanikau| yau sabbalahusau kāmaisumicchācārassa vipākau, manussabhūtassa sapattavairasaṃvattanikau hauti|

‘‘musāvādau, bhikkhavai, āsaivitau bhāvitau bahulīkatau nirayasaṃvattanikau tiracchānayaunisaṃvattanikau paittivisayasaṃvattanikau| yau sabbalahusau musāvādassa vipākau, manussabhūtassa abhūtabbhakkhānasaṃvattanikau hauti|

‘‘pisuṇā, bhikkhavai, vācā āsaivitā bhāvitā bahulīkatā nirayasaṃvattanikā tiracchānayaunisaṃvattanikā paittivisayasaṃvattanikā| yau sabbalahusau pisuṇāya vācāya vipākau, manussabhūtassa mittaihi bhaidanasaṃvattanikau hauti|

‘‘pharusā, bhikkhavai, vācā āsaivitā bhāvitā bahulīkatā nirayasaṃvattanikā tiracchānayaunisaṃvattanikā paittivisayasaṃvattanikā| yau sabbalahusau pharusāya vācāya vipākau, manussabhūtassa amanāpasaddasaṃvattanikau hauti|

‘‘samphappalāpau, bhikkhavai, āsaivitau bhāvitau bahulīkatau nirayasaṃvattanikau tiracchānayaunisaṃvattanikau paittivisayasaṃvattanikau| yau sabbalahusau samphappalāpassa vipākau, manussabhūtassa anādaiyyavācāsaṃvattanikau hauti|

‘‘surāmairayapānaṃ, bhikkhavai, āsaivitaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ nirayasaṃvattanikaṃ tiracchānayaunisaṃvattanikaṃ paittivisayasaṃvattanikaṃ| yau sabbalahusau surāmairayapānassa vipākau, manussabhūtassa ummattakasaṃvattanikau hautī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

dānavaggau catutthau|


tassuddānaṃ –

dvai dānāni vatthuñ‍ca, khaittaṃ dānūpapattiyau|

kiriyaṃ dvai sappurisā, abhisandau vipākau cāti||


5. upausathavaggau

1. saṅkhittūpausathasuttaṃ

41. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatau, bhikkhavai, upausathau upavutthau mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau mahājutikau mahāvipphārau| kathaṃ upavutthau ca, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatau upausathau mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau mahājutikau mahāvipphārau? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yāvajīvaṃ arahantau pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā nihitadaṇḍā nihitasatthā laj‍jī dayāpan‍nā, sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampinau viharanti| ahaṃ paj‍ja imañ‍ca rattiṃ imañ‍ca divasaṃ pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau nihitadaṇḍau nihitasatthau laj‍jī dayāpan‍nau, sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī  viharāmi| imināpaṅgaina imināpi aṅgaina (sī॰ pī॰) a॰ ni॰ 3.71 arahataṃ anukaraumi, upausathau ca mai upavutthau bhavissatī’ti| iminā paṭhamaina aṅgaina saman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘‘yāvajīvaṃ arahantau adin‍nādānaṃ pahāya adin‍nādānā paṭiviratā din‍nādāyī din‍napāṭikaṅkhī, athainaina sucibhūtaina attanā viharanti| ahaṃ paj‍ja imañ‍ca rattiṃ imañ‍ca divasaṃ adin‍nādānaṃ pahāya adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau din‍nādāyī din‍napāṭikaṅkhī, athainaina sucibhūtaina attanā viharāmi| imināpaṅgaina arahataṃ anukaraumi, upausathau ca mai upavutthau bhavissatī’ti| iminā dutiyaina aṅgaina saman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘‘yāvajīvaṃ arahantau abrahmacariyaṃ pahāya brahmacārinau ārācārinau viratā maithunā gāmadhammā| ahaṃ paj‍ja imañ‍ca rattiṃ imañ‍ca divasaṃ abrahmacariyaṃ pahāya brahmacārī ārācārī anācārī (ka॰) viratau  maithunā gāmadhammā| imināpaṅgaina arahataṃ anukaraumi, upausathau ca mai upavutthau bhavissatī’ti| iminā tatiyaina aṅgaina saman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘‘yāvajīvaṃ  arahantau musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭiviratā sac‍cavādinau sac‍casandhā thaitā pac‍cayikā avisaṃvādakau laukassa| ahaṃ paj‍ja imañ‍ca rattiṃ imañ‍ca divasaṃ musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭiviratau sac‍cavādī  sac‍casandhau thaitau pac‍cayikau avisaṃvādakau laukassa| imināpaṅgaina arahataṃ anukaraumi, upausathau ca mai upavutthau bhavissatī’ti| iminā catutthaina aṅgaina saman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘‘yāvajīvaṃ arahantau surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānaṃ pahāya surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratā| ahaṃ  paj‍ja imañ‍ca rattiṃ imañ‍ca divasaṃ surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānaṃ pahāya surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau| imināpaṅgaina arahataṃ anukaraumi, upausathau ca mai upavutthau bhavissatī’ti| iminā pañ‍camaina aṅgaina saman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘‘yāvajīvaṃ arahantau aikabhattikā rattūparatā viratā vikālabhaujanā| ahaṃ paj‍ja imañ‍ca rattiṃ imañ‍ca divasaṃ aikabhattikau rattūparatau viratau vikālabhaujanā| imināpaṅgaina arahataṃ anukaraumi, upausathau ca mai upavutthau bhavissatī’ti| iminā chaṭṭhaina aṅgaina saman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘‘yāvajīvaṃ arahantau nac‍cagītavāditavisūkadassanamālāgandhavilaipanadhāraṇamaṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānaṃ pahāya nac‍cagītavāditavisūkadassanamālāgandhavilaipanadhāraṇamaṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānā paṭiviratā| ahaṃ paj‍ja imañ‍ca rattiṃ imañ‍ca divasaṃ nac‍cagītavāditavisūkadassanamālāgandhavilaipanadhāraṇamaṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānaṃ pahāya nac‍cagītavāditavisūkadassanamālāgandhavilaipanadhāraṇamaṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau| imināpaṅgaina arahataṃ anukaraumi, upausathau ca mai upavutthau bhavissatī’ti| iminā sattamaina aṅgaina saman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘‘yāvajīvaṃ arahantau uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanaṃ pahāya uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanā paṭiviratā nīcasaiyyaṃ kappainti – mañ‍cakai vā tiṇasanthārakai vā| ahaṃ paj‍ja imañ‍ca rattiṃ imañ‍ca divasaṃ  uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanaṃ pahāya uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanā paṭiviratau nīcasaiyyaṃ kappaimi – mañ‍cakai vā tiṇasanthārakai vā| imināpaṅgaina arahataṃ anukaraumi, upausathau ca mai upavutthau bhavissatī’ti| iminā aṭṭhamaina aṅgaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| aivaṃ upavutthau khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatau upausathau mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau mahājutikau mahāvipphārau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. vitthatūpausathasuttaṃ

42. ‘‘aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatau, bhikkhavai, upausathau upavutthau mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau mahājutikau mahāvipphārau| kathaṃ upavutthau ca, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatau upausathau mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau mahājutikau mahāvipphārau? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yāvajīvaṃ arahantau pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā nihitadaṇḍā nihitasatthā laj‍jī dayāpan‍nā, sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampinau viharanti| ahaṃ paj‍ja imañ‍ca rattiṃ imañ‍ca divasaṃ pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau nihitadaṇḍau nihitasatthau laj‍jī dayāpan‍nau, sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharāmi| imināpaṅgaina arahataṃ anukaraumi, upausathau ca mai upavutthau bhavissatī’ti| iminā paṭhamaina aṅgaina saman‍nāgatau hauti…pai॰…|

‘‘‘yāvajīvaṃ arahantau uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanaṃ pahāya uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanā paṭiviratā nīcasaiyyaṃ kappainti – mañ‍cakai vā tiṇasanthārakai vā| ahaṃ paj‍ja imañ‍ca rattiṃ imañ‍ca divasaṃ uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanaṃ pahāya uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanā paṭiviratau nīcasaiyyaṃ kappaimi – mañ‍cakai vā tiṇasanthārakai vā| imināpaṅgaina arahataṃ anukaraumi, upausathau ca mai upavutthau bhavissatī’ti| iminā aṭṭhamaina aṅgaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| aivaṃ upavutthau khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatau upausathau mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau mahājutikau mahāvipphārau|

‘‘kīvamahapphalau hauti kīvamahānisaṃsau kīvamahājutikau kīvamahāvipphārau? saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yau imaisaṃ sauḷasan‍naṃ mahājanapadānaṃ pahūtarattaratanānaṃ  pahūtasattaratanānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) a॰ ni॰ 3.71 pāḷiyā ṭīkāyaṃ dassitapāḷiyaiva| tadaṭṭhakathāpi passitabbā issariyādhipac‍caṃ raj‍jaṃ kāraiyya, saiyyathidaṃ – aṅgānaṃ magadhānaṃ kāsīnaṃ kausalānaṃ vaj‍jīnaṃ mal‍lānaṃ caitīnaṃ vaṅgānaṃ kurūnaṃ pañ‍cālānaṃ macchānaṃ maj‍jānaṃ (ka॰) sūrasainānaṃ assakānaṃ avantīnaṃ gandhārānaṃ kambaujānaṃ, aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatassa  upausathassa aitaṃ aikaṃ (ka॰) kalaṃ nāgghati sauḷasiṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? kapaṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, mānusakaṃ raj‍jaṃ dibbaṃ sukhaṃ upanidhāya|

‘‘yāni, bhikkhavai, mānusakāni pañ‍ñāsa vassāni, cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ aisau aikau rattindivau rattidivau (ka॰)| tāya rattiyā tiṃsarattiyau māsau| taina māsaina dvādasamāsiyau saṃvaccharau| taina saṃvaccharaina dibbāni pañ‍ca vassasatāni cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau itthī vā purisau vā aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ upausathaṃ upavasitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| idaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, sandhāya bhāsitaṃ – ‘kapaṇaṃ mānusakaṃ raj‍jaṃ dibbaṃ sukhaṃ upanidhāya’’’|

‘‘yāni, bhikkhavai, mānusakāni vassasatāni, tāvatiṃsānaṃ daivānaṃ aisau aikau rattindivau| tāya rattiyā tiṃsarattiyau māsau| taina māsaina dvādasamāsiyau saṃvaccharau| taina saṃvaccharaina dibbaṃ vassasahassaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ daivānaṃ  āyuppamāṇaṃ| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau itthī vā purisau vā aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ upausathaṃ upavasitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā tāvatiṃsānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| idaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, sandhāya bhāsitaṃ – ‘kapaṇaṃ  mānusakaṃ raj‍jaṃ dibbaṃ sukhaṃ upanidhāya’’’|

‘‘yāni, bhikkhavai, mānusakāni dvai vassasatāni, yāmānaṃ daivānaṃ aisau aikau rattindivau| tāya rattiyā tiṃsarattiyau māsau| taina māsaina dvādasamāsiyau saṃvaccharau| taina saṃvaccharaina dibbāni dvai vassasahassāni yāmānaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau itthī vā purisau vā aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ upausathaṃ upavasitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā yāmānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| idaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, sandhāya bhāsitaṃ – ‘kapaṇaṃ mānusakaṃ raj‍jaṃ dibbaṃ sukhaṃ upanidhāya’’’|

‘‘yāni, bhikkhavai, mānusakāni cattāri vassasatāni, tusitānaṃ daivānaṃ aisau aikau rattindivau| tāya rattiyā tiṃsarattiyau māsau| taina māsaina dvādasamāsiyau saṃvaccharau| taina saṃvaccharaina dibbāni cattāri vassasahassāni  tusitānaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau itthī vā purisau vā aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ upausathaṃ upavasitvā kāyassa  bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā tusitānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| idaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, sandhāya bhāsitaṃ – ‘kapaṇaṃ mānusakaṃ raj‍jaṃ dibbaṃ sukhaṃ upanidhāya’’’|

‘‘yāni, bhikkhavai, mānusakāni aṭṭha vassasatāni, nimmānaratīnaṃ daivānaṃ aisau aikau rattindivau| tāya rattiyā tiṃsarattiyau māsau| taina māsaina dvādasamāsiyau saṃvaccharau| taina saṃvaccharaina dibbāni aṭṭha vassasahassāni nimmānaratīnaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau itthī vā purisau vā aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ  upausathaṃ upavasitvā  kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā nimmānaratīnaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| idaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, sandhāya bhāsitaṃ – ‘kapaṇaṃ mānusakaṃ raj‍jaṃ dibbaṃ sukhaṃ upanidhāya’’’|

‘‘yāni, bhikkhavai, mānusakāni sauḷasa vassasatāni, paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ daivānaṃ aisau aikau rattindivau| tāya rattiyā tiṃsarattiyau māsau| taina māsaina dvādasamāsiyau saṃvaccharau| taina saṃvaccharaina dibbāni sauḷasa vassasahassāni paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau itthī vā purisau vā aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ upausathaṃ upavasitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| idaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, sandhāya bhāsitaṃ – ‘kapaṇaṃ mānusakaṃ raj‍jaṃ dibbaṃ sukhaṃ upanidhāyā’’’ti|

‘‘pāṇaṃ na hañ‍ñai hānai (sī॰), haina (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 3.71 na cadin‍namādiyai,

musā na bhāsai na ca maj‍japau siyā|

abrahmacariyā viramaiyya maithunā,

rattiṃ na bhuñ‍jaiyya vikālabhaujanaṃ||

‘‘mālaṃ na dhārai na ca gandhamācarai gandhamādharai (ka॰),

mañ‍cai chamāyaṃ va sayaitha santhatai|

aitañhi aṭṭhaṅgikamāhupausathaṃ,

buddhaina dukkhantagunā pakāsitaṃ||

‘‘candau  ca suriyau ca ubhau sudassanā,

aubhāsayaṃ  anupariyanti yāvatā|

tamaunudā tai pana antalikkhagā,

nabhai pabhāsanti disāviraucanā||

‘‘aitasmiṃ  yaṃ vij‍jati antarai dhanaṃ,

muttā maṇi vaiḷuriyañ‍ca bhaddakaṃ|

siṅgīsuvaṇṇaṃ atha vāpi kañ‍canaṃ,

yaṃ jātarūpaṃ haṭakanti vuc‍cati||

‘‘aṭṭhaṅgupaitassa upausathassa,

kalampi tai nānubhavanti sauḷasiṃ|

candappabhā tāragaṇā ca sabbai||

‘‘tasmā hi nārī ca narau ca sīlavā,

aṭṭhaṅgupaitaṃ upavassupausathaṃ|

puñ‍ñāni katvāna sukhudrayāni,

aninditā saggamupainti ṭhāna’’nti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. visākhāsuttaṃ

43. a॰ ni॰ 3.71 aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati pubbārāmai migāramātupāsādai| atha khau visākhā migāramātā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau visākhaṃ migāramātaraṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatau khau, visākhai, upausathau upavutthau mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau mahājutikau mahāvipphārau| kathaṃ upavutthau ca, visākhai, aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatau upausathau  mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau mahājutikau mahāvipphārau? idha, visākhai, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yāvajīvaṃ arahantau pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā nihitadaṇḍā  nihitasatthā laj‍jī dayāpan‍nā, sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampinau viharanti| ahaṃ paj‍ja imañ‍ca rattiṃ imañ‍ca divasaṃ pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau nihitadaṇḍau nihitasatthau laj‍jī dayāpan‍nau, sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharāmi| imināpaṅgaina arahataṃ anukaraumi, upausathau  ca mai upavutthau bhavissatī’’’ti| iminā paṭhamaina aṅgaina saman‍nāgatau hauti…pai॰…|

‘‘‘yāvajīvaṃ arahantau uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanaṃ pahāya uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanā paṭiviratā nīcasaiyyaṃ kappainti – mañ‍cakai vā tiṇasanthārakai vā| ahaṃ paj‍ja imañ‍ca rattiṃ imañ‍ca divasaṃ uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanaṃ pahāya uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanā paṭiviratau nīcasaiyyaṃ kappaimi – mañ‍cakai vā tiṇasanthārakai vā| imināpaṅgaina arahataṃ anukaraumi, upausathau ca mai upavutthau bhavissatī’ti| iminā aṭṭhamaina aṅgaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| aivaṃ upavutthau khau, visākhai, aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatau upausathau mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau mahājutikau mahāvipphārau|
