‘‘kīvamahapphalau hauti, kīvamahānisaṃsau, kīvamahājutikau, kīvamahāvipphārau? saiyyathāpi, visākhai, yau imaisaṃ sauḷasan‍naṃ mahājanapadānaṃ pahūtarattaratanānaṃ issariyādhipac‍caṃ raj‍jaṃ kāraiyya, saiyyathidaṃ – aṅgānaṃ magadhānaṃ kāsīnaṃ kausalānaṃ vaj‍jīnaṃ mal‍lānaṃ caitīnaṃ vaṅgānaṃ kurūnaṃ pañ‍cālānaṃ macchānaṃ sūrasainānaṃ  assakānaṃ avantīnaṃ gandhārānaṃ kambaujānaṃ, aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatassa upausathassa aitaṃ kalaṃ nāgghati sauḷasiṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? kapaṇaṃ, visākhai, mānusakaṃ raj‍jaṃ dibbaṃ sukhaṃ upanidhāya|

‘‘yāni, visākhai, mānusakāni pañ‍ñāsa vassāni, cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ aisau aikau rattindivau| tāya rattiyā tiṃsarattiyau māsau| taina māsaina dvādasamāsiyau saṃvaccharau| taina saṃvaccharaina dibbāni pañ‍ca vassasatāni cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, visākhai, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau itthī vā purisau vā aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ upausathaṃ upavasitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| idaṃ khau panaitaṃ, visākhai, sandhāya bhāsitaṃ – ‘kapaṇaṃ mānusakaṃ raj‍jaṃ dibbaṃ sukhaṃ upanidhāya’’’|

‘‘yaṃ, visākhai, mānusakaṃ vassasataṃ, tāvatiṃsānaṃ daivānaṃ aisau aikau rattindivau| tāya rattiyā tiṃsarattiyau māsau| taina māsaina dvādasamāsiyau saṃvaccharau| taina saṃvaccharaina vassasahassaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, visākhai, vij‍jati  yaṃ idhaikac‍cau itthī vā purisau vā aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ upausathaṃ upavasitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā tāvatiṃsānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| idaṃ khau panaitaṃ, visākhai, sandhāya bhāsitaṃ – ‘kapaṇaṃ mānusakaṃ raj‍jaṃ dibbaṃ sukhaṃ upanidhāya’’’|

‘‘yāni, visākhai, mānusakāni dvai vassasatāni…pai॰… cattāri vassasatāni…pai॰… aṭṭha vassasatāni…pai॰… sauḷasa vassasatāni paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ daivānaṃ aisau aikau rattindivau| tāya rattiyā tiṃsarattiyau māsau| taina māsaina dvādasamāsiyau saṃvaccharau| taina saṃvaccharaina dibbāni sauḷasa vassasahassāni paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, visākhai, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau itthī vā purisau vā aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ upausathaṃ upavasitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| idaṃ khau panaitaṃ, visākhai, sandhāya bhāsitaṃ – ‘kapaṇaṃ mānusakaṃ raj‍jaṃ dibbaṃ sukhaṃ upanidhāyā’’’ti|

‘‘pāṇaṃ na hañ‍ñai na cadin‍namādiyai,

musā na bhāsai na ca maj‍japau siyā|

abrahmacariyā viramaiyya maithunā,

rattiṃ na bhuñ‍jaiyya vikālabhaujanaṃ||

‘‘mālaṃ na dhārai na ca gandhamācarai,

mañ‍cai  chamāyaṃ va sayaitha santhatai|

aitañhi aṭṭhaṅgikamāhupausathaṃ,

buddhaina dukkhantagunā pakāsitaṃ||

‘‘candau ca suriyau ca ubhau sudassanā,

aubhāsayaṃ anupariyanti yāvatā|

tamaunudā  tai pana antalikkhagā,

nabhai pabhāsanti disāviraucanā||

‘‘aitasmiṃ yaṃ vij‍jati antarai dhanaṃ,

muttā  maṇi vaiḷuriyañ‍ca bhaddakaṃ|

siṅgīsuvaṇṇaṃ atha vāpi kañ‍canaṃ,

yaṃ jātarūpaṃ haṭakanti vuc‍cati||

‘‘aṭṭhaṅgupaitassa  upausathassa,

kalampi tai nānubhavanti sauḷasiṃ|

candappabhā tāragaṇā ca sabbai||

‘‘tasmā hi nārī ca narau ca sīlavā,

aṭṭhaṅgupaitaṃ upavassupausathaṃ|

puñ‍ñāni katvāna sukhudrayāni,

aninditā saggamupainti ṭhāna’’nti|| tatiyaṃ|

4. vāsaiṭṭhasuttaṃ

44. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khau vāsaiṭṭhau upāsakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau vāsaiṭṭhaṃ upāsakaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatau, vāsaiṭṭha, upausathau upavutthau mahapphalau hauti…pai॰… aninditā saggamupainti ṭhāna’’nti|

aivaṃ vuttai vāsaiṭṭhau upāsakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘piyā  mai, bhantai, ñātisālauhitā aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ upausathaṃ upavasaiyyuṃ, piyānampi mai assa ñātisālauhitānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya| sabbai caipi, bhantai, khattiyā aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ upausathaṃ upavasaiyyuṃ, sabbaisampissa  khattiyānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya| sabbai caipi, bhantai, brāhmaṇā…pai॰… vaissā … suddā aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ upausathaṃ upavasaiyyuṃ, sabbaisampissa suddānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti|

‘‘aivamaitaṃ, vāsaiṭṭha, aivamaitaṃ, vāsaiṭṭha! sabbai caipi, vāsaiṭṭha, khattiyā aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ upausathaṃ upavasaiyyuṃ, sabbaisampissa khattiyānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya| sabbai caipi, vāsaiṭṭha, brāhmaṇā…pai॰… vaissā… suddā aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ upausathaṃ upavasaiyyuṃ, sabbaisampissa suddānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya| sadaivakau caipi, vāsaiṭṭha, laukau samārakau sabrahmakau sassamaṇabrāhmaṇī pajā sadaivamanussā aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ upausathaṃ upavasaiyyuṃ upavasaiyya (?), sadaivakassapissa sadaivakassa (sabbattha) a॰ ni॰ 4.193; ma॰ ni॰ 3.64 passitabbaṃ laukassa samārakassa  sabrahmakassa sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya| imai caipi, vāsaiṭṭha, mahāsālā aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ upausathaṃ upavasaiyyuṃ, imaisampissa mahāsālānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya ( ) (sacai caitaiyyuṃ) katthaci atthi| a॰ ni॰ 4.193 passitabbaṃ| kau pana vādau manussabhūtassā’’ti! catutthaṃ|

5. baujjhasuttaṃ

45. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau baujjhā upāsikā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau baujjhaṃ upāsikaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatau, baujjhai, upausathau upavutthau mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau mahājutikau mahāvipphārau| kathaṃ  upavutthau ca, baujjhai, aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatau upausathau mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau mahājutikau mahāvipphārau? idha, baujjhai, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yāvajīvaṃ arahantau pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā nihitadaṇḍā nihitasatthā laj‍jī dayāpan‍nā, sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampinau viharanti| ahaṃ paj‍ja imañ‍ca rattiṃ imañ‍ca divasaṃ pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau nihitadaṇḍau nihitasatthau laj‍jī dayāpan‍nau, sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharāmi| imināpaṅgaina arahataṃ anukaraumi, upausathau ca mai upavutthau bhavissatī’ti| iminā paṭhamaina aṅgaina saman‍nāgatau hauti…pai॰…|

‘‘‘yāvajīvaṃ  arahantau uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanaṃ pahāya uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanā paṭiviratā nīcasaiyyaṃ kappainti – mañ‍cakai vā tiṇasanthārakai vā| ahaṃ paj‍ja imañ‍ca rattiṃ imañ‍ca divasaṃ uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanaṃ pahāya uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanā paṭiviratau nīcasaiyyaṃ kappaimi – mañ‍cakai vā tiṇasanthārakai vā| imināpaṅgaina arahataṃ anukaraumi, upausathau ca mai upavutthau bhavissatī’ti| iminā aṭṭhamaina aṅgaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| aivaṃ upavutthau khau, baujjhai, aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatau upausathau mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau mahājutikau mahāvipphārau|

‘‘kīvamahapphalau  hauti, kīvamahānisaṃsau, kīvamahājutikau, kīvamahāvipphārau? saiyyathāpi, baujjhai, yau imaisaṃ sauḷasan‍naṃ mahājanapadānaṃ pahūtarattaratanānaṃ issariyādhipac‍caṃ raj‍jaṃ kāraiyya, saiyyathidaṃ – aṅgānaṃ magadhānaṃ kāsīnaṃ kausalānaṃ vaj‍jīnaṃ mal‍lānaṃ caitīnaṃ vaṅgānaṃ kurūnaṃ pañ‍cālānaṃ macchānaṃ sūrasainānaṃ assakānaṃ  avantīnaṃ gandhārānaṃ kambaujānaṃ, aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatassa  upausathassa aitaṃ kalaṃ nāgghati sauḷasiṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? kapaṇaṃ, baujjhai, mānusakaṃ raj‍jaṃ dibbaṃ sukhaṃ upanidhāya|

‘‘yāni, baujjhai, mānusakāni pañ‍ñāsa vassāni, cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ aisau aikau rattindivau| tāya rattiyā tiṃsarattiyau māsau| taina māsaina dvādasamāsiyau saṃvaccharau| taina saṃvaccharaina dibbāni pañ‍ca vassasatāni cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, baujjhai, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau itthī vā purisau vā aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ upausathaṃ upavasitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| idaṃ khau panaitaṃ, baujjhai, sandhāya bhāsitaṃ – ‘kapaṇaṃ mānusakaṃ raj‍jaṃ dibbaṃ sukhaṃ upanidhāya’’’|

‘‘yaṃ, baujjhai, mānusakaṃ vassasataṃ…pai॰… tāni, baujjhai, mānusakāni dvai vassasatāni…pai॰… cattāri vassasatāni…pai॰… aṭṭha vassasatāni…pai॰… sauḷasa vassasatāni paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ daivānaṃ aisau aikau rattindivau| tāya rattiyā tiṃsarattiyau māsau| taina māsaina dvādasamāsiyau saṃvaccharau| taina saṃvaccharaina dibbāni sauḷasa vassasahassāni paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, baujjhai, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau itthī vā purisau vā aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ upausathaṃ upavasitvā kāyassa bhaidā  paraṃ maraṇā paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| idaṃ khau panaitaṃ, baujjhai, sandhāya bhāsitaṃ – ‘kapaṇaṃ mānusakaṃ raj‍jaṃ dibbaṃ sukhaṃ upanidhāyā’’’ti|

‘‘pāṇaṃ  na hañ‍ñai na cadin‍namādiyai,

musā na bhāsai na ca maj‍japau siyā|

abrahmacariyā  viramaiyya maithunā,

rattiṃ na bhuñ‍jaiyya vikālabhaujanaṃ||

‘‘mālaṃ  na dhārai na ca gandhamācarai,

mañ‍cai chamāyaṃ va sayaitha santhatai|

aitañhi aṭṭhaṅgikamāhupausathaṃ,

buddhaina dukkhantagunā pakāsitaṃ||

‘‘candau ca suriyau ca ubhau sudassanā,

aubhāsayaṃ anupariyanti yāvatā|

tamaunudā tai pana antalikkhagā,

nabhai pabhāsanti disāviraucanā||

‘‘aitasmiṃ yaṃ vij‍jati antarai dhanaṃ,

muttā maṇi vaiḷuriyañ‍ca bhaddakaṃ|

siṅgīsuvaṇṇaṃ atha vāpi kañ‍canaṃ,

yaṃ jātarūpaṃ haṭakanti vuc‍cati||

‘‘aṭṭhaṅgupaitassa upausathassa,

kalampi tai nānubhavanti sauḷasiṃ|

candappabhā tāragaṇā ca sabbai||

‘‘tasmā  hi nārī ca narau ca sīlavā,

aṭṭhaṅgupaitaṃ upavassupausathaṃ|

puñ‍ñāni katvāna sukhudrayāni,

aninditā saggamupainti ṭhāna’’nti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. anuruddhasuttaṃ

46. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā anuruddhau divāvihāraṃ gatau hauti paṭisal‍līnau| atha khau sambahulā manāpakāyikā daivatā yaināyasmā anuruddhau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau tā daivatā āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘mayaṃ, bhantai  anuruddha, manāpakāyikā nāma daivatā tīsu ṭhānaisu issariyaṃ kāraima vasaṃ vattaima| mayaṃ, bhantai anuruddha, yādisakaṃ vaṇṇaṃ ākaṅkhāma tādisakaṃ vaṇṇaṃ ṭhānasau paṭilabhāma; yādisakaṃ saraṃ ākaṅkhāma  tādisakaṃ saraṃ ṭhānasau paṭilabhāma; yādisakaṃ sukhaṃ ākaṅkhāma tādisakaṃ sukhaṃ ṭhānasau paṭilabhāma| mayaṃ, bhantai anuruddha, manāpakāyikā nāma daivatā imaisu tīsu ṭhānaisu issariyaṃ kāraima vasaṃ vattaimā’’ti|

atha khau āyasmatau anuruddhassa aitadahausi – ‘‘ahau vatimā daivatā sabbāva nīlā assu nīlavaṇṇā nīlavatthā nīlālaṅkārā’’ti| atha khau tā daivatā āyasmatau anuruddhassa cittamañ‍ñāya sabbāva nīlā ahaisuṃ nīlavaṇṇā nīlavatthā nīlālaṅkārā|

atha khau āyasmatau anuruddhassa aitadahausi – ‘‘ahau vatimā daivatā sabbāva pītā assu…pai॰… sabbāva lauhitakā assu… sabbāva audātā  assu audātavaṇṇā audātavatthā audātālaṅkārā’’ti| atha khau tā daivatā āyasmatau anuruddhassa cittamañ‍ñāya sabbāva audātā ahaisuṃ audātavaṇṇā audātavatthā audātālaṅkārā|

atha khau tā daivatā aikā ca kau (sī॰), aikāva (syā॰ pī॰) gāyi aikā ca aikā pana (sī॰), aikāva (syā॰ pī॰) nac‍ci aikā ca aikā (sī॰), aikāva (syā॰ pī॰) accharaṃ vādaisi| saiyyathāpi nāma pañ‍caṅgikassa tūriyassa turiyassa (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) suvinītassa suppaṭipatāḷitassa kusalaihi susaman‍nāhatassa  saddau hauti vaggu ca rajanīyau ca kamanīyau ca paimanīyau ca madanīyau ca; aivamaivaṃ  tāsaṃ daivatānaṃ alaṅkārānaṃ saddau hauti vaggu ca rajanīyau ca kamanīyau ca paimanīyau ca madanīyau ca| atha khau āyasmā anuruddhau indriyāni aukkhipi|

atha khau tā daivatā ‘‘na khvayyau anuruddhau sādiyatī’’ti sādayatīti (saddanītidhātumālā) tatthaivantaradhāyiṃsu| atha khau āyasmā anuruddhau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā anuruddhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, divāvihāraṃ gatau haumi paṭisal‍līnau| atha khau, bhantai, sambahulā manāpakāyikā daivatā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau, bhantai, tā  daivatā maṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘mayaṃ, bhantai anuruddha, manāpakāyikā nāma daivatā tīsu ṭhānaisu issariyaṃ kāraima vasaṃ vattaima| mayaṃ, bhantai anuruddha, yādisakaṃ vaṇṇaṃ ākaṅkhāma tādisakaṃ vaṇṇaṃ ṭhānasau paṭilabhāma; yādisakaṃ saraṃ ākaṅkhāma tādisakaṃ saraṃ ṭhānasau  paṭilabhāma; yādisakaṃ sukhaṃ ākaṅkhāma tādisakaṃ sukhaṃ ṭhānasau paṭilabhāma| mayaṃ, bhantai anuruddha, manāpakāyikā nāma daivatā imaisu tīsu ṭhānaisu issariyaṃ kāraima vasaṃ vattaimā’ti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘ahau vatimā daivatā sabbāva nīlā assu nīlavaṇṇā nīlavatthā nīlālaṅkārā’ti| atha khau, bhantai, tā daivatā mama cittamañ‍ñāya sabbāva nīlā ahaisuṃ nīlavaṇṇā nīlavatthā nīlālaṅkārā|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘ahau vatimā daivatā sabbāva pītā assu…pai॰… sabbāva lauhitakā assu…pai॰… sabbāva audātā assu audātavaṇṇā audātavatthā audātālaṅkārā’ti| atha khau, bhantai, tā daivatā mama cittamañ‍ñāya sabbāva audātā ahaisuṃ audātavaṇṇā audātavatthā audātālaṅkārā|

‘‘atha khau, bhantai, tā daivatā aikā ca gāyi aikā ca nac‍ci aikā ca accharaṃ vādaisi| saiyyathāpi nāma pañ‍caṅgikassa tūriyassa suvinītassa suppaṭipatāḷitassa kusalaihi susaman‍nāhatassa saddau hauti vaggu ca rajanīyau ca kamanīyau ca paimanīyau ca madanīyau ca; aivamaivaṃ  tāsaṃ daivatānaṃ alaṅkārānaṃ saddau hauti vaggu ca rajanīyau ca kamanīyau ca paimanīyau ca madanīyau ca| atha khvāhaṃ, bhantai, indriyāni aukkhipiṃ|

‘‘atha khau, bhantai, tā daivatā ‘na khvayyau anuruddhau sādiyatī’ti tatthaivantaradhāyiṃsu| katihi nu khau, bhantai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā manāpakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti?

‘‘aṭṭhahi  khau, anuruddha, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā manāpakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? idha, anuruddha, mātugāmau yassa mātāpitarau bhattunau dainti atthakāmā hitaisinau anukampakā anukampaṃ upādāya tassa  hauti pubbuṭṭhāyinī pacchānipātinī kiṅkārapaṭissāvinī manāpacārinī piyavādinī|

‘‘yai tai bhattu garunau gurunau (ka॰) haunti – mātāti vā pitāti vā samaṇabrāhmaṇāti vā – tai sak‍karauti, garuṃ karauti garukarauti (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), mānaiti, pūjaiti, abbhāgatai ca āsanaudakaina paṭipūjaiti|

‘‘yai tai bhattu abbhantarā kammantā – uṇṇāti vā kappāsāti vā – tattha dakkhā hauti analasā tatrupāyāya tatrūpāyāya (sī॰), a॰ ni॰ 4.35; 11.14 vīmaṃsāya saman‍nāgatā alaṃ kātuṃ alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ|

‘‘yau sau bhattu abbhantarau antaujanau  – dāsāti vā paissāti vā kammakarāti vā – taisaṃ katañ‍ca katatau jānāti akatañ‍ca akatatau jānāti, gilānakānañ‍ca balābalaṃ jānāti khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyañ‍cassa pac‍caṃsaina pac‍cayaina (syā॰), pac‍cattaṃsaina (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 5.33 saṃvibhajati|

‘‘yaṃ bhattu āharati dhanaṃ vā dhañ‍ñaṃ vā jātarūpaṃ vā taṃ ārakkhaina guttiyā sampādaiti, tattha ca hauti adhuttī athainī asauṇḍī avināsikā|

‘‘upāsikā khau pana hauti buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā|

‘‘sīlavatī  khau pana hauti – pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratā, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratā, musāvādā paṭiviratā, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratā|

‘‘cāgavatī khau pana hauti| vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati muttacāgā muttacāgī (syā॰) payatapāṇinī  payatapāṇi (sī॰), payatapāṇī (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) vaussaggaratā yācayaugā dānasaṃvibhāgaratā|

‘‘imaihi khau, anuruddha, aṭṭhahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā manāpakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti|

‘‘yau  naṃ bharati sabbadā, nic‍caṃ ātāpi ussukau|

taṃ sabbakāmadaṃ taṃ sabbakāmaharaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) sabbakāmaharaṃ (a॰ ni॰ 5.33 pausaṃ, bhattāraṃ nātimañ‍ñati||

‘‘na cāpi sautthi bhattāraṃ, issāvādaina rausayai|

bhattu ca garunau sabbai, paṭipūjaiti paṇḍitā||

‘‘uṭṭhāhikā uṭṭhāyikā (ka॰) analasā, saṅgahitaparij‍janā|

bhattu manāpaṃ carati, sambhataṃ anurakkhati||

‘‘yā aivaṃ vattati nārī, bhattu chandavasānugā|

manāpā nāma tai manāpakāyikā (sī॰ ka॰) daivā, yattha sā upapaj‍jatī’’ti|| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dutiyavisākhāsuttaṃ

47. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati pubbārāmai migāramātupāsādai| atha khau visākhā migāramātā…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau visākhaṃ migāramātaraṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘aṭṭhahi khau, visākhai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā manāpakāyikānaṃ  daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? idha, visākhai, mātugāmau yassa mātāpitarau bhattunau dainti atthakāmā hitaisinau anukampakā anukampaṃ upādāya tassa hauti pubbuṭṭhāyinī pacchānipātinī kiṅkārapaṭissāvinī manāpacārinī piyavādinī…pai॰…|

‘‘cāgavatī khau  pana hauti| vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati muttacāgā payatapāṇinī vaussaggaratā yācayaugā dānasaṃvibhāgaratā| imaihi khau, visākhai, aṭṭhahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā manāpakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti|

‘‘yau naṃ bharati sabbadā, nic‍caṃ ātāpi ussukau|

taṃ sabbakāmadaṃ pausaṃ, bhattāraṃ nātimañ‍ñati||

‘‘na  cāpi sautthi bhattāraṃ, issāvādaina rausayai|

bhattu ca garunau sabbai, paṭipūjaiti paṇḍitā||

‘‘uṭṭhāhikā analasā, saṅgahitaparij‍janā|

bhattu manāpaṃ carati, sambhataṃ anurakkhati||

‘‘yā aivaṃ vattati nārī, bhattu chandavasānugā|

manāpā nāma tai manāpakāyikā (sī॰ ka॰) daivā, yattha sā upapaj‍jatī’’ti|| sattamaṃ|

8. nakulamātāsuttaṃ

48. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā bhaggaisu viharati suṃsumāragirai suṃsumāragirai (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) bhaisakaḷāvanai migadāyai| atha khau nakulamātā gahapatānī yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰…| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau nakulamātaraṃ gahapatāniṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘aṭṭhahi khau, nakulamātai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā manāpakāyikānaṃ  daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? idha, nakulamātai, mātugāmau yassa mātāpitarau bhattunau dainti atthakāmā hitaisinau anukampakā anukampaṃ upādāya tassa hauti pubbuṭṭhāyinī pacchānipātinī kiṅkārapaṭissāvinī  manāpacārinī piyavādinī|

‘‘yai tai bhattu garunau haunti – mātāti vā pitāti vā samaṇabrāhmaṇāti vā – tai sak‍karauti garuṃ karauti mānaiti pūjaiti, abbhāgatai ca āsanaudakaina paṭipūjaiti|

‘‘yai tai bhattu abbhantarā kammantā – uṇṇāti vā kappāsāti vā – tattha dakkhā hauti analasā tatrupāyāya vīmaṃsāya saman‍nāgatā alaṃ kātuṃ alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ|

‘‘yau sau bhattu abbhantarau antaujanau – dāsāti vā paissāti vā kammakarāti vā – taisaṃ katañ‍ca katatau jānāti akatañ‍ca akatatau jānāti, gilānakānañ‍ca balābalaṃ jānāti khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyañ‍cassa pac‍caṃsaina saṃvibhajati|

‘‘yaṃ  bhattā āharati dhanaṃ vā dhañ‍ñaṃ vā rajataṃ vā jātarūpaṃ vā taṃ ārakkhaina guttiyā sampādaiti, tattha ca hauti adhuttī athainī asauṇḍī avināsikā|

‘‘upāsikā khau pana hauti buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā|

‘‘sīlavatī khau pana hauti – pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā…pai॰… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratā…pai॰…|

‘‘cāgavatī khau pana hauti vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā  agāraṃ ajjhāvasati muttacāgā payatapāṇinī vaussaggaratā yācayaugā dānasaṃvibhāgaratā|

‘‘imaihi khau, nakulamātai, aṭṭhahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā manāpakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti|

‘‘yau  naṃ bharati sabbadā, nic‍caṃ ātāpi ussukau|

taṃ sabbakāmadaṃ pausaṃ, bhattāraṃ nātimañ‍ñati||

‘‘na cāpi sautthi bhattāraṃ, issāvādaina rausayai|

bhattu ca garunau sabbai, paṭipūjaiti paṇḍitā||

‘‘uṭṭhāhikā analasā, saṅgahitaparij‍janā|

bhattu manāpaṃ carati, sambhataṃ anurakkhati||

‘‘yā aivaṃ vattati nārī, bhattu chandavasānugā|

manāpā nāma tai manāpakāyikā (sī॰) daivā, yattha sā upapaj‍jatī’’ti|| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamaidhalaukikasuttaṃ

49. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati pubbārāmai migāramātupāsādai| atha khau visākhā migāramātā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰…| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau visākhaṃ migāramātaraṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘catūhi  khau, visākhai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau idhalaukavijayāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, ayaṃsa laukau āraddhau hauti| katamaihi catūhi? idha, visākhai, mātugāmau susaṃvihitakammantau hauti, saṅgahitaparijanau, bhattu manāpaṃ carati, sambhataṃ anurakkhati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, visākhai, mātugāmau susaṃvihitakammantau hauti? idha, visākhai, mātugāmau yai tai bhattu abbhantarā kammantā – uṇṇāti vā kappāsāti vā – tattha dakkhā hauti analasā tatrupāyāya vīmaṃsāya saman‍nāgatā alaṃ kātuṃ alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ| aivaṃ khau, visākhai, mātugāmau susaṃvihitakammantau  hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, visākhai, mātugāmau saṅgahitaparijanau hauti? idha, visākhai, mātugāmau yau sau  bhattu abbhantarau antaujanau – dāsāti vā paissāti vā kammakarāti vā – taisaṃ katañ‍ca katatau jānāti akatañ‍ca akatatau jānāti, gilānakānañ‍ca balābalaṃ jānāti khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyañ‍cassa pac‍caṃsaina saṃvibhajati| aivaṃ khau, visākhai, mātugāmau saṅgahitaparijanau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, visākhai, mātugāmau bhattu manāpaṃ carati? idha, visākhai, mātugāmau yaṃ bhattu amanāpasaṅkhātaṃ taṃ jīvitahaitupi na ajjhācarati| aivaṃ khau, visākhai, mātugāmau bhattu manāpaṃ carati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, visākhai, mātugāmau sambhataṃ anurakkhati? idha, visākhai, mātugāmau yaṃ bhattā āharati dhanaṃ vā dhañ‍ñaṃ vā rajataṃ vā jātarūpaṃ vā taṃ ārakkhaina guttiyā sampādaiti, tattha ca hauti adhuttī athainī asauṇḍī avināsikā| aivaṃ khau, visākhai, mātugāmau sambhataṃ anurakkhati| imaihi khau, visākhai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau idhalaukavijayāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, ayaṃsa laukau āraddhau hauti|

‘‘catūhi khau, visākhai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau paralaukavijayāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, paralaukau āraddhau hauti| katamaihi catūhi? idha, visākhai, mātugāmau saddhāsampan‍nau hauti, sīlasampan‍nau hauti, cāgasampan‍nau hauti, pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, visākhai, mātugāmau saddhāsampan‍nau hauti? idha, visākhai, mātugāmau saddhau hauti, saddahati tathāgatassa baudhiṃ – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| aivaṃ khau, visākhai, mātugāmau saddhāsampan‍nau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, visākhai, mātugāmau sīlasampan‍nau hauti? idha, visākhai, mātugāmau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti| aivaṃ khau, visākhai, mātugāmau sīlasampan‍nau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, visākhai, mātugāmau cāgasampan‍nau hauti? idha, visākhai, mātugāmau vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati muttacāgā payatapāṇinī vaussaggaratā yācayaugā dānasaṃvibhāgaratā| aivaṃ khau, visākhai, mātugāmau cāgasampan‍nau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, visākhai, mātugāmau pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau hauti? idha, visākhai, mātugāmau pañ‍ñavā hauti…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, visākhai, mātugāmau pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau hauti| imaihi khau, visākhai, catūhi  dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau paralaukavijayāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, paralaukau āraddhau hautī’’ti|

‘‘susaṃvihitakammantā, saṅgahitaparij‍janā|

bhattu manāpaṃ carati, sambhataṃ anurakkhati||

‘‘saddhā sīlaina sampan‍nā, vadañ‍ñū vītamaccharā|

nic‍caṃ maggaṃ visaudhaiti, sautthānaṃ samparāyikaṃ||

‘‘ic‍caitai aṭṭha dhammā ca, yassā vij‍janti nāriyā|

tampi sīlavatiṃ āhu, dhammaṭṭhaṃ sac‍cavādiniṃ||

‘‘sauḷasākārasampan‍nā, aṭṭhaṅgasusamāgatā|

tādisī sīlavatī upāsikā|

upapaj‍jati daivalaukaṃ manāpa’’nti|| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyaidhalaukikasuttaṃ

50. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau idhalaukavijayāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, ayaṃsa laukau āraddhau hauti| katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau susaṃvihitakammantau hauti, saṅgahitaparijanau, bhattu manāpaṃ carati, sambhataṃ anurakkhati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau susaṃvihitakammantau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau yai  tai bhattu abbhantarā kammantā…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau susaṃvihitakammantau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau saṅgahitaparijanau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau yau sau bhattu abbhantarau antaujanau…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau saṅgahitaparijanau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau bhattu manāpaṃ carati? idha, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau yaṃ bhattu amanāpasaṅkhātaṃ taṃ jīvitahaitupi na ajjhācarati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau bhattu manāpaṃ carati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau sambhataṃ anurakkhati? idha, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau yaṃ bhattā āharati…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau sambhataṃ anurakkhati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau idhalaukavijayāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, ayaṃsa laukau āraddhau hauti|

‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau paralaukavijayāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, paralaukau āraddhau hauti| katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau saddhāsampan‍nau hauti, sīlasampan‍nau hauti, cāgasampan‍nau hauti, pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau saddhāsampan‍nau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau saddhau hauti…pai॰… aivaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau saddhāsampan‍nau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau sīlasampan‍nau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau sīlasampan‍nau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau cāgasampan‍nau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau vigatamalamacchairaina  caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau cāgasampan‍nau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau pañ‍ñavā hauti…pai॰… aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau paralaukavijayāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, paralaukau āraddhau hautī’’ti|

‘‘susaṃvihitakammantā, saṅgahitaparij‍janā|

bhattu manāpaṃ carati, sambhataṃ anurakkhati||

‘‘saddhā sīlaina sampan‍nā, vadañ‍ñū vītamaccharā|

nic‍caṃ maggaṃ visaudhaiti, sautthānaṃ samparāyikaṃ||

‘‘ic‍caitai aṭṭha dhammā ca, yassā vij‍janti nāriyā|

tampi sīlavatiṃ āhu, dhammaṭṭhaṃ sac‍cavādiniṃ||

‘‘sauḷasākārasampan‍nā, aṭṭhaṅgasusamāgatā|

tādisī sīlavatī upāsikā, upapaj‍jati daivalaukaṃ manāpa’’nti|| dasamaṃ|

upausathavaggau pañ‍camau|


tassuddānaṃ –

saṃkhittai vitthatai visākhai, vāsaiṭṭhau baujjhāya pañ‍camaṃ|

anuruddhaṃ puna visākhai, nakulā idhalaukikā dvaiti||

paṭhamapaṇṇāsakaṃ samattaṃ|



2. dutiyapaṇṇāsakaṃ


(6) 1. gautamīvaggau

1. gautamīsuttaṃ

51. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| atha khau mahāpajāpatī mahāpajāpati (syā॰) cūḷava॰ 402 gautamī yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau mahāpajāpatī gautamī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu, bhantai, labhaiyya mātugāmau tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍ja’’nti| ‘‘alaṃ, gautami! mā tai ruc‍ci mātugāmassa tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jā’’ti|

dutiyampi khau mahāpajāpatī gautamī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu, bhantai, labhaiyya mātugāmau tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍ja’’nti| ‘‘alaṃ, gautami! mā tai ruc‍ci mātugāmassa tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jā’’ti| ‘‘tatiyampi khau mahāpajāpatī gautamī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu bhantai, labhaiyya mātugāmau tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍ja’’nti| ‘‘alaṃ, gautami! mā tai ruc‍ci mātugāmassa tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jā’’ti|

atha khau mahāpajāpatī gautamī ‘‘na bhagavā anujānāti mātugāmassa tathāgatappavaiditai  dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍ja’’nti dukkhī dummanā assumukhī rudamānā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau bhagavā kapilavatthusmiṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina vaisālī taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina vaisālī tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khau mahāpajāpatī gautamī kaisai chaidāpaitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā sambahulāhi sākiyānīhi saddhiṃ yaina vaisālī  taina pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina yaina vaisālī mahāvanaṃ kūṭāgārasālā tainupasaṅkami| atha khau mahāpajāpatī gautamī sūnaihi pādaihi rajaukiṇṇaina gattaina dukkhī dummanā assumukhī rudamānā bahidvārakauṭṭhakai aṭṭhāsi|

addasā khau āyasmā ānandau mahāpajāpatiṃ gautamiṃ sūnaihi pādaihi rajaukiṇṇaina gattaina dukkhiṃ dummanaṃ assumukhiṃ rudamānaṃ bahidvārakauṭṭhakai ṭhitaṃ| disvāna mahāpajāpatiṃ gautamiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu tvaṃ, gautami, sūnaihi pādaihi rajaukiṇṇaina gattaina dukkhī dummanā assumukhī rudamānā bahidvārakauṭṭhakai ṭhitā’’ti? ‘‘tathā hi pana, bhantai ānanda, na bhagavā anujānāti mātugāmassa tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍ja’’nti| ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, gautami, muhuttaṃ idhaiva tāva hauhi, yāvāhaṃ bhagavantaṃ yācāmi mātugāmassa tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍ja’’nti|

atha  khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aisā, bhantai, mahāpajāpatī gautamī sūnaihi pādaihi rajaukiṇṇaina gattaina dukkhī dummanā assumukhī rudamānā bahidvārakauṭṭhakai ṭhitā – ‘na bhagavā anujānāti mātugāmassa tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍ja’nti| sādhu, bhantai, labhaiyya mātugāmau tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍ja’’nti| ‘‘alaṃ, ānanda! mā tai ruc‍ci mātugāmassa tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jā’’ti|

dutiyampi khau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu, bhantai, labhaiyya mātugāmau tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍ja’’nti| ‘‘alaṃ, ānanda! mā tai ruc‍ci mātugāmassa tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jā’’ti|

atha  khau āyasmatau ānandassa aitadahausi – ‘‘na bhagavā anujānāti mātugāmassa tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jaṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ añ‍ñainapi pariyāyaina bhagavantaṃ yācaiyyaṃ mātugāmassa tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍ja’’nti| atha khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhabbau nu khau, bhantai, mātugāmau tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitvā sautāpattiphalaṃ vā  sakadāgāmiphalaṃ vā anāgāmiphalaṃ vā arahattaphalaṃ vā sacchikātu’’nti? ‘‘bhabbau, ānanda, mātugāmau tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitvā sautāpattiphalampi sakadāgāmiphalampi anāgāmiphalampi arahattaphalampi sacchikātu’’nti| ‘‘sacai, bhantai, bhabbau mātugāmau tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitvā sautāpattiphalampi…pai॰… arahattaphalampi sacchikātuṃ, bahukārā, bhantai, mahāpajāpatī gautamī bhagavatau mātucchā āpādikā pausikā khīrassa dāyikā; bhagavantaṃ janaittiyā kālaṅkatāya thañ‍ñaṃ pāyaisi| sādhu, bhantai, labhaiyya mātugāmau tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍ja’’nti|

‘‘sacai, ānanda, mahāpajāpatī gautamī aṭṭha garudhammai paṭiggaṇhāti, sāvassā hautu upasampadā –

pāci॰ 149; cūḷava॰ 403 ‘‘vassasatūpasampan‍nāya bhikkhuniyā tadahūpasampan‍nassa bhikkhunau abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ añ‍jalikammaṃ sāmīcikammaṃ kattabbaṃ| ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā garukatvā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau|

‘‘na bhikkhuniyā abhikkhukai āvāsai vassaṃ upagantabbaṃ| ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau|

‘‘anvaḍḍhamāsaṃ bhikkhuniyā bhikkhusaṅghatau dvai dhammā pac‍cāsīsitabbā pac‍cāsiṃsitabbā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) – upausathapucchakañ‍ca, auvādūpasaṅkamanañ‍ca| ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau|

‘‘vassaṃvuṭṭhāya  bhikkhuniyā ubhatausaṅghai tīhi ṭhānaihi pavāraitabbaṃ – diṭṭhaina vā sutaina vā parisaṅkāya vā| ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā  yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau|

‘‘garudhammaṃ  ajjhāpan‍nāya bhikkhuniyā ubhatausaṅghai pakkhamānattaṃ caritabbaṃ| ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau|

‘‘dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhāya sikkhamānāya ubhatausaṅghai upasampadā pariyaisitabbā| ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau|

‘‘na kainaci pariyāyaina bhikkhuniyā bhikkhu ak‍kausitabbau paribhāsitabbau| ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau|

‘‘aj‍jataggai auvaṭau bhikkhunīnaṃ bhikkhūsu vacanapathau, anauvaṭau bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīsu vacanapathau| ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau|

‘‘sacai, ānanda, mahāpajāpatī gautamī imai aṭṭha garudhammai paṭiggaṇhāti, sāvassā hautu upasampadā’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau santikai imai aṭṭha garudhammai uggahaitvā yaina mahāpajāpatī gautamī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā mahāpajāpatiṃ gautamiṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘sacai khau tvaṃ, gautami, aṭṭha garudhammai paṭiggaṇhaiyyāsi, sāva tai bhavissati upasampadā –

‘‘vassasatūpasampan‍nāya bhikkhuniyā tadahūpasampan‍nassa bhikkhunau abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ añ‍jalikammaṃ sāmīcikammaṃ kattabbaṃ| ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau…pai॰…|

‘‘aj‍jataggai  auvaṭau bhikkhunīnaṃ bhikkhūsu vacanapathau, anauvaṭau  bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīsu vacanapathau| ayampi dhammau  sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau| sacai khau tvaṃ, gautami, imai aṭṭha garudhammai paṭiggaṇhaiyyāsi, sāva tai bhavissati upasampadā’’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai ānanda, itthī vā purisau vā daharau yuvā maṇḍanakajātikau maṇḍanakajātiyau (sī॰ pī॰) sīsaṃnhātau sīsaṃnahātau (sī॰ pī॰), sīsanahātau (syā॰) uppalamālaṃ vā vassikamālaṃ vā adhimuttakamālaṃ atimuttakamālaṃ (sī॰) vā labhitvā ubhauhi hatthaihi paṭiggahaitvā uttamaṅgai sirasmiṃ patiṭṭhāpaiyya; aivamaivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai ānanda, imai aṭṭha garudhammai paṭiggaṇhāmi yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyai’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘paṭiggahitā, bhantai, mahāpajāpatiyā gautamiyā aṭṭha garudhammā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyā’’ti|

‘‘sacai, ānanda, nālabhissa mātugāmau tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jaṃ, ciraṭṭhitikaṃ, ānanda, brahmacariyaṃ abhavissa, vassasahassamaiva saddhammau tiṭṭhaiyya| yatau ca khau, ānanda, mātugāmau tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau, na dāni, ānanda, brahmacariyaṃ ciraṭṭhitikaṃ bhavissati| pañ‍caiva dāni, ānanda, vassasatāni saddhammau ṭhassati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, ānanda, yāni kānici kulāni bahutthikāni bahukitthikāni (sī॰ pī॰), bahuitthikāni (syā॰) appapurisakāni, tāni suppadhaṃsiyāni haunti cauraihi kumbhatthainakaihi; aivamaivaṃ khau, ānanda, yasmiṃ dhammavinayai labhati mātugāmau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jaṃ, na taṃ brahmacariyaṃ ciraṭṭhitikaṃ hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, ānanda, sampan‍nai sālikkhaittai saitaṭṭhikā  nāma raugajāti nipatati, aivaṃ taṃ sālikkhaittaṃ na ciraṭṭhitikaṃ hauti; aivamaivaṃ khau, ānanda, yasmiṃ dhammavinayai labhati mātugāmau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jaṃ, na taṃ brahmacariyaṃ ciraṭṭhitikaṃ hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, ānanda, sampan‍nai ucchukkhaittai mañ‍jiṭṭhikā mañ‍jaiṭṭhikā (sī॰ syā॰) nāma raugajāti nipatati, aivaṃ taṃ ucchukkhaittaṃ na ciraṭṭhitikaṃ hauti; aivamaivaṃ khau, ānanda, yasmiṃ dhammavinayai labhati mātugāmau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jaṃ, na taṃ brahmacariyaṃ ciraṭṭhitikaṃ hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, ānanda, purisau mahatau taḷākassa paṭikac‍caiva paṭigac‍caiva (sī॰ pī॰) āḷiṃ bandhaiyya yāvadaiva udakassa anatik‍kamanāya; aivamaivaṃ khau, ānanda, mayā paṭikac‍caiva bhikkhunīnaṃ aṭṭha garudhammā pañ‍ñattā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. auvādasuttaṃ

52. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘katihi nu khau, bhantai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bhikkhunauvādakau samman‍nitabbau’’ti?

pāci॰ 147 ‘‘aṭṭhahi khau, ānanda, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bhikkhunauvādakau samman‍nitabbau| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? idhānanda, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti…pai॰… samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu; bahussutau hauti…pai॰… diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā; ubhayāni khau panassa pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina svāgatāni  haunti suvibhattāni suppavattīni suvinicchitāni suttasau anubyañ‍janasau; kalyāṇavācau hauti kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau, pauriyā vācāya saman‍nāgatau vissaṭṭhāya visaṭṭhāya (ka॰) anailagaḷāya anaiḷagaḷāya (sī॰ ka॰) atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā; paṭibalau hauti  bhikkhunisaṅghassa dhammiyā kathāya sandassaituṃ samādapaituṃ samuttaijaituṃ sampahaṃsaituṃ; yaibhuyyaina bhikkhunīnaṃ piyau hauti manāpau; na khau panaitaṃ bhagavantaṃ uddissa pabbajitāya kāsāyavatthanivasanāya garudhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍napubbau hauti; vīsativassau vā hauti atiraikavīsativassau vā| imaihi khau, ānanda, aṭṭhahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bhikkhunauvādakau samman‍nitabbau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. saṃkhittasuttaṃ

53. cūḷava॰ 406 aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khau mahāpajāpatī gautamī yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau sā mahāpajāpatī gautamī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘sādhu  mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu, yamahaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ sutvā aikā vūpakaṭṭhā appamattā ātāpinī pahitattā viharaiyya’’nti| ‘‘yai khau tvaṃ, gautami, dhammai jānaiyyāsi – ‘imai dhammā sarāgāya saṃvattanti, nau virāgāya; saṃyaugāya saṃvattanti, nau visaṃyaugāya; ācayāya saṃvattanti, nau apacayāya; mahicchatāya saṃvattanti, nau appicchatāya; asantuṭṭhiyā saṃvattanti, nau santuṭṭhiyā; saṅgaṇikāya saṃvattanti, nau pavivaikāya; kausaj‍jāya saṃvattanti, nau vīriyārambhāya; dubbharatāya saṃvattanti, nau subharatāyā’ti, aikaṃsaina, gautami, dhāraiyyāsi – ‘naisau dhammau, naisau vinayau, naitaṃ satthusāsana’’’nti|

‘‘yai ca khau tvaṃ, gautami, dhammai jānaiyyāsi – ‘imai dhammā virāgāya saṃvattanti, nau sarāgāya; visaṃyaugāya saṃvattanti, nau saṃyaugāya; apacayāya saṃvattanti, nau ācayāya; appicchatāya saṃvattanti, nau mahicchatāya; santuṭṭhiyā saṃvattanti, nau asantuṭṭhiyā; pavivaikāya saṃvattanti, nau saṅgaṇikāya; vīriyārambhāya saṃvattanti, nau kausaj‍jāya; subharatāya saṃvattanti, nau dubbharatāyā’ti, aikaṃsaina, gautami, dhāraiyyāsi – ‘aisau dhammau, aisau vinayau, aitaṃ satthusāsana’’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dīghajāṇusuttaṃ

54. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kauliyaisu viharati kak‍karapattaṃ nāma kauliyānaṃ nigamau| atha khau dīghajāṇu kauliyaputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau dīghajāṇu kauliyaputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mayaṃ, bhantai, gihī kāmabhauginau kāmabhaugī (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) puttasambādhasayanaṃ ajjhāvasāma, kāsikacandanaṃ pac‍canubhauma, mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ dhārayāma, jātarūparajataṃ sādayāma| taisaṃ nau, bhantai, bhagavā amhākaṃ tathā dhammaṃ daisaitu yai amhākaṃ assu dhammā diṭṭhadhammahitāya diṭṭhadhammasukhāya, samparāyahitāya samparāyasukhāyā’’ti|

‘‘cattāraumai, byagghapaj‍ja, dhammā kulaputtassa diṭṭhadhammahitāya saṃvattanti diṭṭhadhammasukhāya| katamai cattārau? uṭṭhānasampadā, ārakkhasampadā, kalyāṇamittatā, samajīvitā samajīvikatā (sī॰) a॰ ni॰ 8.75| katamā  ca, byagghapaj‍ja, uṭṭhānasampadā? idha, byagghapaj‍ja, kulaputtau  yaina kammaṭṭhānaina jīvikaṃ jīvitaṃ (ka॰) kappaiti – yadi kasiyā, yadi vaṇij‍jāya, yadi gaurakkhaina, yadi issattaina issatthaina (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), yadi rājapaurisaina, yadi sippañ‍ñataraina – tattha dakkhau hauti analasau, tatrupāyāya vīmaṃsāya saman‍nāgatau, alaṃ kātuṃ alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, byagghapaj‍ja, uṭṭhānasampadā|

‘‘katamā ca, byagghapaj‍ja, ārakkhasampadā? idha, byagghapaj‍ja, kulaputtassa bhaugā haunti uṭṭhānavīriyādhigatā  bāhābalaparicitā, saidāvakkhittā, dhammikā dhammaladdhā| tai ārakkhaina guttiyā sampādaiti – ‘kinti mai imai bhaugai naiva rājānau haraiyyuṃ, na caurā haraiyyuṃ, na aggi ḍahaiyya, na udakaṃ vahaiyya, na appiyā dāyādā haraiyyu’nti! ayaṃ vuc‍cati, byagghapaj‍ja, ārakkhasampadā|

‘‘katamā ca, byagghapaj‍ja, kalyāṇamittatā? idha, byagghapaj‍ja, kulaputtau yasmiṃ gāmai vā nigamai vā paṭivasati, tattha yai tai haunti – gahapatī vā gahapatiputtā vā daharā vā vuddhasīlinau, vuddhā vā vuddhasīlinau, saddhāsampan‍nā, sīlasampan‍nā, cāgasampan‍nā, pañ‍ñāsampan‍nā – taihi saddhiṃ santiṭṭhati sal‍lapati sākacchaṃ samāpaj‍jati; yathārūpānaṃ saddhāsampan‍nānaṃ saddhāsampadaṃ anusikkhati, yathārūpānaṃ sīlasampan‍nānaṃ sīlasampadaṃ anusikkhati, yathārūpānaṃ cāgasampan‍nānaṃ cāgasampadaṃ anusikkhati, yathārūpānaṃ pañ‍ñāsampan‍nānaṃ pañ‍ñāsampadaṃ anusikkhati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, byagghapaj‍ja, kalyāṇamittatā|

‘‘katamā  ca, byagghapaj‍ja, samajīvitā? idha, byagghapaj‍ja, kulaputtau āyañ‍ca bhaugānaṃ viditvā, vayañ‍ca bhaugānaṃ viditvā, samaṃ jīvikaṃ samajīvikaṃ (syā॰), samajīvitaṃ (ka॰) kappaiti nāc‍caugāḷhaṃ nātihīnaṃ – ‘aivaṃ mai āyau vayaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati, na ca mai vayau āyaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatī’ti| saiyyathāpi, byagghapaj‍ja, tulādhārau vā tulādhārantaivāsī vā tulaṃ paggahaitvā jānāti – ‘aittakaina vā aunataṃ auṇataṃ (ka॰), aittakaina vā un‍nata’nti uṇṇatanti (ka॰); aivamaivaṃ khau, byagghapaj‍ja, kulaputtau āyañ‍ca bhaugānaṃ viditvā, vayañ‍ca bhaugānaṃ viditvā, samaṃ jīvikaṃ kappaiti nāc‍caugāḷhaṃ nātihīnaṃ – ‘aivaṃ mai āyau vayaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati, na ca mai vayau āyaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatī’ti| sacāyaṃ, byagghapaj‍ja, kulaputtau appāyau  samānau uḷāraṃ jīvikaṃ jīvitaṃ (ka॰) kappaiti, tassa bhavanti vattārau – ‘udumbarakhādīvāyaṃ udumbarakhādikaṃ vāyaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), udumbarakhādakaṃ cāyaṃ (syā॰) kulaputtau bhaugai khādatī’ti| sacai panāyaṃ, byagghapaj‍ja, kulaputtau mahāyau samānau kasiraṃ jīvikaṃ jīvitaṃ (ka॰) kappaiti, tassa bhavanti vattārau – ‘ajaiṭṭhamaraṇaṃvāyaṃ ajaddhumārikaṃ vāyaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), addhamārakaṃ cāyaṃ (syā॰), aittha jaddhūti asanaṃ = bhattabhuñ‍janaṃ, tasmā ajaddhumārikanti anasanamaraṇanti vuttaṃ hauti| ma॰ ni॰ 1.379 adhaulipiyā ‘‘ajaddhuka’’nti padaṃ dassitaṃ kulaputtau marissatī’ti| yatau ca khauyaṃ, byagghapaj‍ja, kulaputtau āyañ‍ca bhaugānaṃ viditvā, vayañ‍ca bhaugānaṃ viditvā, samaṃ jīvikaṃ kappaiti nāc‍caugāḷhaṃ nātihīnaṃ – ‘aivaṃ mai āyau vayaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati, na ca mai vayau āyaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatī’ti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, byagghapaj‍ja, samajīvitā|

‘‘aivaṃ samuppan‍nānaṃ, byagghapaj‍ja, bhaugānaṃ cattāri apāyamukhāni haunti – itthidhuttau, surādhuttau, akkhadhuttau, pāpamittau pāpasahāyau pāpasampavaṅkau| saiyyathāpi, byagghapaj‍ja, mahatau taḷākassa cattāri caiva  āyamukhāni, cattāri ca apāyamukhāni| tassa purisau yāni caiva āyamukhāni tāni pidahaiyya, yāni ca apāyamukhāni tāni vivaraiyya; daivau ca na sammā dhāraṃ anuppavaicchaiyya| aivañhi tassa, byagghapaj‍ja, mahatau taḷākassa parihāniyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā, nau vuddhi; aivamaivaṃ, byagghapaj‍ja, aivaṃ samuppan‍nānaṃ bhaugānaṃ cattāri apāyamukhāni haunti – itthidhuttau, surādhuttau, akkhadhuttau, pāpamittau pāpasahāyau pāpasampavaṅkau|

‘‘aivaṃ samuppan‍nānaṃ, byagghapaj‍ja, bhaugānaṃ cattāri āyamukhāni haunti – na itthidhuttau, na surādhuttau, na akkhadhuttau, kalyāṇamittau kalyāṇasahāyau kalyāṇasampavaṅkau| saiyyathāpi, byagghapaj‍ja, mahatau taḷākassa cattāri caiva āyamukhāni, cattāri ca apāyamukhāni| tassa purisau yāni caiva āyamukhāni tāni vivaraiyya, yāni ca apāyamukhāni tāni pidahaiyya; daivau ca sammā dhāraṃ anuppavaicchaiyya| aivañhi tassa, byagghapaj‍ja, mahatau taḷākassa vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni; aivamaivaṃ khau, byagghapaj‍ja, aivaṃ samuppan‍nānaṃ bhaugānaṃ cattāri āyamukhāni haunti – na itthidhuttau, na surādhuttau, na akkhadhuttau, kalyāṇamittau kalyāṇasahāyau  kalyāṇasampavaṅkau| imai khau, byagghapaj‍ja, cattārau dhammā kulaputtassa diṭṭhadhammahitāya saṃvattanti diṭṭhadhammasukhāya|

‘‘cattāraumai, byagghapaj‍ja, dhammā kulaputtassa samparāyahitāya saṃvattanti samparāyasukhāya| katamai cattārau? saddhāsampadā, sīlasampadā, cāgasampadā, pañ‍ñāsampadā| katamā ca, byagghapaj‍ja, saddhāsampadā? idha, byagghapaj‍ja, kulaputtau saddhau hauti, saddahati tathāgatassa baudhiṃ – ‘itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, byagghapaj‍ja, saddhāsampadā|

‘‘katamā ca, byagghapaj‍ja, sīlasampadā? idha, byagghapaj‍ja, kulaputtau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, byagghapaj‍ja, sīlasampadā|

‘‘katamā ca, byagghapaj‍ja, cāgasampadā? idha, byagghapaj‍ja, kulaputtau vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati muttacāgau payatapāṇi vaussaggaratau yācayaugau dānasaṃvibhāgaratau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, byagghapaj‍ja, cāgasampadā|

‘‘katamā ca, byagghapaj‍ja, pañ‍ñāsampadā? idha, byagghapaj‍ja, kulaputtau pañ‍ñavā hauti, udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, byagghapaj‍ja, pañ‍ñāsampadā| imai khau, byagghapaj‍ja, cattārau dhammā kulaputtassa samparāyahitāya saṃvattanti samparāyasukhāyā’’ti|

‘‘uṭṭhātā kammadhaiyyaisu, appamattau vidhānavā|

samaṃ kappaiti jīvikaṃ jīvitaṃ (ka॰), sambhataṃ anurakkhati||

‘‘saddhau sīlaina sampan‍nau, vadañ‍ñū vītamaccharau|

nic‍caṃ  maggaṃ visaudhaiti, sautthānaṃ samparāyikaṃ||

‘‘ic‍caitai  aṭṭha dhammā ca, saddhassa gharamaisinau|

akkhātā sac‍canāmaina, ubhayattha sukhāvahā||

‘‘diṭṭhadhammahitatthāya, samparāyasukhāya ca|

aivamaitaṃ gahaṭṭhānaṃ, cāgau puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhatī’’ti|| catutthaṃ|

5. uj‍jayasuttaṃ

55. atha  khau uj‍jayau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau uj‍jayau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mayaṃ, bhau gautama, pavāsaṃ gantukāmā| taisaṃ nau bhavaṃ gautamau amhākaṃ tathā dhammaṃ daisaitu – yai amhākaṃ assu dhammā diṭṭhadhammahitāya, diṭṭhadhammasukhāya, samparāyahitāya, samparāyasukhāyā’’ti|

‘‘cattāraumai, brāhmaṇa, dhammā kulaputtassa diṭṭhadhammahitāya saṃvattanti, diṭṭhadhammasukhāya| katamai cattārau? uṭṭhānasampadā, ārakkhasampadā, kalyāṇamittatā, samajīvitā| katamā ca, brāhmaṇa, uṭṭhānasampadā? idha, brāhmaṇa, kulaputtau yaina kammaṭṭhānaina jīvikaṃ kappaiti – yadi kasiyā, yadi vaṇij‍jāya, yadi gaurakkhaina, yadi issattaina, yadi rājapaurisaina, yadi sippañ‍ñataraina – tattha dakkhau hauti analasau, tatrupāyāya vīmaṃsāya saman‍nāgatau, alaṃ kātuṃ alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, uṭṭhānasampadā|

‘‘katamā ca, brāhmaṇa, ārakkhasampadā? idha, brāhmaṇa, kulaputtassa  bhaugā haunti uṭṭhānavīriyādhigatā, bāhābalaparicitā, saidāvakkhittā, dhammikā dhammaladdhā| tai ārakkhaina guttiyā sampādaiti – ‘kinti mai imai bhaugai naiva rājānau haraiyyuṃ, na caurā haraiyyuṃ, na aggi ḍahaiyya, na udakaṃ vahaiyya, na appiyā dāyādā haraiyyu’nti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, ārakkhasampadā|

‘‘katamā ca, brāhmaṇa, kalyāṇamittatā? idha, brāhmaṇa, kulaputtau yasmiṃ gāmai vā nigamai  vā paṭivasati tatra yai tai haunti – gahapatī vā gahapatiputtā vā daharā vā vuddhasīlinau, vuddhā vā vuddhasīlinau, saddhāsampan‍nā, sīlasampan‍nā, cāgasampan‍nā, pañ‍ñāsampan‍nā – taihi saddhiṃ santiṭṭhati sal‍lapati sākacchaṃ samāpaj‍jati; yathārūpānaṃ saddhāsampan‍nānaṃ saddhāsampadaṃ anusikkhati, yathārūpānaṃ sīlasampan‍nānaṃ sīlasampadaṃ anusikkhati, yathārūpānaṃ cāgasampan‍nānaṃ cāgasampadaṃ anusikkhati, yathārūpānaṃ pañ‍ñāsampan‍nānaṃ pañ‍ñāsampadaṃ anusikkhati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, kalyāṇamittatā|

‘‘katamā  ca, brāhmaṇa, samajīvitā? idha, brāhmaṇa, kulaputtau āyañ‍ca bhaugānaṃ viditvā vayañ‍ca bhaugānaṃ viditvā samaṃ jīvikaṃ kappaiti nāc‍caugāḷhaṃ  nātihīnaṃ – ‘aivaṃ mai āyau vayaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati, na ca mai vayau āyaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatī’ti| saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, tulādhārau vā tulādhārantaivāsī vā tulaṃ paggahaitvā jānāti – ‘aittakaina vā aunataṃ, aittakaina vā un‍nata’nti; aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, kulaputtau āyañ‍ca  bhaugānaṃ viditvā vayañ‍ca bhaugānaṃ viditvā samaṃ jīvikaṃ kappaiti nāc‍caugāḷhaṃ nātihīnaṃ – ‘aivaṃ mai āyau vayaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati, na ca mai vayau āyaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatī’ti| sacāyaṃ, brāhmaṇa, kulaputtau appāyau samānau uḷāraṃ jīvikaṃ kappaiti, tassa bhavanti vattārau – ‘udumbarakhādīvāyaṃ kulaputtau bhaugai khādatī’ti| sacai panāyaṃ, brāhmaṇa, kulaputtau mahāyau samānau kasiraṃ jīvikaṃ kappaiti, tassa bhavanti vattārau – ‘ajaiṭṭhamaraṇaṃvāyaṃ kulaputtau marissatī’ti| yatau ca khauyaṃ, brāhmaṇa, kulaputtau āyañ‍ca bhaugānaṃ viditvā vayañ‍ca bhaugānaṃ viditvā samaṃ jīvikaṃ kappaiti nāc‍caugāḷhaṃ nātihīnaṃ – ‘aivaṃ mai āyau vayaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati, na ca mai vayau āyaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatī’ti, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, samajīvitā|

‘‘aivaṃ samuppan‍nānaṃ, brāhmaṇa, bhaugānaṃ cattāri apāyamukhāni haunti – itthidhuttau, surādhuttau, akkhadhuttau, pāpamittau pāpasahāyau pāpasampavaṅkau| saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, mahatau taḷākassa cattāri caiva āyamukhāni, cattāri ca apāyamukhāni| tassa purisau yāni caiva āyamukhāni tāni pidahaiyya, yāni ca apāyamukhāni tāni vivaraiyya; daivau ca na sammā dhāraṃ anuppavaicchaiyya| aivañhi tassa brāhmaṇa, mahatau taḷākassa parihāniyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā, nau vuddhi; aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, aivaṃ samuppan‍nānaṃ bhaugānaṃ cattāri apāyamukhāni haunti – itthidhuttau, surādhuttau, akkhadhuttau, pāpamittau pāpasahāyau pāpasampavaṅkau|

‘‘aivaṃ  samuppan‍nānaṃ, brāhmaṇa, bhaugānaṃ cattāri āyamukhāni haunti – na itthidhuttau, na surādhuttau, na akkhadhuttau, kalyāṇamittau kalyāṇasahāyau kalyāṇasampavaṅkau| saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, mahatau taḷākassa cattāri caiva āyamukhāni cattāri ca apāyamukhāni| tassa purisau yāni caiva āyamukhāni tāni vivaraiyya, yāni ca apāyamukhāni  tāni pidahaiyya; daivau ca sammā dhāraṃ anuppavaicchaiyya| aivañhi tassa, brāhmaṇa, mahatau taḷākassa vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā, nau parihāni; aivamaivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, aivaṃ samuppan‍nānaṃ bhaugānaṃ cattāri āyamukhāni haunti – na itthidhuttau…pai॰… kalyāṇasampavaṅkau| imai khau, brāhmaṇa, cattārau dhammā kulaputtassa diṭṭhadhammahitāya saṃvattanti diṭṭhadhammasukhāya|

‘‘cattāraumai, brāhmaṇa, kulaputtassa dhammā samparāyahitāya saṃvattanti samparāyasukhāya| katamai cattārau? saddhāsampadā, sīlasampadā, cāgasampadā, pañ‍ñāsampadā| katamā ca, brāhmaṇa, saddhāsampadā? idha, brāhmaṇa, kulaputtau saddhau hauti, saddahati tathāgatassa baudhiṃ – ‘itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, saddhāsampadā|

‘‘katamā ca, brāhmaṇa, sīlasampadā? idha, brāhmaṇa, kulaputtau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, sīlasampadā|

‘‘katamā  ca, brāhmaṇa, cāgasampadā? idha, brāhmaṇa, kulaputtau vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati muttacāgau payatapāṇi vaussaggaratau yācayaugau dānasaṃvibhāgaratau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, cāgasampadā|

‘‘katamā ca, brāhmaṇa, pañ‍ñāsampadā? idha, brāhmaṇa, kulaputtau pañ‍ñavā hauti…pai॰… sammā  dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, pañ‍ñāsampadā| imai khau, brāhmaṇa, cattārau dhammā kulaputtassa samparāyahitāya saṃvattanti samparāyasukhāyā’’ti|

‘‘uṭṭhātā kammadhaiyyaisu, appamattau vidhānavā|

samaṃ kappaiti jīvikaṃ, sambhataṃ anurakkhati||

‘‘saddhau sīlaina sampan‍nau, vadañ‍ñū vītamaccharau|

nic‍caṃ maggaṃ visaudhaiti, sautthānaṃ samparāyikaṃ||

‘‘ic‍caitai aṭṭha dhammā ca, saddhassa gharamaisinau|

akkhātā sac‍canāmaina, ubhayattha sukhāvahā||

‘‘diṭṭhadhammahitatthāya, samparāyasukhāya ca|

aivamaitaṃ gahaṭṭhānaṃ, cāgau puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhatī’’ti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. bhayasuttaṃ

56. ‘‘‘bhaya’nti  cūḷani॰ khaggavisāṇasuttaniddaisa 137, bhikkhavai, kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘dukkha’nti, bhikkhavai, kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘raugau’ti, bhikkhavai, kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘gaṇḍau’ti, bhikkhavai, kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘sal‍la’nti, bhikkhavai, kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘saṅgau’ti, bhikkhavai, kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘paṅkau’ti, bhikkhavai, kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘gabbhau’ti, bhikkhavai, kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| kasmā ca, bhikkhavai, ‘bhaya’nti kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ? yasmā ca kāmarāgarattāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, chandarāgavinibaddhau diṭṭhadhammikāpi bhayā na parimuc‍cati, samparāyikāpi  bhayā na parimuc‍cati, tasmā ‘bhaya’nti kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| kasmā ca, bhikkhavai, ‘dukkha’nti…pai॰… ‘raugau’ti… ‘gaṇḍau’ti… ‘sal‍la’nti… ‘saṅgau’ti… ‘paṅkau’ti… ‘gabbhau’ti kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ? yasmā ca kāmarāgarattāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, chandarāgavinibaddhau diṭṭhadhammikāpi gabbhā na parimuc‍cati, samparāyikāpi gabbhā na parimuc‍cati, tasmā ‘gabbhau’ti kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ’’|

‘‘bhayaṃ  dukkhañ‍ca raugau ca, gaṇḍau sal‍lañ‍ca saṅgau ca|

paṅkau gabbhau ca ubhayaṃ, aitai kāmā pavuc‍canti|

yattha sattau puthuj‍janau||

‘‘autiṇṇau sātarūpaina, puna gabbhāya gacchati|

yatau ca bhikkhu ātāpī, sampajañ‍ñaṃ sampajañ‍ñau (syā॰ ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.251 passitabbaṃ na ric‍cati||

‘‘sau imaṃ palipathaṃ duggaṃ, atik‍kamma tathāvidhau|

pajaṃ jātijarūpaitaṃ, phandamānaṃ avaikkhatī’’ti|| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamaāhunaiyyasuttaṃ

57. ‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti  …pai॰… samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu; bahussutau hauti…pai॰… diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā; kalyāṇamittau hauti kalyāṇasahāyau kalyāṇasampavaṅkau; sammādiṭṭhikau hauti, sammādassanaina  saman‍nāgatau; catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī; anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati; dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti; āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyaāhunaiyyasuttaṃ

58. ‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti  …pai॰… samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu; bahussutau hauti…pai॰… diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā; āraddhavīriyau viharati thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu; ārañ‍ñikau hauti pantasaināsanau; aratiratisahau hauti, uppan‍naṃ aratiṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharati; bhayabhairavasahau hauti, uppan‍naṃ bhayabhairavaṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharati; catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī; āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā  upasampaj‍ja viharati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamapuggalasuttaṃ

59. ‘‘aṭṭhimai bhikkhavai, puggalā āhunaiyyā pāhunaiyyā dakkhiṇaiyyā añ‍jalikaraṇīyā anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa? katamai aṭṭha? sautāpan‍nau, sautāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau, sakadāgāmī, sakadāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau, anāgāmī, anāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau, arahā, arahattāya paṭipan‍nau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha puggalā āhunaiyyā…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti|

‘‘cattārau  ca paṭipan‍nā, cattārau ca phalai ṭhitā|

aisa saṅghau ujubhūtau, pañ‍ñāsīlasamāhitau||

‘‘yajamānānaṃ manussānaṃ, puñ‍ñapaikkhāna pāṇinaṃ|

karautaṃ aupadhikaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, saṅghai din‍naṃ mahapphala’’nti|| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyapuggalasuttaṃ

60. ‘‘aṭṭhimai, bhikkhavai, puggalā āhunaiyyā…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamai aṭṭha? sautāpan‍nau, sautāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau…pai॰… arahā, arahattāya  paṭipan‍nau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha puggalā āhunaiyyā…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti|

‘‘cattārau ca paṭipan‍nā, cattārau ca phalai ṭhitā|

aisa  saṅghau samuk‍kaṭṭhau, sattānaṃ aṭṭha puggalā||

‘‘yajamānānaṃ manussānaṃ, puñ‍ñapaikkhāna pāṇinaṃ|

karautaṃ aupadhikaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, aittha din‍naṃ mahapphala’’nti|| dasamaṃ|

gautamīvaggau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

gautamī auvādaṃ saṃkhittaṃ, dīghajāṇu ca uj‍jayau|

bhayā dvai āhunaiyyā ca, dvai ca aṭṭha puggalāti||


(7) 2. bhūmicālavaggau

1. icchāsuttaṃ

61. a॰ ni॰ 8.77 ‘‘aṭṭhimai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai aṭṭha? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pavivittassa viharatau nirāyattavuttinau icchā uppaj‍jati lābhāya| sau uṭṭhahati ghaṭati vāyamati lābhāya| tassa uṭṭhahatau ghaṭatau vāyamatau lābhāya lābhau nuppaj‍jati| sau taina alābhaina saucati kilamati paridaivati, urattāḷiṃ kandati, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai – ‘bhikkhu icchau viharati lābhāya, uṭṭhahati  ghaṭati vāyamati lābhāya, na ca lābhī, saucī ca paridaivī ca, cutau ca saddhammā’’’|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pavivittassa viharatau nirāyattavuttinau icchā uppaj‍jati lābhāya| sau uṭṭhahati ghaṭati vāyamati lābhāya| tassa uṭṭhahatau ghaṭatau vāyamatau lābhāya lābhau uppaj‍jati| sau taina lābhaina maj‍jati pamaj‍jati pamādamāpaj‍jati| ayaṃ  vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai – ‘bhikkhu icchau viharati lābhāya, uṭṭhahati ghaṭati vāyamati lābhāya, lābhī ca madī ca pamādī ca, cutau ca saddhammā’’’|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pavivittassa viharatau nirāyattavuttinau icchā uppaj‍jati lābhāya| sau na uṭṭhahati na ghaṭati na vāyamati lābhāya| tassa anuṭṭhahatau aghaṭatau  avāyamatau lābhāya lābhau nuppaj‍jati| sau taina alābhaina saucati, kilamati, paridaivati, urattāḷiṃ kandati, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai – ‘bhikkhu icchau viharati lābhāya, na uṭṭhahati na ghaṭati na vāyamati lābhāya, na ca lābhī, saucī ca paridaivī ca, cutau ca saddhammā’’’|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pavivittassa viharatau nirāyattavuttinau icchā uppaj‍jati lābhāya| sau na uṭṭhahati, na ghaṭati, na vāyamati lābhāya| tassa anuṭṭhahatau, aghaṭatau, avāyamatau lābhāya lābhau uppaj‍jati| sau taina lābhaina maj‍jati, pamaj‍jati, pamādamāpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai – ‘bhikkhu icchau viharati lābhāya, na uṭṭhahati na ghaṭati na vāyamati lābhāya, lābhī ca madī ca, pamādī ca, cutau ca saddhammā’’’|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pavivittassa viharatau nirāyattavuttinau icchā uppaj‍jati lābhāya| sau uṭṭhahati ghaṭati vāyamati lābhāya| tassa uṭṭhahatau ghaṭatau vāyamatau  lābhāya lābhau nuppaj‍jati| sau taina alābhaina na saucati na kilamati na paridaivati, na urattāḷiṃ kandati, na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai – ‘bhikkhu icchau viharati lābhāya, uṭṭhahati ghaṭati vāyamati lābhāya, na  ca lābhī, na ca saucī na ca paridaivī, ac‍cutau ca saddhammā’’’|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pavivittassa viharatau nirāyattavuttinau icchā uppaj‍jati lābhāya| sau uṭṭhahati ghaṭati vāyamati lābhāya| tassa  uṭṭhahatau ghaṭatau vāyamatau lābhāya lābhau uppaj‍jati| sau taina lābhaina na maj‍jati, na pamaj‍jati, na pamādamāpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai – ‘bhikkhu icchau viharati lābhāya, uṭṭhahati ghaṭati vāyamati lābhāya, lābhī ca, na ca madī na ca pamādī, ac‍cutau ca saddhammā’’’|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pavivittassa viharatau nirāyattavuttinau icchā uppaj‍jati lābhāya| sau na uṭṭhahati, na ghaṭati, na vāyamati lābhāya| tassa anuṭṭhahatau, aghaṭatau, avāyamatau lābhāya lābhau nuppaj‍jati| sau taina alābhaina na saucati, na kilamati, na paridaivati, na urattāḷiṃ kandati, na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai – ‘bhikkhu icchau viharati lābhāya, na uṭṭhahati, na ghaṭati, na vāyamati lābhāya, na ca lābhī, na ca saucī na ca paridaivī, ac‍cutau ca saddhammā’’’|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pavivittassa viharatau nirāyattavuttinau icchā uppaj‍jati lābhāya| sau na uṭṭhahati, na ghaṭati, na vāyamati lābhāya| tassa anuṭṭhahatau aghaṭatau avāyamatau lābhāya lābhau uppaj‍jati| sau taina lābhaina na maj‍jati, na pamaj‍jati, na pamādamāpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai – ‘bhikkhu icchau viharati lābhāya, na uṭṭhahati, na ghaṭati, na vāyamati lābhāya, lābhī ca, na ca madī na ca pamādī, ac‍cutau ca saddhammā ’| imai khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. alaṃsuttaṃ

62. ‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ attanau alaṃ paraisaṃ| katamaihi chahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu khippanisanti ca hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu; sutānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ dhāraṇajātikau dhārakajātikau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) a॰ ni॰ 8.78 hauti; dhātānañ‍ca dhatānañ‍ca (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhitā atthūpavarikkhī (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) hauti; atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau ca hauti; kalyāṇavācau ca hauti kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau, pauriyā vācāya saman‍nāgatau vissaṭṭhāya anailagaḷāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā; sandassakau ca hauti samādapakau samādāpakau (?) samuttaijakau  sampahaṃsakau sabrahmacārīnaṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ attanau alaṃ paraisaṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ attanau alaṃ paraisaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na haiva khau khippanisanti ca hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu; sutānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ dhāraṇajātikau hauti; dhātānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhitā hauti; atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau ca hauti; kalyāṇavācau ca hauti…pai॰… atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā; sandassakau ca hauti samādapakau samuttaijakau sampahaṃsakau sabrahmacārīnaṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ attanau alaṃ paraisaṃ|

‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ attanau nālaṃ paraisaṃ| katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu khippanisanti ca hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu; sutānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ dhāraṇajātikau hauti; dhātānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhitā hauti; atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau ca hauti; nau ca kalyāṇavācau hauti kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau, pauriyā vācāya saman‍nāgatau vissaṭṭhāya anailagaḷāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā; nau ca sandassakau hauti samādapakau  samuttaijakau sampahaṃsakau sabrahmacārīnaṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ attanau nālaṃ paraisaṃ|

‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ paraisaṃ nālaṃ attanau| katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu khippanisanti ca hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu; sutānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ dhāraṇajātikau hauti; nau ca dhātānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhitā hauti; na ca atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau hauti; kalyāṇavācau ca hauti kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau…pai॰… atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā; sandassakau ca hauti…pai॰… sabrahmacārīnaṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ paraisaṃ, nālaṃ attanau|

‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ attanau nālaṃ paraisaṃ| katamaihi tīhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na haiva khau khippanisanti ca hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu; sutānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ dhāraṇajātikau hauti; dhātānañ‍ca  dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhitā hauti; atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau ca hauti; nau ca kalyāṇavācau hauti kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau, pauriyā vācāya saman‍nāgatau vissaṭṭhāya anailagaḷāya  atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā; nau ca sandassakau hauti samādapakau samuttaijakau sampahaṃsakau sabrahmacārīnaṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ attanau, nālaṃ paraisaṃ|

‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ paraisaṃ, nālaṃ attanau| katamaihi tīhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na haiva khau khippanisanti ca hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu; sutānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ dhāraṇajātikau hauti; nau ca dhātānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhitā hauti; nau ca atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau hauti; kalyāṇavācau ca hauti…pai॰… atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā; sandassakau ca hauti samādapakau samuttaijakau sampahaṃsakau sabrahmacārīnaṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ paraisaṃ, nālaṃ attanau|

‘‘dvīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ attanau, nālaṃ paraisaṃ| katamaihi dvīhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na haiva khau khippanisanti ca hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu; nau ca  sutānaṃ dhammānaṃ dhāraṇajātikau hauti; dhātānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhitā hauti; atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau ca hauti; nau ca kalyāṇavācau hauti…pai॰… atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā; nau ca sandassakau hauti…pai॰… sabrahmacārīnaṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, dvīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ attanau, nālaṃ paraisaṃ|

‘‘dvīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ paraisaṃ, nālaṃ attanau| katamaihi dvīhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na haiva khau khippanisanti ca hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu; nau ca sutānaṃ dhammānaṃ dhāraṇajātikau hauti; nau ca dhātānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhitā  hauti; nau ca atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau hauti; kalyāṇavācau ca hauti kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau, pauriyā vācāya saman‍nāgatau vissaṭṭhāya anailagaḷāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā; sandassakau ca hauti samādapakau samuttaijakau sampahaṃsakau sabrahmacārīnaṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, dvīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ paraisaṃ, nālaṃ attanau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. saṃkhittasuttaṃ

63. atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu, yamahaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ sutvā aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti| ‘‘aivamaivaṃ panidhaikac‍cai maughapurisā mamañ‍ñaiva ajjhaisanti| dhammai ca bhāsitai mamañ‍ñaiva anubandhitabbaṃ mañ‍ñantī’’ti| ‘‘daisaitu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ, daisaitu sugatau saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ| appaiva nāmāhaṃ bhagavatau bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānaiyyaṃ, appaiva nāmāhaṃ bhagavatau bhāsitassa dāyādau assa’’nti| ‘‘tasmātiha  tai, bhikkhu aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘ajjhattaṃ mai cittaṃ ṭhitaṃ bhavissati susaṇṭhitaṃ, na ca uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassantī’ti| aivañhi tai, bhikkhu, sikkhitabbaṃ’’|

‘‘yatau khau tai, bhikkhu, ajjhattaṃ cittaṃ ṭhitaṃ hauti susaṇṭhitaṃ, na ca uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti, tatau tai, bhikkhu, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘maittā  mai caitauvimutti bhāvitā bhavissati bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā’ti| aivañhi tai, bhikkhu, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘yatau khau tai, bhikkhu, ayaṃ samādhi aivaṃ bhāvitau hauti bahulīkatau, tatau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, imaṃ samādhiṃ savitak‍kampi savicāraṃ savitak‍kasavicārampi (ka॰) bhāvaiyyāsi, avitak‍kampi vicāramattaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattampi (ka॰) visuddhi॰ 1.271 passitabbaṃ bhāvaiyyāsi, avitak‍kampi avicāraṃ avitak‍kaavicārampi (ka॰) bhāvaiyyāsi, sappītikampi bhāvaiyyāsi, nippītikampi bhāvaiyyāsi, sātasahagatampi bhāvaiyyāsi, upaikkhāsahagatampi bhāvaiyyāsi|

‘‘yatau khau, tai bhikkhu, ayaṃ samādhi aivaṃ bhāvitau hauti subhāvitau, tatau tai, bhikkhu, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘karuṇā mai caitauvimutti… muditā mai caitauvimutti… upaikkhā mai caitauvimutti bhāvitā bhavissati  bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā’ti| aivañhi tai, bhikkhu, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘yatau khau tai, bhikkhu, ayaṃ samādhi aivaṃ bhāvitau hauti subhāvitau, tatau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, imaṃ samādhiṃ savitak‍kasavicārampi bhāvaiyyāsi, avitak‍kavicāramattampi bhāvaiyyāsi, avitak‍kaavicārampi bhāvaiyyāsi, sappītikampi bhāvaiyyāsi, nippītikampi bhāvaiyyāsi, sātasahagatampi bhāvaiyyāsi, upaikkhāsahagatampi bhāvaiyyāsi|

‘‘yatau khau tai, bhikkhu, ayaṃ samādhi aivaṃ bhāvitau hauti subhāvitau, tatau tai, bhikkhu, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘kāyai kāyānupassī viharissāmi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassa’nti| aivañhi tai, bhikkhu, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘yatau khau tai, bhikkhu, ayaṃ samādhi aivaṃ bhāvitau hauti bahulīkatau, tatau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, imaṃ samādhiṃ savitak‍kasavicārampi bhāvaiyyāsi, avitak‍kavicāramattampi  bhāvaiyyāsi, avitak‍kaavicārampi bhāvaiyyāsi, sappītikampi bhāvaiyyāsi, nippītikampi bhāvaiyyāsi, sātasahagatampi bhāvaiyyāsi, upaikkhāsahagatampi bhāvaiyyāsi|

‘‘yatau khau tai, bhikkhu, ayaṃ samādhi aivaṃ bhāvitau hauti subhāvitau, tatau tai, bhikkhu, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharissāmi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassa’nti; cittai cittānupassī viharissāmi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassa’nti; dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharissāmi ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassa’nti| aivañhi tai, bhikkhu, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘yatau khau tai, bhikkhu, ayaṃ samādhi aivaṃ bhāvitau hauti bahulīkatau, tatau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, imaṃ samādhiṃ savitak‍kasavicārampi bhāvaiyyāsi, avitak‍kavicāramattampi bhāvaiyyāsi, avitak‍kaavicārampi bhāvaiyyāsi, sappītikampi bhāvaiyyāsi, nippītikampi bhāvaiyyāsi, sātasahagatampi bhāvaiyyāsi, upaikkhāsahagatampi bhāvaiyyāsi|

‘‘yatau khau tai, bhikkhu, ayaṃ samādhi aivaṃ bhāvitau hauti subhāvitau, tatau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, yaina yainaiva gagghasi phāsuṃyaiva gagghasi, yattha yattha ṭhassasi phāsuṃyaiva ṭhassasi, yattha yattha nisīdissasi phāsuṃyaiva nisīdissasi, yattha yattha saiyyaṃ kappaissasi  phāsuṃyaiva saiyyaṃ kappaissasī’’ti|

atha  khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatā iminā auvādaina auvaditau uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau sau bhikkhu aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘‘khīṇā  jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca pana sau bhikkhu arahataṃ ahausīti| tatiyaṃ|

4. gayāsīsasuttaṃ

64. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā gayāyaṃ viharati gayāsīsai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi…pai॰… ‘‘pubbāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sambaudhā anabhisambuddhau baudhisattauva samānau aubhāsañ‍ñaiva khau sañ‍jānāmi, nau ca rūpāni passāmi’’|

‘‘tassa  mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘sacai khau ahaṃ aubhāsañ‍caiva sañ‍jānaiyyaṃ rūpāni ca passaiyyaṃ; aivaṃ mai idaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ parisuddhataraṃ assā’’’ti|

‘‘sau khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, aparaina samayaina appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau aubhāsañ‍caiva sañ‍jānāmi, rūpāni ca passāmi; nau ca khau tāhi daivatāhi saddhiṃ santiṭṭhāmi sal‍lapāmi sākacchaṃ samāpaj‍jāmi|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘sacai khau ahaṃ aubhāsañ‍caiva sañ‍jānaiyyaṃ, rūpāni ca passaiyyaṃ, tāhi ca daivatāhi saddhiṃ santiṭṭhaiyyaṃ sal‍lapaiyyaṃ  sākacchaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyyaṃ; aivaṃ mai idaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ parisuddhataraṃ assā’’’ti|

‘‘sau khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, aparaina samayaina appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau aubhāsañ‍caiva sañ‍jānāmi, rūpāni ca passāmi, tāhi ca daivatāhi saddhiṃ santiṭṭhāmi sal‍lapāmi sākacchaṃ samāpaj‍jāmi; nau ca khau tā daivatā jānāmi – imā daivatā amukamhā vā amukamhā vā daivanikāyāti|

‘‘tassa  mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘sacai khau ahaṃ aubhāsañ‍caiva sañ‍jānaiyyaṃ, rūpāni ca passaiyyaṃ, tāhi ca daivatāhi saddhiṃ santiṭṭhaiyyaṃ sal‍lapaiyyaṃ sākacchaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyyaṃ, tā ca daivatā jānaiyyaṃ – imā daivatā amukamhā vā amukamhā vā daivanikāyā’ti; aivaṃ mai idaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ parisuddhataraṃ assā’’’ti|

‘‘sau khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, aparaina samayaina appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau aubhāsañ‍caiva sañ‍jānāmi, rūpāni ca passāmi, tāhi ca daivatāhi saddhiṃ santiṭṭhāmi sal‍lapāmi sākacchaṃ samāpaj‍jāmi, tā ca daivatā jānāmi – ‘imā daivatā amukamhā vā amukamhā vā daivanikāyā’ti; nau ca khau tā daivatā jānāmi – ‘imā daivatā imassa kammassa vipākaina itau cutā tattha upapan‍nā’ti…pai॰… tā ca daivatā jānāmi – ‘imā daivatā imassa kammassa vipākaina itau cutā tattha upapan‍nā’ti; nau ca khau tā daivatā jānāmi – ‘imā daivatā imassa kammassa vipākaina aivamāhārā aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidiniyau’ti  …pai॰… tā ca daivatā jānāmi – ‘imā daivatā imassa kammassa vipākaina aivamāhārā aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidiniyau’ti; nau ca khau tā daivatā jānāmi – ‘imā daivatā aivaṃdīghāyukā  aivaṃciraṭṭhitikā’ti…pai॰… tā ca daivatā jānāmi – ‘imā daivatā aivaṃdīghāyukā aivaṃciraṭṭhitikā’ti; nau ca khau tā daivatā jānāmi yadi vā mai imāhi daivatāhi saddhiṃ san‍nivutthapubbaṃ yadi vā na san‍nivutthapubbanti|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘sacai khau ahaṃ aubhāsañ‍caiva sañ‍jānaiyyaṃ, rūpāni ca passaiyyaṃ, tāhi ca daivatāhi saddhiṃ santiṭṭhaiyyaṃ sal‍lapaiyyaṃ sākacchaṃ samāpaj‍jaiyyaṃ, tā ca daivatā jānaiyyaṃ – ‘imā daivatā amukamhā vā amukamhā vā daivanikāyā’ti, tā ca daivatā jānaiyyaṃ – ‘imā daivatā imassa kammassa vipākaina itau cutā tattha upapan‍nā’ti, tā ca daivatā jānaiyyaṃ – ‘imā daivatā aivamāhārā aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidiniyau’ti, tā ca daivatā jānaiyyaṃ – ‘imā daivatā aivaṃdīghāyukā aivaṃciraṭṭhitikā’ti, tā ca daivatā jānaiyyaṃ yadi vā mai imāhi daivatāhi saddhiṃ san‍nivutthapubbaṃ yadi vā na san‍nivutthapubbanti; aivaṃ mai idaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ parisuddhataraṃ assā’’’ti|

‘‘sau  khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, aparaina samayaina appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau aubhāsañ‍caiva sañ‍jānāmi, rūpāni ca passāmi, tāhi ca daivatāhi saddhiṃ santiṭṭhāmi sal‍lapāmi sākacchaṃ samāpaj‍jāmi, tā ca daivatā jānāmi – ‘imā daivatā amukamhā vā amukamhā vā daivanikāyā’ti, tā ca daivatā jānāmi – ‘imā daivatā imassa kammassa vipākaina itau cutā tattha upapan‍nā’ti, tā ca daivatā jānāmi – ‘imā daivatā aivamāhārā aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidiniyau’ti, tā ca daivatā jānāmi – ‘imā daivatā aivaṃdīghāyukā aivaṃciraṭṭhitikā’ti, tā ca daivatā jānāmi yadi vā mai daivatāhi saddhiṃ san‍nivutthapubbaṃ yadi vā na san‍nivutthapubbanti|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca mai, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ aṭṭhaparivaṭṭaṃ adhidaivañāṇadassanaṃ na suvisuddhaṃ ahausi, naiva tāvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ‘sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau’ti abhisambuddhau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| yatau ca khau mai, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ aṭṭhaparivaṭṭaṃ adhidaivañāṇadassanaṃ suvisuddhaṃ ahausi, athāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ‘sadaivakai  laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau’ti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ; ñāṇañ‍ca pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi; akuppā mai caitauvimutti vimutti (ka॰ sī॰ ka॰); ayamantimā jāti natthi dāni punabbhavau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. abhibhāyatanasuttaṃ

65. dī॰ ni॰ 3.338, 358; a॰ ni॰ 10.29 ‘‘aṭṭhimāni, bhikkhavai, abhibhāyatanāni| katamāni aṭṭha? ajjhattaṃ rūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati parittāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni| ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti, aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ paṭhamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ rūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati appamāṇāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni| ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti, aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ dutiyaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ  arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati parittāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni| ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti, aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ tatiyaṃ  abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati appamāṇāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni| ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti, aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ catutthaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati nīlāni nīlavaṇṇāni nīlanidassanāni nīlanibhāsāni| ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti, aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ pañ‍camaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati pītāni pītavaṇṇāni pītanidassanāni pītanibhāsāni| ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti, aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ  arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati lauhitakāni lauhitakavaṇṇāni lauhitakanidassanāni lauhitakanibhāsāni| ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti, aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ sattamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati audātāni audātavaṇṇāni audātanidassanāni audātanibhāsāni| ‘tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmī’ti, aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| idaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha abhibhāyatanānī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. vimaukkhasuttaṃ

66. ‘‘aṭṭhimai, bhikkhavai, vimaukkhā| katamai aṭṭha? rūpī rūpāni passati| ayaṃ paṭhamau vimaukkhau|

‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī, bahiddhā arūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) dī॰ ni॰ 2.129; dī॰ ni॰ 3.338, 358; a॰ ni॰ 8.119; ma॰ ni॰ 2.248 passitabbaṃ rūpāni passati| ayaṃ dutiyau vimaukkhau|

‘‘subhantaiva adhimuttau hauti| ayaṃ tatiyau vimaukkhau|

‘‘sabbasau  rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau  ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ catutthau vimaukkhau|

‘‘sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ pañ‍camau vimaukkhau|

‘‘sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ chaṭṭhau vimaukkhau|

‘‘sabbasau  ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ sattamau vimaukkhau|

‘‘sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ aṭṭhamau vimaukkhau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha vimaukkhā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. anariyavauhārasuttaṃ

67. ‘‘aṭṭhimai, bhikkhavai, anariyavauhārā| katamai aṭṭha? adiṭṭhai diṭṭhavāditā, asutai sutavāditā, amutai mutavāditā, aviñ‍ñātai viñ‍ñātavāditā, diṭṭhai adiṭṭhavāditā, sutai asutavāditā, mutai amutavāditā, viñ‍ñātai aviñ‍ñātavāditā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha anariyavauhārā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. ariyavauhārasuttaṃ

68. ‘‘aṭṭhimai, bhikkhavai, ariyavauhārā| katamai aṭṭha? adiṭṭhai adiṭṭhavāditā, asutai asutavāditā, amutai amutavāditā, aviñ‍ñātai aviñ‍ñātavāditā, diṭṭhai diṭṭhavāditā, sutai sutavāditā, mutai mutavāditā, viñ‍ñātai viñ‍ñātavāditā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha ariyavauhārā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. parisāsuttaṃ

69. ‘‘aṭṭhimā, bhikkhavai, parisā| katamā aṭṭha? khattiyaparisā, brāhmaṇaparisā, gahapatiparisā, samaṇaparisā, cātumahārājikaparisā, tāvatiṃsaparisā, māraparisā, brahmaparisā| abhijānāmi khau panāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, anaikasataṃ  khattiyaparisaṃ upasaṅkamitā| tatrapi mayā san‍nisin‍napubbañ‍caiva sal‍lapitapubbañ‍ca sākacchā ca samāpan‍napubbā| tattha yādisakau taisaṃ vaṇṇau hauti tādisakau mayhaṃ vaṇṇau hauti, yādisakau taisaṃ sarau hauti tādisakau mayhaṃ sarau hauti| dhammiyā ca kathāya sandassaimi samādapaimi samuttaijaimi sampahaṃsaimi| bhāsamānañ‍ca maṃ na jānanti – ‘kau nu khau ayaṃ bhāsati daivau vā manussau vā’ti| dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā antaradhāyāmi| antarahitañ‍ca maṃ na jānanti – ‘kau nu khau ayaṃ antarahitau daivau vā manussau vā’’’ti|

‘‘abhijānāmi  khau panāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, anaikasataṃ brāhmaṇaparisaṃ…pai॰… gahapatiparisaṃ… samaṇaparisaṃ… cātumahārājikaparisaṃ… tāvatiṃsaparisaṃ… māraparisaṃ… brahmaparisaṃ upasaṅkamitā| tatrapi mayā san‍nisin‍napubbañ‍caiva sal‍lapitapubbañ‍ca sākacchā ca samāpan‍napubbā| tattha yādisakau taisaṃ vaṇṇau hauti tādisakau mayhaṃ vaṇṇau hauti, yādisakau taisaṃ sarau hauti tādisakau mayhaṃ sarau hauti| dhammiyā ca kathāya sandassaimi samādapaimi samuttaijaimi sampahaṃsaimi| bhāsamānañ‍ca maṃ na jānanti – ‘kau nu khau ayaṃ bhāsati daivau vā manussau vā’ti| dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā antaradhāyāmi| antarahitañ‍ca maṃ na jānanti – ‘kau nu  khau ayaṃ antarahitau daivau vā manussau vā’ti| imā khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha parisā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. bhūmicālasuttaṃ

70. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya vaisāliṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| vaisāliyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘gaṇhāhi, ānanda, nisīdanaṃ| yaina cāpālaṃ caitiyaṃ pāvālacaitiyaṃ (syā॰), cāpālacaitiyaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) tainupasaṅkamissāma divāvihārāyā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau paṭissutvā nisīdanaṃ ādāya bhagavantaṃ piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhi|
