atha  khau bhagavā yaina cāpālaṃ caitiyaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘ramaṇīyā, ānanda, vaisālī, ramaṇīyaṃ udainaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ gautamakaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ sattambaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ bahuputtakaṃ caitiyaṃ; ramaṇīyaṃ sārandadaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ cāpālaṃ caitiyaṃ| yassa kassaci, ānanda, cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, ākaṅkhamānau sau, ānanda, kappaṃ vā tiṭṭhaiyya kappāvasaisaṃ vā| tathāgatassa khau, ānanda, cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā| ākaṅkhamānau, ānanda, tathāgatau kappaṃ vā tiṭṭhaiyya kappāvasaisaṃ vā’’ti| aivampi khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatā auḷārikai nimittai kayiramānai auḷārikai  aubhāsai kayiramānai nāsakkhi paṭivijjhituṃ; na bhagavantaṃ yāci – ‘‘tiṭṭhatu, bhantai, bhagavā kappaṃ, tiṭṭhatu sugatau kappaṃ bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussāna’’nti, yathā taṃ māraina pariyuṭṭhitacittau|

dutiyampi khau bhagavā…pai॰… tatiyampi khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘ramaṇīyā, ānanda, vaisālī, ramaṇīyaṃ udainaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ gautamakaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ sattambaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ bahuputtakaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ sārandadaṃ caitiyaṃ, ramaṇīyaṃ cāpālaṃ caitiyaṃ| yassa kassaci, ānanda, cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, ākaṅkhamānau sau, ānanda, kappaṃ vā tiṭṭhaiyya kappāvasaisaṃ vā| tathāgatassa khau, ānanda, cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā…pai॰… ākaṅkhamānau, ānanda, tathāgatau kappaṃ vā tiṭṭhaiyya kappāvasaisaṃ vā’’ti| aivampi khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatā  auḷārikai nimittai kayiramānai auḷārikai aubhāsai kayiramānai nāsakkhi paṭivijjhituṃ; na bhagavantaṃ yāci – ‘‘tiṭṭhatu, bhantai, bhagavā kappaṃ, tiṭṭhatu sugatau kappaṃ bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussāna’’nti, yathā taṃ māraina pariyuṭṭhitacittau|

atha  khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘gaccha tvaṃ gaccha khau tvaṃ (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.822) udā॰ 51 passitabbaṃ, ānanda, yassa dāni kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau paṭissutvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā bhagavatau avidūrai añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisīdi| atha khau mārau pāpimā acirapak‍kantai āyasmantai ānandai bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca –

‘‘parinibbātu dāni, bhantai, bhagavā, parinibbātu sugatau| parinibbānakālau dāni, bhantai, bhagavatau| bhāsitā khau panaisā, bhantai, bhagavatā vācā – ‘na tāvāhaṃ, pāpima, parinibbāyissāmi yāva mai bhikkhū na sāvakā bhavissanti viyattā vinītā visāradā pattayaugakkhaimā idaṃ padaṃ dī॰ ni॰ 2.168 ca saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.822 ca na dissati bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā sāmīcippaṭipan‍nā anudhammacārinau, sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ uggahaitvā ācikkhissanti daisaissanti pañ‍ñapaissanti paṭṭhapaissanti vivarissanti vibhajissanti uttānīkarissanti uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaissantī’ti| aitarahi, bhantai, bhikkhū bhagavatau sāvakā viyattā vinītā visāradā pattayaugakkhaimā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā sāmīcippaṭipan‍nā anudhammacārinau, sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ uggahaitvā ācikkhanti daisainti pañ‍ñapainti paṭṭhapainti vivaranti vibhajanti uttānīkaraunti uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisainti|

‘‘parinibbātu dāni, bhantai, bhagavā, parinibbātu sugatau| parinibbānakālau dāni, bhantai, bhagavatau| bhāsitā khau panaisā, bhantai, bhagavatā vācā – ‘na tāvāhaṃ, pāpima, parinibbāyissāmi yāva mai bhikkhuniyau na sāvikā bhavissanti…pai॰… yāva mai upāsakā na sāvakā  bhavissanti…pai॰… yāva mai upāsikā na sāvikā bhavissanti viyattā vinītā visāradā pattayaugakkhaimā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā sāmīcippaṭipan‍nā anudhammacāriniyau, sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ uggahaitvā  ācikkhissanti daisaissanti pañ‍ñapaissanti paṭṭhapaissanti vivarissanti vibhajissanti uttānīkarissanti, uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaissantī’ti| aitarahi, bhantai, upāsikā bhagavatau sāvikā viyattā vinītā visāradā pattayaugakkhaimā bahussutā  dhammadharā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā sāmīcippaṭipan‍nā anudhammacāriniyau, sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ uggahaitvā ācikkhanti daisainti pañ‍ñapainti paṭṭhapainti vivaranti vibhajanti uttānīkaraunti, uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisainti|

‘‘parinibbātu dāni, bhantai, bhagavā, parinibbātu sugatau| parinibbānakālau dāni, bhantai, bhagavatau| bhāsitā khau panaisā, bhantai, bhagavatā vācā – ‘na tāvāhaṃ, pāpima, parinibbāyissāmi yāva mai idaṃ brahmacariyaṃ na iddhañ‍caiva bhavissati phītañ‍ca vitthārikaṃ bāhujañ‍ñaṃ  puthubhūtaṃ, yāva daivamanussaihi suppakāsita’nti| aitarahi, bhantai, bhagavatau brahmacariyaṃ iddhañ‍caiva phītañ‍ca vitthārikaṃ bāhujañ‍ñaṃ puthubhūtaṃ, yāva daivamanussaihi suppakāsitaṃ|

‘‘parinibbātu dāni, bhantai, bhagavā, parinibbātu sugatau| parinibbānakālau dāni, bhantai, bhagavatau’’ti| ‘‘appaussuk‍kau tvaṃ, pāpima, hauhi| naciraṃ tathāgatassa parinibbānaṃ bhavissati| itau tiṇṇaṃ māsānaṃ ac‍cayaina tathāgatau parinibbāyissatī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā cāpālai caitiyai satau sampajānau āyusaṅkhāraṃ aussaji| aussaṭṭhai ca bhagavatā āyusaṅkhārai  mahābhūmicālau ahausi bhiṃsanakau salaumahaṃsau, daivadundubhiyau ca phaliṃsu| atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘tulamatulañ‍ca  sambhavaṃ, bhavasaṅkhāramavassaji muni|

ajjhattaratau samāhitau, abhindi kavacamivattasambhava’’nti||

atha khau āyasmatau ānandassa aitadahausi – ‘‘mahā vatāyaṃ bhūmicālau; sumahā vatāyaṃ bhūmicālau bhiṃsanakau salaumahaṃsau, daivadundubhiyau ca phaliṃsu| kau nu khau haitu, kau pac‍cayau mahatau bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāyā’’ti?

atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mahā vatāyaṃ, bhantai, bhūmicālau; sumahā vatāyaṃ, bhantai, bhūmicālau bhiṃsanakau salaumahaṃsau, daivadundubhiyau ca phaliṃsu| kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau mahatau bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāyā’’ti?

‘‘aṭṭhimai, ānanda, haitū, aṭṭha pac‍cayā mahatau bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya| katamai aṭṭha? ayaṃ, ānanda, mahāpathavī udakai patiṭṭhitā; udakaṃ vātai patiṭṭhitaṃ; vātau ākāsaṭṭhau hauti| sau, ānanda, samayau yaṃ mahāvātā vāyanti; mahāvātā vāyantā udakaṃ kampainti; udakaṃ  kampitaṃ pathaviṃ kampaiti| ayaṃ, ānanda, paṭhamau haitu, paṭhamau pac‍cayau mahatau bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau  vā iddhimā caitauvasippattau daivatā vā mahiddhikā mahānubhāvā| tassa parittā pathavīsañ‍ñā bhāvitā hauti, appamāṇā āpausañ‍ñā| sau imaṃ pathaviṃ kampaiti saṅkampaiti sampakampaiti sampavaidhaiti| ayaṃ, ānanda, dutiyau haitu, dutiyau pac‍cayau mahatau bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, yadā baudhisattau tusitā kāyā cavitvā satau sampajānau mātukucchiṃ auk‍kamati, tadāyaṃ pathavī  kampati saṅkampati sampakampati sampavaidhati| ayaṃ, ānanda, tatiyau haitu; tatiyau pac‍cayau mahatau bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, yadā baudhisattau satau sampajānau mātukucchismā nikkhamati, tadāyaṃ pathavī kampati saṅkampati sampakampati sampavaidhati| ayaṃ, ānanda, catutthau haitu, catutthau pac‍cayau mahatau bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, yadā tathāgatau anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambujjhati, tadāyaṃ pathavī kampati saṅkampati sampakampati sampavaidhati| ayaṃ, ānanda, pañ‍camau haitu, pañ‍camau pac‍cayau mahatau bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, yadā tathāgatau anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti, tadāyaṃ pathavī kampati saṅkampati sampakampati sampavaidhati| ayaṃ, ānanda, chaṭṭhau haitu, chaṭṭhau pac‍cayau mahatau bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, ānanda, yadā tathāgatau satau sampajānau āyusaṅkhāraṃ aussaj‍jati, tadāyaṃ pathavī kampati saṅkampati sampakampati sampavaidhati| ayaṃ, ānanda, sattamau haitu, sattamau pac‍cayau mahatau bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, ānanda, yadā tathāgatau anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā  parinibbāyati, tadāyaṃ pathavī kampati saṅkampati sampakampati sampavaidhati| ayaṃ, ānanda, aṭṭhamau haitu, aṭṭhamau pac‍cayau mahatau bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāya| imai khau, ānanda, aṭṭha haitū, aṭṭha pac‍cayā mahatau bhūmicālassa pātubhāvāyā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

bhūmicālavaggau dutiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

icchā  alañ‍ca saṃkhittaṃ, gayā abhibhunā saha|

vimaukkhau dvai ca vauhārā, parisā bhūmicālaināti||


(8) 3. yamakavaggau

1. paṭhamasaddhāsuttaṃ

71. ‘‘saddhau  ca saddhau (syā॰) aitthaiva| a॰ ni॰ 9.4, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu hauti, nau ca nau (syā॰) aivamuparipi ‘‘nau’’tvaiva dissati sīlavā| aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina aparipūrau hauti| taina taṃ aṅgaṃ paripūraitabbaṃ – ‘kintāhaṃ saddhau ca assaṃ sīlavā cā’ti| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhau ca hauti sīlavā ca, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina paripūrau hauti|

‘‘saddhau ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu hauti sīlavā ca, nau ca bahussutau| aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina aparipūrau hauti| taina taṃ aṅgaṃ paripūraitabbaṃ – ‘kintāhaṃ saddhau ca assaṃ, sīlavā ca, bahussutau cā’ti| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhau ca hauti sīlavā ca bahussutau ca, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina paripūrau hauti|

‘‘saddhau  ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu hauti sīlavā ca bahussutau ca, nau ca dhammakathikau…pai॰… dhammakathikau ca, nau ca parisāvacarau…pai॰… parisāvacarau ca, nau ca visāradau parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiti…pai॰… visāradau ca parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, nau ca catun‍naṃ  jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī…pai॰… catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, nau ca āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati; aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina aparipūrau hauti| taina taṃ aṅgaṃ paripūraitabbaṃ  – ‘kintāhaṃ saddhau ca assaṃ, sīlavā ca, bahussutau ca, dhammakathikau ca, parisāvacarau ca, visāradau ca parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ, catun‍nañ‍ca jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī assaṃ akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, āsavānañ‍ca khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’’’nti|

‘‘yatau  ca khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhau ca hauti, sīlavā ca, bahussutau ca, dhammakathikau ca, parisāvacarau ca, visāradau ca parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, catun‍nañ‍ca jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, āsavānañ‍ca khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati; aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina paripūrau hauti| imaihi, khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu samantapāsādikau ca hauti sabbākāraparipūrau cā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyasaddhāsuttaṃ

72. ‘‘saddhau ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu hauti, nau ca sīlavā| aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina  aparipūrau hauti| taina taṃ aṅgaṃ paripūraitabbaṃ – ‘kintāhaṃ saddhau ca assaṃ sīlavā cā’ti| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhau ca hauti sīlavā ca, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina paripūrau hauti|

‘‘saddhau ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu hauti sīlavā ca, nau ca bahussutau…pai॰… bahussutau ca, nau ca dhammakathikau…pai॰… dhammakathikau ca, nau ca parisāvacarau…pai॰… parisāvacarau ca, nau ca visāradau parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiti  …pai॰…  visāradau ca parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, nau ca yai tai santā vimaukkhā atik‍kamma rūpai āruppā tai kāyaina phusitvā viharati…pai॰… yai tai santā vimaukkhā atik‍kamma rūpai āruppā tai kāyaina phusitvā viharati, nau ca āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati; aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina aparipūrau hauti| taina taṃ aṅgaṃ paripūraitabbaṃ – ‘kintāhaṃ saddhau ca assaṃ, sīlavā ca, bahussutau ca, dhammakathikau ca, parisāvacarau ca, visāradau ca parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ, yai tai santā vimaukkhā atik‍kamma rūpai āruppā tai kāyaina phusitvā viharaiyyaṃ, āsavānañ‍ca khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’’’nti|

‘‘yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saddhau ca hauti, sīlavā ca, bahussutau ca, dhammakathikau ca, parisāvacarau ca, visāradau ca parisāya dhammaṃ daisaiti| yai tai santā vimaukkhā  atik‍kamma rūpai āruppā tai ca kāyaina phusitvā viharati, āsavānañ‍ca khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati; aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina paripūrau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu  samantapāsādikau ca hauti sabbākāraparipūrau cā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. paṭhamamaraṇassatisuttaṃ

73. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā nātikai nādikai (sī॰ syā॰), nāṭikai (pī॰) a॰ ni॰ 6.19 viharati giñ‍jakāvasathai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘maraṇassati, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā| bhāvaitha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, maraṇassati’’nti|

aivaṃ vuttai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahaṃ khau, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhāvaisi maraṇassati’’nti? ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ rattindivaṃ jīvaiyyaṃ, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu bahuṃ (sī॰ pī॰) vata mai kataṃ assā’ti| aivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti|

añ‍ñataraupi khau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahampi khau, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhāvaisi maraṇassati’’nti? ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ divasaṃ jīvaiyyaṃ, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’ti| aivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti|

añ‍ñataraupi khau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahampi khau, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhāvaisi maraṇassati’’nti? ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ upaḍḍhadivasaṃ jīvaiyyaṃ, bhagavatau  sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’ti| aivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti|

añ‍ñataraupi khau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahampi khau, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhāvaisi maraṇassati’’nti? ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīvaiyyaṃ yadantaraṃ aikapiṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jāmi, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’ti| aivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti|

añ‍ñataraupi khau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahampi khau, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhāvaisi maraṇassati’’nti? ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīvaiyyaṃ yadantaraṃ upaḍḍhapiṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jāmi, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’ti| aivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti|

añ‍ñataraupi khau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahampi khau, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhāvaisi maraṇassati’’nti? ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīvaiyyaṃ yadantaraṃ cattārau pañ‍ca ālaupai saṅkhāditvā saṅkharitvā (ka॰) ajjhauharāmi, bhagavatau  sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’ti| aivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti|

añ‍ñataraupi khau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahampi khau, bhantai, bhāvaimi  maraṇassati’’nti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhāvaisi maraṇassati’’nti? ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīvaiyyaṃ yadantaraṃ aikaṃ ālaupaṃ saṅkhāditvā saṅkharitvā (ka॰) ajjhauharāmi, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’ti| aivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti|

añ‍ñataraupi khau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahampi khau, bhantai, bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, bhāvaisi maraṇassati’’nti? ‘‘idha mayhaṃ bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīvaiyyaṃ yadantaraṃ assasitvā vā passasāmi, passasitvā vā assasāmi, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’ti| aivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai bhāvaimi maraṇassati’’nti|

aivaṃ  vuttai bhagavā tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘yvāyaṃ yau cāyaṃ (ka॰ sī॰), bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvaiti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ rattindivaṃ jīvaiyyaṃ, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’ti| yau cāyaṃ yau pāyaṃ (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 6.19 passitabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvaiti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ  divasaṃ jīvaiyyaṃ, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’ti; yau cāyaṃ yau pāyaṃ (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 6.19 passitabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvaiti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ upaḍḍhadivasaṃ jīvaiyyaṃ, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’ti| yau cāyaṃ yau pāyaṃ (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 6.19 passitabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvaiti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīvaiyyaṃ yadantaraṃ aikapiṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jāmi, bhagavatau  sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’ti; yau cāyaṃ yau pāyaṃ (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 6.19 passitabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvaiti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīvaiyyaṃ yadantaraṃ upaḍḍhapiṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jāmi, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’ti| yau cāyaṃ yau pāyaṃ (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 6.19 passitabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvaiti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ  jīvaiyyaṃ yadantaraṃ cattārau pañ‍ca ālaupai saṅkhāditvā ajjhauharāmi, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’ti – imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, ‘bhikkhū pamattā viharanti, dandhaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvainti āsavānaṃ khayāya’’’|

‘‘yau ca khvāyaṃ yau cāyaṃ (syā॰), yau ca khau yaṃ (ka॰), bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvaiti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīvaiyyaṃ yadantaraṃ aikaṃ ālaupaṃ saṅkhāditvā ajjhauharāmi, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’ti| yau cāyaṃ yau pāyaṃ (ka॰), bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvaiti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīvaiyyaṃ yadantaraṃ assasitvā vā passasāmi, passasitvā vā assasāmi, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasi karaiyyaṃ, bahu vata mai kataṃ assā’ti – imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, ‘bhikkhū appamattā viharanti, tikkhaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvainti āsavānaṃ khayāya’’’|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘appamattā viharissāma, tikkhaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvayissāma āsavānaṃ khayāyā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyamaraṇassatisuttaṃ

74. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā nātikai viharati giñ‍jakāvasathai| tatra  khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi  …pai॰… maraṇassati, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā|

‘‘kathaṃ bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, maraṇassati kathaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu divasai nikkhantai rattiyā patihitāya paṭihitāya (pī॰), (a॰ ni॰ 6.20 passitabbaṃ) iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘bahukā khau mai pac‍cayā maraṇassa – ahi vā maṃ ḍaṃsaiyya, vicchikau vā maṃ ḍaṃsaiyya, satapadī vā maṃ ḍaṃsaiyya; taina mai assa kālakiriyā| sau mama assa mamassa (a॰ ni॰ 6.20) antarāyau| upakkhalitvā vā papataiyyaṃ, bhattaṃ vā mai bhuttaṃ byāpaj‍jaiyya, pittaṃ vā mai kuppaiyya, saimhaṃ vā mai kuppaiyya, satthakā vā mai vātā kuppaiyyuṃ, manussā vā maṃ upak‍kamaiyyuṃ, amanussā vā maṃ upak‍kamaiyyuṃ; taina mai assa kālakiriyā| sau mama assa antarāyau’ti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ – ‘atthi nu khau mai pāpakā akusalā dhammā appahīnā yai mai assu rattiṃ kālaṃ karauntassa antarāyāyā’’’ti|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘atthi mai pāpakā akusalā dhammā appahīnā yai mai assu rattiṃ kālaṃ karauntassa antarāyāyā’ti, taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya adhimattau chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaṇīyaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ādittacailau vā ādittasīsau vā tassaiva cailassa vā sīsassa vā nibbāpanāya adhimattaṃ chandañ‍ca vāyāmañ‍ca ussāhañ‍ca ussauḷhiñ‍ca  appaṭivāniñ‍ca satiñ‍ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaiyya; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, taina bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ  dhammānaṃ pahānāya adhimattau chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaṇīyaṃ|

‘‘sacai pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘natthi mai pāpakā akusalā dhammā appahīnā yai mai assu rattiṃ kālaṃ karauntassa antarāyāyā’ti, taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu rattiyā nikkhantāya divasai patihitai iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘bahukā khau mai pac‍cayā maraṇassa – ahi vā maṃ ḍaṃsaiyya, vicchikau vā maṃ ḍaṃsaiyya, satapadī vā maṃ ḍaṃsaiyya; taina mai assa kālakiriyā| sau mama assa antarāyau| upakkhalitvā vā papataiyyaṃ, bhattaṃ vā mai bhuttaṃ byāpaj‍jaiyya, pittaṃ vā mai kuppaiyya, saimhaṃ vā mai kuppaiyya, satthakā vā mai vātā kuppaiyyuṃ, manussā vā maṃ upak‍kamaiyyuṃ, amanussā vā maṃ upak‍kamaiyyuṃ; taina mai assa kālakiriyā| sau mama assa antarāyau’ti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ – ‘atthi nu khau mai pāpakā akusalā dhammā appahīnā yai mai assu divā kālaṃ karauntassa antarāyāyā’’’ti|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘atthi mai pāpakā akusalā dhammā appahīnā yai mai assu divā kālaṃ karauntassa antarāyāyā’ti, taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya adhimattau chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaṇīyaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ādittacailau vā ādittasīsau vā tassaiva cailassa vā sīsassa vā nibbāpanāya adhimattaṃ chandañ‍ca vāyāmañ‍ca ussāhañ‍ca ussauḷhiñ‍ca appaṭivāniñ‍ca satiñ‍ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaiyya; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, taina bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya adhimattau chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaṇīyaṃ|

‘‘sacai pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘natthi mai pāpakā akusalā dhammā appahīnā  yai mai assu divā kālaṃ karauntassa antarāyāyā’ti, taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu| aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, maraṇassati aivaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamasampadāsuttaṃ

75. ‘‘aṭṭhimā, bhikkhavai, sampadā| katamā aṭṭha? a॰ ni॰ 8.54 uṭṭhānasampadā, ārakkhasampadā, kalyāṇamittatā, samajīvitā, saddhāsampadā, sīlasampadā, cāgasampadā, pañ‍ñāsampadā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha sampadā’’ti|

‘‘uṭṭhātā kammadhaiyyaisu, appamattau vidhānavā|

samaṃ kappaiti jīvikaṃ, sambhataṃ anurakkhati||

‘‘saddhau  sīlaina sampan‍nau, vadañ‍ñū vītamaccharau|

nic‍caṃ maggaṃ visaudhaiti, sautthānaṃ samparāyikaṃ||

‘‘ic‍caitai aṭṭha dhammā ca, saddhassa gharamaisinau|

akkhātā sac‍canāmaina, ubhayattha sukhāvahā||

‘‘diṭṭhadhammahitatthāya, samparāyasukhāya ca|

aivamaitaṃ gahaṭṭhānaṃ, cāgau puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhatī’’ti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyasampadāsuttaṃ

76. ‘‘aṭṭhimā, bhikkhavai, sampadā| katamā aṭṭha? uṭṭhānasampadā, ārakkhasampadā, kalyāṇamittatā, samajīvitā, saddhāsampadā, sīlasampadā, cāgasampadā, pañ‍ñāsampadā| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, uṭṭhānasampadā? idha, bhikkhavai, kulaputtau yaina kammaṭṭhānaina jīvitaṃ kappaiti – yadi kasiyā yadi vaṇij‍jāya yadi gaurakkhaina yadi issattaina yadi rājapaurisaina yadi sippañ‍ñataraina – tattha  dakkhau hauti analasau, tatrupāyāya vīmaṃsāya saman‍nāgatau, alaṃ kātuṃ alaṃ saṃvidhātunti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, uṭṭhānasampadā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, ārakkhasampadā? idha, bhikkhavai, kulaputtassa bhaugā haunti uṭṭhānavīriyādhigatā  bāhābalaparicitā saidāvakkhittā dhammikā dhammaladdhā tai ārakkhaina guttiyā sampādaiti – ‘kinti mai bhaugai naiva rājānau haraiyyuṃ, na caurā haraiyyuṃ, na aggi ḍahaiyya, na udakaṃ vahaiyya, na appiyā dāyādā haraiyyu’nti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ārakkhasampadā|

‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, kalyāṇamittatā? idha, bhikkhavai, kulaputtau yasmiṃ gāmai vā nigamai vā paṭivasati, tattha yai tai haunti gahapatī vā gahapatiputtā vā daharā vā vuddhasīlinau vuddhā vā vuddhasīlinau saddhāsampan‍nā sīlasampan‍nā cāgasampan‍nā pañ‍ñāsampan‍nā, taihi saddhiṃ santiṭṭhati sal‍lapati sākacchaṃ samāpaj‍jati; yathārūpānaṃ saddhāsampan‍nānaṃ saddhāsampadaṃ anusikkhati, yathārūpānaṃ sīlasampan‍nānaṃ sīlasampadaṃ anusikkhati, yathārūpānaṃ cāgasampan‍nānaṃ cāgasampadaṃ anusikkhati, yathārūpānaṃ pañ‍ñāsampan‍nānaṃ pañ‍ñāsampadaṃ anusikkhati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kalyāṇamittatā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, samajīvitā? idha, bhikkhavai, kulaputtau āyañ‍ca bhaugānaṃ viditvā vayañ‍ca bhaugānaṃ viditvā samaṃ jīvikaṃ kappaiti nāc‍caugāḷhaṃ nātihīnaṃ – ‘aivaṃ mai āyau vayaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati, na ca mai vayau āyaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatī’ti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, tulādhārau vā tulādhārantaivāsī vā tulaṃ paggahaitvā jānāti – ‘aittakaina vā aunataṃ, aittakaina  vā un‍nata’nti; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, kulaputtau āyañ‍ca bhaugānaṃ viditvā vayañ‍ca bhaugānaṃ viditvā samaṃ jīvikaṃ kappaiti nāc‍caugāḷhaṃ nātihīnaṃ – ‘aivaṃ mai āyau vayaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati, na ca mai vayau āyaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatī’ti| sacāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, kulaputtau  appāyau samānau uḷāraṃ jīvikaṃ kappaiti, tassa bhavanti vattārau ‘udumbarakhādī vāyaṃ kulaputtau bhaugai khādatī’ti| sacai panāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, kulaputtau  mahāyau samānau kasiraṃ jīvikaṃ kappaiti, tassa bhavanti vattārau – ‘ajaiṭṭhamaraṇaṃ vāyaṃ kulaputtau marissatī’ti| yatau ca khauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, kulaputtau āyañ‍ca bhaugānaṃ viditvā vayañ‍ca bhaugānaṃ viditvā samaṃ jīvikaṃ kappaiti nāc‍caugāḷhaṃ nātihīnaṃ – ‘aivaṃ mai āyau vayaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati, na ca mai vayau āyaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatī’ti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, samajīvitā|

‘‘katamā  ca bhikkhavai, saddhāsampadā? idha, bhikkhavai, kulaputtau saddhau hauti, saddahati tathāgatassa baudhiṃ – ‘itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, saddhāsampadā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sīlasampadā? idha, bhikkhavai, kulaputtau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sīlasampadā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, cāgasampadā? idha, bhikkhavai, kulaputtau vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati…pai॰… yācayaugau dānasaṃvibhāgaratau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, cāgasampadā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñāsampadā? idha, bhikkhavai, kulaputtau pañ‍ñavā hauti…pai॰… sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñāsampadā| imā khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha sampadā’’ti|

‘‘uṭṭhātā kammadhaiyyaisu, appamattau vidhānavā|

samaṃ kappaiti jīvikaṃ, sambhataṃ anurakkhati||

‘‘saddhau  sīlaina sampan‍nau, vadañ‍ñū vītamaccharau|

nic‍caṃ maggaṃ visaudhaiti, sautthānaṃ samparāyikaṃ||

‘‘ic‍caitai aṭṭha dhammā ca, saddhassa gharamaisinau|

akkhātā sac‍canāmaina, ubhayattha sukhāvahā||

‘‘diṭṭhadhammahitatthāya, samparāyasukhāya ca|

aivamaitaṃ gahaṭṭhānaṃ, cāgau puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhatī’’ti|| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. icchāsuttaṃ

77. tatra  khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau bhikkhavau’’ti! ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca –

a॰ ni॰ 8.61 ‘‘aṭṭhimai, āvusau, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai aṭṭha? idhāvusau, bhikkhunau pavivittassa viharatau nirāyattavuttinau icchā uppaj‍jati lābhāya| sau uṭṭhahati, ghaṭati, vāyamati lābhāya| tassa uṭṭhahatau, ghaṭatau, vāyamatau lābhāya lābhau nuppaj‍jati| sau taina alābhaina saucati kilamati paridaivati, urattāḷiṃ kandati, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, ‘bhikkhu icchau viharati lābhāya, uṭṭhahati, ghaṭati, vāyamati lābhāya, na ca lābhī, saucī ca paridaivī ca, cutau ca saddhammā’’’|

‘‘idha panāvusau, bhikkhunau pavivittassa viharatau nirāyattavuttinau icchā uppaj‍jati lābhāya| sau uṭṭhahati, ghaṭati, vāyamati lābhāya| tassa uṭṭhahatau ghaṭatau vāyamatau lābhāya lābhau uppaj‍jati| sau taina  lābhaina maj‍jati pamaj‍jati pamādamāpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, ‘bhikkhu icchau viharati lābhāya, uṭṭhahati ghaṭati vāyamati lābhāya, lābhī ca, madī ca pamādī ca, cutau ca saddhammā’’’|

‘‘idha panāvusau, bhikkhunau pavivittassa viharatau nirāyattavuttinau icchā uppaj‍jati lābhāya| sau na uṭṭhahati, na ghaṭati, na vāyamati lābhāya| tassa anuṭṭhahatau, aghaṭatau, avāyamatau lābhāya lābhau nuppaj‍jati| sau taina alābhaina saucati kilamati paridaivati, urattāḷiṃ kandati, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, ‘bhikkhu icchau viharati lābhāya, na uṭṭhahati, na ghaṭati, na vāyamati lābhāya, na ca lābhī, saucī ca paridaivī ca, cutau ca saddhammā’’’|

‘‘idha panāvusau, bhikkhunau pavivittassa viharatau nirāyattavuttinau icchā uppaj‍jati lābhāya| sau na uṭṭhahati, na ghaṭati, na vāyamati lābhāya| tassa anuṭṭhahatau, aghaṭatau, avāyamatau  lābhāya lābhau uppaj‍jati| sau taina lābhaina maj‍jati pamaj‍jati pamādamāpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, ‘bhikkhu icchau viharati lābhāya, na uṭṭhahati na  ghaṭati na vāyamati lābhāya, lābhī ca, madī ca pamādī ca, cutau ca saddhammā’’’|

‘‘idha panāvusau, bhikkhunau pavivittassa viharatau nirāyattavuttinau icchā uppaj‍jati lābhāya| sau uṭṭhahati, ghaṭati, vāyamati lābhāya| tassa uṭṭhahatau, ghaṭatau, vāyamatau lābhāya, lābhau nuppaj‍jati| sau taina alābhaina na saucati na kilamati  na paridaivati, na urattāḷiṃ kandati, na sammauhaṃ  āpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, ‘bhikkhu icchau viharati lābhāya, uṭṭhahati ghaṭati vāyamati lābhāya, na ca lābhī, na ca saucī na ca paridaivī, ac‍cutau ca saddhammā’’’|

‘‘idha panāvusau, bhikkhunau pavivittassa viharatau nirāyattavuttinau icchā uppaj‍jati lābhāya| sau uṭṭhahati, ghaṭati, vāyamati lābhāya| tassa uṭṭhahatau, ghaṭatau, vāyamatau lābhāya, lābhau uppaj‍jati| sau taina lābhaina na maj‍jati na pamaj‍jati na pamādamāpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, ‘bhikkhu icchau viharati lābhāya, uṭṭhahati, ghaṭati, vāyamati lābhāya, lābhī ca, na ca madī na ca pamādī, ac‍cutau ca saddhammā’’’|

‘‘idha panāvusau, bhikkhunau pavivittassa viharatau nirāyattavuttinau icchā uppaj‍jati lābhāya| sau na uṭṭhahati, na ghaṭati, na vāyamati lābhāya| tassa anuṭṭhahatau, aghaṭatau, avāyamatau lābhāya, lābhau nuppaj‍jati| sau taina alābhaina na saucati na kilamati na paridaivati, na urattāḷiṃ kandati, na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, ‘bhikkhu icchau viharati lābhāya, na uṭṭhahati, na ghaṭati, na vāyamati lābhāya, na ca lābhī, na ca saucī na ca paridaivī, ac‍cutau ca saddhammā’’’|

‘‘idha panāvusau, bhikkhunau pavivittassa viharatau nirāyattavuttinau icchā uppaj‍jati lābhāya| sau na uṭṭhahati, na ghaṭati, na vāyamati lābhāya| tassa anuṭṭhahatau, aghaṭatau, avāyamatau lābhāya, lābhau uppaj‍jati| sau taina lābhaina na maj‍jati na pamaj‍jati na pamādamāpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, ‘bhikkhu icchau viharati lābhāya, na uṭṭhahati, na  ghaṭati, na vāyamati lābhāya, lābhī ca, na ca madī na ca pamādī, ac‍cutau ca saddhammā’| imai khau, āvusau, aṭṭha puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. alaṃsuttaṃ

78. a॰ ni॰ 8.62 tatra  khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi…pai॰… chahāvusau, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ attanau, alaṃ paraisaṃ| katamaihi chahi? idhāvusau, bhikkhu khippanisanti ca hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu; sutānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ dhāraṇajātikau hauti; dhātānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhitā hauti; atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau ca hauti; kalyāṇavācau ca hauti kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau, pauriyā vācāya saman‍nāgatau vissaṭṭhāya anailagaḷāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā; sandassakau ca hauti samādapakau samuttaijakau sampahaṃsakau sabrahmacārīnaṃ| imaihi khau, āvusau, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ attanau, alaṃ paraisaṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahāvusau, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ attanau, alaṃ paraisaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idhāvusau, bhikkhu na haiva khau khippanisanti ca hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu; sutānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ dhāraṇajātikau hauti; dhātānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhitā hauti; atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau ca hauti; kalyāṇavācau ca hauti…pai॰… sandassakau ca hauti…pai॰… sabrahmacārīnaṃ| imaihi  khau, āvusau, pañ‍cahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ attanau, alaṃ paraisaṃ|

‘‘catūhāvusau, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ attanau, nālaṃ paraisaṃ| katamaihi catūhi? idhāvusau, bhikkhu khippanisanti ca hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu; sutānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ dhāraṇajātikau hauti; dhātānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhitā hauti; atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau ca hauti; nau ca kalyāṇavācau hauti…pai॰… nau ca sandassakau hauti…pai॰… sabrahmacārīnaṃ| imaihi khau, āvusau, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ attanau, nālaṃ paraisaṃ|

‘‘catūhāvusau, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ paraisaṃ, nālaṃ attanau| katamaihi catūhi? idhāvusau, bhikkhu khippanisanti ca hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu; sutānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ dhāraṇajātikau hauti; nau ca dhātānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhitā hauti; nau ca atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau hauti; kalyāṇavācau ca hauti…pai॰… sandassakau  ca hauti…pai॰… sabrahmacārīnaṃ| imaihi khau, āvusau, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ paraisaṃ, nālaṃ attanau|

‘‘tīhāvusau, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ attanau, nālaṃ paraisaṃ| katamaihi tīhi? idhāvusau, bhikkhu na haiva khau khippanisanti ca hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu; sutānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ dhāraṇajātikau  hauti; dhātānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhitā hauti; atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau ca hauti; nau ca kalyāṇavācau hauti…pai॰… nau ca sandassakau hauti…pai॰… sabrahmacārīnaṃ| imaihi khau, āvusau, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ attanau, nālaṃ paraisaṃ|

‘‘tīhāvusau, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ paraisaṃ, nālaṃ attanau| katamaihi tīhi? idhāvusau, bhikkhu na haiva khau khippanisanti ca hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu; sutānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ dhāraṇajātikau hauti; nau ca dhātānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhitā hauti; nau ca atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau hauti; kalyāṇavācau ca hauti…pai॰… atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā, sandassakau ca hauti…pai॰… sabrahmacārīnaṃ| imaihi khau, āvusau, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ paraisaṃ, nālaṃ attanau|

‘‘dvīhāvusau, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ attanau, nālaṃ paraisaṃ| katamaihi dvīhi? idhāvusau, bhikkhu na haiva khau khippanisanti ca hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu; nau ca sutānaṃ dhammānaṃ dhāraṇajātikau hauti; dhātānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhitā hauti; atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau ca hauti; nau ca kalyāṇavācau hauti…pai॰… nau ca sandassakau hauti…pai॰… sabrahmacārīnaṃ| imaihi khau, āvusau, dvīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ attanau, nālaṃ paraisaṃ|

‘‘dvīhāvusau, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ paraisaṃ, nālaṃ attanau| katamaihi dvīhi? idhāvusau, bhikkhu na haiva khau khippanisanti ca hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu; nau ca sutānaṃ dhammānaṃ dhāraṇajātikau hauti; nau ca dhātānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhitā hauti; nau ca atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau hauti; kalyāṇavācau ca hauti kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau, pauriyā vācāya saman‍nāgatau vissaṭṭhāya anailagaḷāya atthassa  viñ‍ñāpaniyā; sandassakau ca hauti samādapakau samuttaijakau sampahaṃsakau sabrahmacārīnaṃ| imaihi khau, āvusau, dvīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ paraisaṃ, nālaṃ attanau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. parihānasuttaṃ

79. ‘‘aṭṭhimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā saikhassa bhikkhunau parihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai aṭṭha? kammārāmatā, bhassārāmatā, niddārāmatā, saṅgaṇikārāmatā, indriyaisu aguttadvāratā, bhaujanai amattañ‍ñutā, saṃsaggārāmatā, papañ‍cārāmatā – imai khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha dhammā saikhassa bhikkhunau parihānāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘aṭṭhimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā saikhassa bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattanti| katamai aṭṭha? na kammārāmatā, na bhassārāmatā, na niddārāmatā, na saṅgaṇikārāmatā, indriyaisu guttadvāratā, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñutā, asaṃsaggārāmatā, nippapañ‍cārāmatā – imai khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha dhammā saikhassa bhikkhunau aparihānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. kusītārambhavatthusuttaṃ

80. di॰ ni॰ 3.334, 358 ‘‘aṭṭhimāni, bhikkhavai, kusītavatthūni| katamāni aṭṭha? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā kammaṃ kattabbaṃ hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘kammaṃ khau mai kattabbaṃ bhavissati| kammaṃ khau pana mai karauntassa kāyau kilamissati| handāhaṃ nipaj‍jāmī’ti| sau nipaj‍jati, na vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ kusītavatthu|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā kammaṃ kataṃ hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ  khau kammaṃ akāsiṃ| kammaṃ khau pana mai karauntassa kāyau kilantau| handāhaṃ nipaj‍jāmī’ti| sau nipaj‍jati, na vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyaṃ kusītavatthu|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā maggau gantabbau hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘maggau mai gantabbau bhavissati| maggaṃ khau pana mai gacchantassa kāyau kilamissati| handāhaṃ nipaj‍jāmī’ti| sau nipaj‍jati, na vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyaṃ kusītavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā maggau gatau hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau maggaṃ agamāsiṃ| maggaṃ khau pana mai gacchantassa kāyau kilantau| handāhaṃ nipaj‍jāmī’ti| sau nipaj‍jati, na vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| idaṃ bhikkhavai, catutthaṃ kusītavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya carantau na labhati lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ| tassa aivaṃ hauti  – ‘ahaṃ khau gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya carantau nālatthaṃ lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ| tassa mai kāyau kilantau akammañ‍ñau| handāhaṃ nipaj‍jāmī’ti| sau nipaj‍jati, na vīriyaṃ ārabhati…pai॰… idaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camaṃ kusītavatthu|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya carantau labhati lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya carantau alatthaṃ lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ| tassa mai kāyau garukau akammañ‍ñau māsācitaṃ mañ‍ñai| handāhaṃ nipaj‍jāmī’ti| sau nipaj‍jati, na vīriyaṃ ārabhati…pai॰… idaṃ, bhikkhavai, chaṭṭhaṃ kusītavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau uppan‍nau hauti appamattakau ābādhau| tassa aivaṃ hauti  – ‘uppan‍nau khau mai ayaṃ appamattakau ābādhau atthi kappau nipaj‍jituṃ| handāhaṃ nipaj‍jāmī’ti| sau nipaj‍jati, na vīriyaṃ ārabhati…pai॰… idaṃ, bhikkhavai, sattamaṃ kusītavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu gilānā vuṭṭhitau a॰ ni॰ 6.16 suttavaṇṇanā ṭīkā aulaukaitabbā hauti aciravuṭṭhitau gailañ‍ñā| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau gilānā vuṭṭhitau aciravuṭṭhitau gailañ‍ñā| tassa mai kāyau dubbalau akammañ‍ñau| handāhaṃ nipaj‍jāmī’ti| sau nipaj‍jati, na vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhamaṃ kusītavatthu| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha kusītavatthūni|

dī॰ ni॰ 3.335, 358 ‘‘aṭṭhimāni, bhikkhavai, ārambhavatthūni| katamāni aṭṭha? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā kammaṃ kattabbaṃ hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘kammaṃ khau mai kattabbaṃ bhavissati| kammaṃ khau mayā karauntaina na sukaraṃ buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ manasi kātuṃ| handāhaṃ paṭikac‍caiva vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi appattassa  pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāyā’ti| sau vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ ārambhavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā kammaṃ kataṃ hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau kammaṃ akāsiṃ| kammaṃ khau panāhaṃ karauntau nāsakkhiṃ buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ manasi kātuṃ| handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāyā’ti| sau vīriyaṃ ārabhati| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyaṃ ārambhavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā maggau gantabbau hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – maggau khau mai gantabbau bhavissati| maggaṃ khau pana mai gacchantaina na sukaraṃ buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ manasi kātuṃ| handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyaṃ ārambhavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā maggau gatau hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ahaṃ khau maggaṃ  agamāsiṃ| maggaṃ khau panāhaṃ gacchantau nāsakkhiṃ buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ manasi kātuṃ| handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi…pai॰… idaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthaṃ ārambhavatthu|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya carantau na labhati lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ahaṃ khau gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya carantau nālatthaṃ lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ| tassa mai  kāyau lahukau kammañ‍ñau| handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi…pai॰… idaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍camaṃ ārambhavatthu|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya carantau labhati lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ahaṃ khau gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya carantau alatthaṃ lūkhassa vā paṇītassa vā bhaujanassa yāvadatthaṃ pāripūriṃ| tassa mai kāyau balavā kammañ‍ñau| handāhaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi…pai॰… idaṃ, bhikkhavai, chaṭṭhaṃ ārambhavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau uppan‍nau hauti appamattakau ābādhau| tassa aivaṃ hauti – uppan‍nau khau mai ayaṃ appamattakau ābādhau| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ mai ābādhau pavaḍḍhaiyya| handāhaṃ paṭikac‍caiva vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi…pai॰… idaṃ, bhikkhavai, sattamaṃ ārambhavatthu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu gilānā vuṭṭhitau hauti aciravuṭṭhitau gailañ‍ñā| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau gilānā vuṭṭhitau aciravuṭṭhitau gailañ‍ñā| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ mai ābādhau pac‍cudāvattaiyya| handāhaṃ paṭikac‍caiva vīriyaṃ ārabhāmi appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāyā’ti| sau vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhamaṃ ārambhavatthu| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha ārambhavatthūnī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

yamakavaggau tatiyau|


tassuddānaṃ  –

dvai  saddhā dvai maraṇassatī, dvai sampadā athāparai|

icchā alaṃ parihānaṃ, kusītārambhavatthūnīti||


(9) 4. sativaggau

1. satisampajañ‍ñasuttaṃ

81. ‘‘satisampajañ‍ñai, bhikkhavai, asati satisampajañ‍ñavipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti hirauttappaṃ| hirauttappai asati hirauttappavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti indriyasaṃvarau| indriyasaṃvarai asati indriyasaṃvaravipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti  sīlaṃ| sīlai asati sīlavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti sammāsamādhi| sammāsamādhimhi asati sammāsamādhivipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ| yathābhūtañāṇadassanai asati yathābhūtañāṇadassanavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti nibbidāvirāgau| nibbidāvirāgai asati nibbidāvirāgavipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rukkhau sākhāpalāsavipan‍nau| tassa papaṭikāpi na pāripūriṃ gacchati, tacaupi… phaiggupi… sāraupi na pāripūriṃ gacchati| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, satisampajañ‍ñai asati satisampajañ‍ñavipan‍nassa hatūpanisaṃ hauti hirauttappaṃ; hirauttappai asati hirauttappavipan‍nassa hatūpanisau hauti…pai॰… vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ|

‘‘satisampajañ‍ñai, bhikkhavai, sati satisampajañ‍ñasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti hirauttappaṃ| hirauttappai sati hirauttappasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti indriyasaṃvarau| indriyasaṃvarai sati indriyasaṃvarasampan‍nassa  upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti sīlaṃ| sīlai sati sīlasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti sammāsamādhi| sammāsamādhimhi sati sammāsamādhisampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ| yathābhūtañāṇadassanai sati yathābhūtañāṇadassanasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti nibbidāvirāgau| nibbidāvirāgai sati nibbidāvirāgasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rukkhau sākhāpalāsasampan‍nau| tassa papaṭikāpi pāripūriṃ gacchati, tacaupi… phaiggupi… sāraupi pāripūriṃ gacchati| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, satisampajañ‍ñai sati satisampajañ‍ñasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍naṃ hauti hirauttappaṃ; hirauttappai sati hirauttappasampan‍nassa upanisasampan‍nau hauti…pai॰… vimuttiñāṇadassana’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. puṇṇiyasuttaṃ

82. atha  khau āyasmā puṇṇiyau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā puṇṇiyau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau yaina appaikadā tathāgataṃ dhammadaisanā paṭibhāti, appaikadā na paṭibhātī’’ti? ‘‘saddhau ca, puṇṇiya, bhikkhu hauti, nau cupasaṅkamitā; naiva tathāgataṃ dhammadaisanā paṭibhāti| yatau ca khau, puṇṇiya, bhikkhu saddhau ca hauti, upasaṅkamitā ca; aivaṃ tathāgataṃ dhammadaisanā paṭibhāti| saddhau ca, puṇṇiya, bhikkhu hauti, upasaṅkamitā ca, nau ca payirupāsitā…pai॰… payirupāsitā ca, nau ca paripucchitā… paripucchitā  ca, nau ca auhitasautau dhammaṃ suṇāti… auhitasautau ca dhammaṃ suṇāti, nau ca sutvā dhammaṃ dhāraiti… sutvā ca dhammaṃ dhāraiti, nau ca dhātānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhati… dhātānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhati, nau ca atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau hauti| naiva tāva tathāgataṃ dhammadaisanā paṭibhāti|

‘‘yatau ca khau, puṇṇiya, bhikkhu saddhau ca  hauti, upasaṅkamitā ca, payirupāsitā ca, paripucchitā ca, auhitasautau ca dhammaṃ suṇāti, sutvā ca dhammaṃ dhāraiti, dhātānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhati, atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau ca hauti; aivaṃ tathāgataṃ dhammadaisanā paṭibhāti| imaihi khau, puṇṇiya, aṭṭhahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā saman‍nāgatau (sī॰ pī॰), saman‍nāgataṃ (syā॰ ka॰) aikantapaṭibhānā aikantapaṭibhānaṃ (sabbattha) a॰ ni॰ 10.83 pana passitabbaṃ tathāgataṃ dhammadaisanā hautī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. mūlakasuttaṃ

83. a॰ ni॰ 10.58 passitabbaṃ ‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘kiṃmūlakā, āvusau, sabbai dhammā, kiṃsambhavā sabbai dhammā, kiṃsamudayā sabbai dhammā, kiṃsamausaraṇā sabbai dhammā, kiṃpamukhā sabbai dhammā, kiṃadhipataiyyā sabbai dhammā, kiṃuttarā sabbai dhammā, kiṃsārā sabbai dhammā’ti, aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ kinti byākaraiyyāthā’’ti? ‘‘bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā, bhagavaṃnaittikā bhagavaṃpaṭisaraṇā| sādhu, bhantai, bhagavantaṃyaiva paṭibhātu aitassa bhāsitassa atthau| bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti|

‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘kiṃmūlakā, āvusau, sabbai dhammā, kiṃsambhavā sabbai dhammā, kiṃsamudayā sabbai dhammā, kiṃsamausaraṇā sabbai dhammā, kiṃpamukhā sabbai dhammā, kiṃadhipataiyyā  sabbai dhammā, kiṃuttarā sabbai dhammā, kiṃsārā sabbai dhammā’ti, aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyātha – ‘chandamūlakā, āvusau, sabbai dhammā, manasikārasambhavā sabbai dhammā, phassasamudayā sabbai dhammā, vaidanāsamausaraṇā sabbai dhammā, samādhippamukhā sabbai dhammā, satādhipataiyyā sabbai dhammā, pañ‍ñuttarā sabbai dhammā, vimuttisārā sabbai dhammā’ti, aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyāthā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. caurasuttaṃ

84. ‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau mahācaurau khippaṃ pariyāpaj‍jati, na ciraṭṭhitikau hauti| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? appaharantassa paharati, anavasaisaṃ ādiyati, itthiṃ hanati, kumāriṃ dūsaiti, pabbajitaṃ vilumpati, rājakausaṃ vilumpati, ac‍cāsan‍nai kammaṃ karauti, na ca nidhānakusalau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau mahācaurau khippaṃ pariyāpaj‍jati, na ciraṭṭhitikau hauti|

‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau mahācaurau na khippaṃ pariyāpaj‍jati, ciraṭṭhitikau hauti| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? na appaharantassa paharati, na anavasaisaṃ ādiyati, na itthiṃ hanati, na kumāriṃ dūsaiti, na pabbajitaṃ vilumpati, na rājakausaṃ vilumpati, na ac‍cāsan‍nai kammaṃ karauti, nidhānakusalau ca hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau mahācaurau na khippaṃ pariyāpaj‍jati, ciraṭṭhitikau hautī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. samaṇasuttaṃ

85. ‘‘‘samaṇau’ti, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| ‘brāhmaṇau’ti, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| ‘vaidagū’ti, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| ‘bhisak‍kau’ti, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| ‘nimmalau’ti, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| ‘vimalau’ti, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| ‘ñāṇī’ti, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ arahatau  sammāsambuddhassa| ‘vimuttau’ti, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassā’’ti|

‘‘yaṃ samaṇaina pattabbaṃ, brāhmaṇaina vusīmatā|

yaṃ vaidagunā pattabbaṃ, bhisak‍kaina anuttaraṃ||

‘‘yaṃ nimmalaina pattabbaṃ, vimalaina sucīmatā|

yaṃ ñāṇinā ca pattabbaṃ, vimuttaina anuttaraṃ||

‘‘sauhaṃ vijitasaṅgāmau, muttau maucaimi bandhanā|

nāgaumhi paramadantau, asaikhau parinibbutau’’ti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. yasasuttaṃ

86. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina  saddhiṃ yaina icchānaṅgalaṃ nāma kausalānaṃ brāhmaṇagāmau tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā icchānaṅgalai viharati icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍai| assausuṃ khau icchānaṅgalakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā – ‘‘samaṇau khalu bhau gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau icchānaṅgalaṃ anuppattau icchānaṅgalai viharati icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍai| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi  sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau…pai॰… sādhu khau pana tathārūpānaṃ arahataṃ dassanaṃ hautī’’’ti|

atha khau icchānaṅgalakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina pahutaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ ādāya yaina icchānaṅgalavanasaṇḍau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bahidvārakauṭṭhakai aṭṭhaṃsu uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā nāgitau bhagavatau upaṭṭhākau hauti| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ nāgitaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kai pana tai, nāgita, uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā kaivaṭṭā mañ‍ñai macchavilaupai’’ti? ‘‘aitai, bhantai, icchānaṅgalakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā pahutaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ ādāya bahidvārakauṭṭhakai ṭhitā bhagavantaṃyaiva uddissa bhikkhusaṅghañ‍cā’’ti| ‘‘māhaṃ, nāgita, yasaina samāgamaṃ, mā ca mayā yasau| yau khau, nāgita, nayimassa naikkhammasukhassa pavivaikasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambaudhasukhassa nikāmalābhī assa akicchalābhī akasiralābhī| yassāhaṃ naikkhammasukhassa pavivaikasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambaudhasukhassa  nikāmalābhī nikāmalābhī assaṃ (bahūsu) a॰ ni॰ 5.30 passitabbaṃ| tattha hi ayaṃ pāṭhabhaidā natthi akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, sau  taṃ mīḷhasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ lābhasak‍kārasilaukasukhaṃ sādiyaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘adhivāsaitu dāni, bhantai, bhagavā| adhivāsaitu sugatau| adhivāsanakālau dāni, bhantai, bhagavatau| yaina yainaiva dāni, bhantai, bhagavā gamissati tan‍nin‍nāva bhavissanti brāhmaṇagahapatikā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, thul‍laphusitakai daivai vassantai yathānin‍naṃ udakāni pavattanti; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhantai, yaina yainaiva dāni bhagavā gamissati tan‍nin‍nāva bhavissanti brāhmaṇagahapatikā naigamā caiva jānapadā ca| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi, bhantai, bhagavatau sīlapañ‍ñāṇa’’nti|

‘‘māhaṃ, nāgita, yasaina samāgamaṃ, mā ca mayā yasau| yau khau, nāgita, nayimassa naikkhammasukhassa pavivaikasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambaudhasukhassa nikāmalābhī assa akicchalābhī akasiralābhī| yassāhaṃ naikkhammasukhassa pavivaikasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambaudhasukhassa nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, sau taṃ mīḷhasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ lābhasak‍kārasilaukasukhaṃ sādiyaiyya|

‘‘daivatāpi  khau, nāgita, aikac‍cā nayimassa aikac‍cā imassa (?) naikkhammasukhassa pavivaikasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambaudhasukhassa nikāmalābhiniyau assu idaṃ padaṃ katthaci natthi akicchalābhiniyau nikāmalābhiniyau akicchalābhiniyau (?) akasiralābhiniyau, yassāhaṃ naikkhammasukhassa pavivaikasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambaudhasukhassa nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī| tumhākampi tāsampi (?) khau, nāgita, saṅgamma samāgamma saṅgaṇikavihāraṃ anuyuttānaṃ viharataṃ anuyuttai viharantai disvā (?) aivaṃ hauti – ‘na hi nūnamai na hanūnamai (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) āyasmantau imassa naikkhammasukhassa pavivaikasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambaudhasukhassa nikāmalābhinau assu idaṃ padaṃ katthaci natthi akicchalābhinau akasiralābhinau| yassāhaṃ naikkhammasukhassa pavivaikasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambaudhasukhassa nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī| tathā hi  pana mai āyasmantau  saṅgamma samāgamma saṅgaṇikavihāraṃ anuyuttā viharanti’’’|

‘‘idhāhaṃ, nāgita, bhikkhū passāmi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ aṅgulipataudakaina aṅgulipataudakaihi (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) sañ‍jagghantai saṅkīḷantai| tassa mayhaṃ, nāgita, aivaṃ hauti – ‘na hi nūnamai āyasmantau imassa naikkhammasukhassa pavivaikasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambaudhasukhassa nikāmalābhinau assu akicchalābhinau akasiralābhinau| yassāhaṃ naikkhammasukhassa pavivaikasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambaudhasukhassa nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī| tathā hi pana mai āyasmantau añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ aṅgulipataudakaina sañ‍jagghanti saṅkīḷanti’’’|

‘‘idha panāhaṃ idhāhaṃ (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), nāgita, bhikkhū passāmi yāvadatthaṃ udarāvadaihakaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā saiyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyuttai viharantai| tassa mayhaṃ, nāgita, aivaṃ hauti – ‘na hi nūnamai āyasmantau imassa naikkhammasukhassa pavivaikasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambaudhasukhassa nikāmalābhinau assu akicchalābhinau akasiralābhinau| yassāhaṃ naikkhammasukhassa pavivaikasukhassa upasamasukhassa sambaudhasukhassa nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī| tathā hi pana mai āyasmantau yāvadatthaṃ udarāvadaihakaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā saiyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyuttā viharanti’’’|

‘‘idhāhaṃ idha panāhaṃ (?), nāgita, bhikkhuṃ passāmi gāmantavihāriṃ samāhitaṃ nisin‍naṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, nāgita, aivaṃ hauti – ‘idāni imaṃ idānimaṃ (katthaci) a॰ ni॰ 6.42 āyasmantaṃ ārāmikau vā upaṭṭhahissati pac‍caissati (sī॰ pī॰), upaṭṭhahati (ka॰) samaṇuddaisau vā| taṃ tamhā sau tamhā (ka॰ sī॰), sau taṃ tamhā (?) samādhimhā cāvaissatī’ti| taināhaṃ, nāgita, tassa bhikkhunau na attamanau haumi gāmantavihāraina|

‘‘idha panāhaṃ, nāgita, bhikkhuṃ passāmi ārañ‍ñikaṃ arañ‍ñai pacalāyamānaṃ nisin‍naṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, nāgita, aivaṃ hauti – ‘idāni ayamāyasmā imaṃ niddākilamathaṃ paṭivinaudaitvā arañ‍ñasañ‍ñaṃyaiva manasi karissati aikatta’nti| taināhaṃ, nāgita, tassa bhikkhunau attamanau haumi arañ‍ñavihāraina|

‘‘idha panāhaṃ, nāgita, bhikkhuṃ passāmi ārañ‍ñikaṃ arañ‍ñai asamāhitaṃ nisin‍naṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, nāgita, aivaṃ hauti – ‘idāni ayamāyasmā asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ samādahissati samādahaissati (katthaci), samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ anurakkhissatī’ti| taināhaṃ, nāgita, tassa bhikkhunau attamanau haumi arañ‍ñavihāraina|

‘‘idha panāhaṃ, nāgita, bhikkhuṃ passāmi ārañ‍ñikaṃ arañ‍ñai samāhitaṃ nisin‍naṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, nāgita, aivaṃ hauti – ‘idāni ayamāyasmā avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ vimuc‍cissati, vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ anurakkhissatī’ti| taināhaṃ, nāgita, tassa bhikkhunau attamanau haumi arañ‍ñavihāraina|

‘‘idha panāhaṃ, nāgita, bhikkhuṃ passāmi gāmantavihāriṃ lābhiṃ cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| sau taṃ lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ nikāmayamānau riñ‍cati paṭisal‍lānaṃ, riñ‍cati arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni; gāmanigamarājadhāniṃ ausaritvā vāsaṃ kappaiti| taināhaṃ, nāgita, tassa bhikkhunau na attamanau haumi gāmantavihāraina|

‘‘idha panāhaṃ, nāgita, bhikkhuṃ passāmi ārañ‍ñikaṃ lābhiṃ cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| sau taṃ lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ paṭipaṇāmaitvā na riñ‍cati paṭisal‍lānaṃ, na riñ‍cati arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni| taināhaṃ, nāgita, tassa bhikkhunau attamanau haumi arañ‍ñavihāraina| [ ] aitthantarai pāṭhau a॰ ni॰ 6.42 chak‍kanipātaiyaiva dissati, na aittha aṭṭhakanipātai

‘‘yasmāhaṃ  yasmiṃhaṃ (katthaci), nāgita, samayai addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau na kañ‍ci passāmi puratau vā pacchatau vā, phāsu mai, nāgita, tasmiṃ samayai hauti antamasau uc‍cārapassāvakammāyā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. pattanikuj‍janasuttaṃ

87. cūḷava॰ 265 ‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa upāsakassa ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau pattaṃ nik‍kuj‍jaiyya nikuj‍jaiyya (ka॰)| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? bhikkhūnaṃ  alābhāya parisak‍kati, bhikkhūnaṃ anatthāya parisak‍kati, bhikkhūnaṃ avāsāya anāvāsāya (sī॰ syā॰) parisak‍kati, bhikkhū ak‍kausati paribhāsati, bhikkhū bhikkhūhi bhaidaiti vibhaidaiti (bahūsu), buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa upāsakassa ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau pattaṃ nik‍kuj‍jaiyya|

‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa upāsakassa ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau pattaṃ uk‍kuj‍jaiyya| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? na bhikkhūnaṃ alābhāya parisak‍kati, na bhikkhūnaṃ anatthāya parisak‍kati, na bhikkhūnaṃ avāsāya parisak‍kati, na bhikkhū ak‍kausati paribhāsati, na bhikkhū bhikkhūhi bhaidaiti, buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa upāsakassa ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau pattaṃ uk‍kuj‍jaiyyā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. appasādapavaidanīyasuttaṃ

88. ‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau ākaṅkhamānā upāsakā appasādaṃ pavaidaiyyuṃ| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? gihīnaṃ alābhāya parisak‍kati, gihīnaṃ anatthāya parisak‍kati, gihī ak‍kausati paribhāsati, gihī gihīhi bhaidaiti, buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, agaucarai ca naṃ passanti| imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau ākaṅkhamānā upāsakā appasādaṃ pavaidaiyyuṃ|

‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau ākaṅkhamānā upāsakā pasādaṃ pavaidaiyyuṃ| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? na gihīnaṃ alābhāya parisak‍kati, na gihīnaṃ anatthāya parisak‍kati, na gihī ak‍kausati paribhāsati, na gihī gihīhi bhaidaiti, buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, gaucarai ca naṃ passanti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau ākaṅkhamānā upāsakā pasādaṃ pavaidaiyyu’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭisāraṇīyasuttaṃ

89. cūḷava॰ 39 thaukaṃ visadisaṃ ‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karaiyya| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? gihīnaṃ alābhāya parisak‍kati, gihīnaṃ anatthāya parisak‍kati, gihī ak‍kausati paribhāsati, gihī gihīhi bhaidaiti, buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammikañ‍ca gihipaṭissavaṃ na sac‍cāpaiti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau paṭisāraṇīyaṃ kammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaiyya| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? na gihīnaṃ alābhāya parisak‍kati, na gihīnaṃ anatthāya parisak‍kati, na gihī ak‍kausati paribhāsati, na gihī gihīhi bhaidaiti, buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa  vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammikañ‍ca gihipaṭissavaṃ sac‍cāpaiti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaiyyā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. sammāvattanasuttaṃ

90. cūḷava॰ 211 ‘‘tassapāpiyasikakammakataina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā aṭṭhasu dhammaisu sammā vattitabbaṃ – na upasampādaitabbau, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau, na bhikkhunauvādakasammuti sāditabbā, sammatainapi bhikkhuniyau na auvaditabbā, na kāci saṅghasammuti  sāditabbā, na kismiñ‍ci pac‍caikaṭṭhānai ṭhapaitabbau, na ca taina mūlaina vuṭṭhāpaitabbau| tassapāpiyasikakammakataina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā imaisu aṭṭhasu dhammaisu sammā vattitabba’’nti| dasamaṃ|

sativaggau catutthau|


tassuddānaṃ –

satipuṇṇiyamūlaina, caurasamaṇaina pañ‍camaṃ|

yasau pattappasādaina, paṭisāraṇīyañ‍ca vattananti||


(10) 5. sāmañ‍ñavaggau

91-116. atha  khau aittha ‘‘atha khau’’ti ca, ‘‘upāsikā’’ti ca idaṃ aṭṭhakathāyamaiva dissati, na pāḷipautthakaisu baujjhā baujjhaṅgā (ka॰ sī॰) upāsikā aittha ‘‘atha khau’’ti ca, upāsikā’’ti ca idaṃ aṭṭhakathāyamaiva dissati, na pāḷipautthakaisu, sirīmā, padumā, sutanā sudhanā (sī॰ pī॰), sudhammā (syā॰), manujā, uttarā, muttā, khaimā, rucī rūpī (sī॰ pī॰), cundī, bimbī, sumanā, mal‍likā, tissā, tissamātā tissāya mātā (sī॰ pī॰), sauṇā, sauṇāya mātā sauṇamātā (syā॰), kāṇā, kāṇamātā kāṇāya mātā (sī॰ pī॰), uttarā nandamātā, visākhā migāramātā, khuj‍juttarā upāsikā, sāmāvatī upāsikā, suppavāsā kauliyadhītā kauḷiyadhītā (syā॰ pī॰), suppiyā upāsikā, nakulamātā gahapatānī|

sāmañ‍ñavaggau pañ‍camau|


dutiyapaṇṇāsakaṃ samattaṃ|



(11). rāgapaiyyālaṃ

117. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya aṭṭha dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai aṭṭha? sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi – rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya imai aṭṭha dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti|

118. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya aṭṭha dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai aṭṭha? ajjhattaṃ rūpasañ‍ñī bahiddhā rūpāni passati parittāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni, tāni abhibhuyya ‘jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| ajjhattaṃ rūpasañ‍ñī bahiddhā rūpāni passati appamāṇāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni, tāni abhibhuyya ‘jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī bahiddhā rūpāni passati parittāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni, tāni abhibhuyya ‘jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī bahiddhā rūpāni passati appamāṇāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni, tāni abhibhuyya ‘jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti| ajjhattaṃ  arūpasañ‍ñī bahiddhā rūpāni passati nīlāni nīlavaṇṇāni nīlanidassanāni nīlanibhāsāni pītāni pītavaṇṇāni…pai॰… lauhitakāni lauhitakavaṇṇāni…pai॰… audātāni audātavaṇṇāni…pai॰… audātanibhāsāni, tāni abhibhuyya ‘jānāmi passāmī’ti aivaṃsañ‍ñī hauti – rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya imai aṭṭha dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’|

119. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya aṭṭha dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai  aṭṭha? rūpī rūpāni passati, ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī bahiddhā rūpāni passati, subhantaiva adhimuttau hauti, sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja  viharati, sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati – rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya imai aṭṭha dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’|

120-146. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñāya…pai॰… parikkhayāya… pahānāya… khayāya… vayāya… virāgāya… niraudhāya… cāgāya… paṭinissaggāya…pai॰… imai aṭṭha dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’|

147-626. ‘‘dausassa…pai॰… mauhassa… kaudhassa… upanāhassa… makkhassa… paḷāsassa… issāya… macchariyassa … māyāya… sāṭhaiyyassa… thambhassa… sārambhassa… mānassa… atimānassa… madassa… pamādassa abhiñ‍ñāya…pai॰… pariñ‍ñāya… parikkhayāya… pahānāya… khayāya… vayāya… virāgāya… niraudhāya… cāgāya… paṭinissaggāya…pai॰… imai aṭṭha dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti|

rāgapaiyyālaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|


aṭṭhakanipātapāḷi niṭṭhitā|
